### [142] [EnSI-RAG: Entity-Structure-Indexed Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Long-Document Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21252)
*Xuanyu Meng,Jiashuo Sun,Jash Rajesh Parekh,Jiawei Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EnSI-RAG是一个基于实体结构索引的检索增强生成框架，通过构建查询无关的实体中心索引（每条记录包含实体、类型、语义类别和值），解决了长文档中多跳推理QA和chunk边界割裂证据的问题。在Loong和Oolong基准上平均准确率达78.24，比基线高6.62个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG方法将文档作为原始chunk进行嵌入相似度检索，当chunk边界将实体与其支撑证据分离，或问题需要跨语料多跳推理时，性能会显著下降。因此需要一种能保留实体间关系、支持多跳推理的检索机制。

Method: 构建查询无关的实体中心索引，每条记录为(e, t, k, v)四元组，其中e代表实体、t代表实体类型、k∈{property, relation, aspect}表示语义类别、v对应具体值，同时保留到原始源文本的链接。查询时以这些记录作为检索句柄，再由LLM将检索到的段落综合生成为最终答案。该设计将证据定位与答案合成分离，同时保留可追溯的源证据。

Result: 在Loong和Oolong两个长文档QA基准上，EnSI-RAG平均准确率达到78.24，比已发表的基线分数高出6.62个百分点，验证了其跨不同设置的有效性。

Conclusion: EnSI-RAG通过实体结构化索引有效缓解了chunk边界导致的证据割裂问题，并支持跨语料的多跳推理，同时保持证据可追溯性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Question answering (QA) over long, connected documents remains challenging because relevant evidence may span multiple entities and their relationships. Existing retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) methods typically index documents as raw chunks and retrieve them through embedding similarity. Their performance degrades when chunk boundaries separate entities from supporting evidence or when a question requires multi-hop reasoning across the corpus. We propose EnSI-RAG (Entity-Structure-Indexed Retrieval-Augmented Generation), a framework that constructs a query-independent, entity-centered index. Each record (e, t, k, v) represents an entity e, its type t, a semantic category k in {property, relation, aspect}, and a value v, while retaining links to the original source passages. At query time, these records serve as retrieval handles, and an LLM synthesizes the retrieved passages into the final answer. This design separates evidence localization from answer synthesis while preserving traceable source evidence. Across Loong and Oolong, EnSI-RAG achieves an average accuracy of 78.24. Relative to the published baseline scores used as references, this is 6.62 points higher, suggesting its effectiveness across these settings. The code is available at https://github.com/RamonMeng/EnSI-RAG.

</details>


### [143] [Memory Augmentation Unlocks Efficient Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21265)
*Simeng Zhang,Yilong Chen,Wenyuan Zhang,Zhenyu Zhang,Yao Chen,Junyuan Shang,Tingwen Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出记忆增强压缩（Memory-Augmented Compression）框架，通过从历史推理轨迹中构建可复用的推理记忆并将其作为预填充端脚手架，以补偿CoT压缩过程中丢失的信息，从而在加速推理的同时保持甚至提升推理质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型依赖Chain-of-Thought（CoT）推理解决复杂任务，但冗长的推理轨迹带来大量推理开销。虽然CoT压缩（如Chain-of-Draft）可以缩短生成长度，但激进的压缩会打断逻辑连贯性并降低性能。现有方法未能在推理压缩和保持逻辑连贯性之间取得良好平衡。

Method: 作者形式化提出了"上下文-生成替代定律"（Context-Generation Substitution Law），即显式推理上下文可以替代部分解码时生成。基于此原理，提出Memory-Augmented Compression：一种无需训练的框架，从历史推理轨迹中构建可复用的推理记忆（总结可复用推理模式、关键约束和关键操作），并将其检索作为预填充端的脚手架。该方法支持与token级、推理轨迹级和推理状态级压缩机制兼容。

Result: 在GSM8K、MATH、BBH和MMLU-Sci上，相比CoD分别提升了21.4、28.0、29.5和6.61个百分点的准确率，同时相比标准CoT获得了1.14-1.49倍的延迟加速。实验结果还表明性能提升来源于相关的推理记忆而非简单增加上下文长度。

Conclusion: 记忆增强压缩是一种有效的训练-free框架，通过将在解码端生成的推理信息转移到预填充端的记忆检索中，实现了推理质量与推理效率的双赢，为CoT压缩提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Large language models often rely on Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning to solve complex tasks, but verbose reasoning traces introduce substantial inference overhead. CoT compression shortens generation, yet aggressive compression may disrupt logical coherence and degrade performance. We formalize this trade-off as the \textit{Context-Generation Substitution Law}, where explicit reasoning context substitutes for part of decode-time generation. Based on this principle, we propose \textit{Memory-Augmented Compression}, a training-free framework that constructs reusable reasoning memories from historical traces and retrieves them as prefill-side scaffolds. Rather than using raw demonstrations, these memories summarize reusable reasoning patterns, key constraints, and critical operations to compensate for information lost during compression. Experiments show that Memory consistently improves prompt-based Chain-of-Draft (CoD) compression across mathematical reasoning, complex reasoning, and science question answering tasks, yielding accuracy gains of 21.4, 28.0, 29.5, and 6.61 points over CoD on GSM8K, MATH, BBH, and MMLU-Sci, while achieving a 1.14--1.49$\times$ latency speedup over standard CoT. Memory is also compatible with token-level, reasoning-trace-level, and inference-state compression mechanisms. Further analyzes show that the gains come from relevant reasoning memories rather than simply increasing context length.

</details>


### [144] [Prompt-Model Interaction Reaches the Fixed Points: A deterministic, task-free structural readout -- and the factorizations of it that failed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21315)
*Nicolás Vera Zúñiga*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文证明提示词的效果并非提示词本身的属性，而是提示词-模型对（prompt-model pair）的属性。作者在无任务的短窗口argmax映射固定点结构这一读出指标上，发现交互效应以完整幅度显现：9个token的条件化即可大幅改变固定点比例并重排模型排序，而60.5 IFEval点的指令微调却对此类毫无影响。多项候选解释（前缀长度、散文vs标记、普遍方向、双向性、指令抵抗、注意力汇机制）均被证伪或不适用，最终结论是解释单元为提示词-模型对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有证据（提示词跨模型降级、重格式化下排序变化）仅涉及任务准确率，无法区分交互是源于任务机制本身还是条件分布。作者希望在一个无任务的读出指标上检验提示词效应是否存在，从而剥离任务因素，直指模型读取片段这一核心问题。

Method: 采用短窗口argmax映射x_{t+1} = argmax_x p(x | x_{t-1}, x_t)的固定点结构作为无任务读出指标，从96个起点普查固定点。对6个模型进行系统性实验，检验条件化token数量、指令微调、前缀长度、文本风格（散文vs标记）、方向性、注意力汇机制等候选解释，并通过加宽样本、交叉验证等手段逐层证伪。

Result: （1）交互在无任务读出上以完整幅度存在：9个token使固定点比例跨越大部分范围、改变四方式结构类并重排模型，但60.5 IFEval点的指令微调对结构类影响为零。（2）所有候选解释均失败：前缀长度效应非单调；四个现象学因素（散文vs标记、普遍方向、双向性、指令抵抗）均在加宽样本后被消解；注意力汇机制仅对5个模型中2个预测正确（等于随机），且长度-内容交叉显示该机制适用于真实文本而非均匀随机探测输入。（3）一个固定的9-token前缀驱动4个模型趋向0、2个趋向1，双向性在分布内起点上仍然成立。

Conclusion: 在该读出指标下，解释单元是提示词-模型对而非提示词本身。作者指出自身反复犯下的方法论错误：对没有变化空间的量施加了具有形状的判据。这提示研究者需要在正确粒度上定位效应的归属，避免将模型-提示词协同效应误归因于单一方面。

Abstract: That a prompt's effect is not a property of the prompt is established: prompts optimised for one model degrade on another, and rankings reorder under neutral reformatting. That evidence is about task accuracy, which cannot say whether the interaction is a fact about task machinery or about the conditional distribution itself. We ask on a readout with no task in it: the fixed-point structure of the short-window argmax map x_{t+1} = argmax_x p(x | x_{t-1}, x_t), censused from 96 starts. It is deterministic, so nothing can be helped or hurt, and it exists only at short windows -- four of six models lose it entirely by window 16 -- so everything here concerns how a model reads a fragment. Two results. First, the interaction reaches this readout at full magnitude: nine tokens of conditioning move the fixed-point fraction across most of its range, change a four-way structural class, and reorder models, while instruction tuning worth 60.5 IFEval points moves the class by zero. Second, nothing we proposed carries it. Prefix length fails: the effect is not monotone. Four phenomenological factors -- prose-versus-markup, a universal direction, bidirectionality, instruct-resistance -- were each withdrawn within one run of being proposed, dissolved by widening the sample. And the nearest mechanistic account, attention-sink dominance of early tokens, predicts the sign of the shift on 2 of 5 models -- chance -- while a length-by-content cross shows it holds on real text and fails on our probe's uniformly random input, so we are outside its regime, not against it. One fixed nine-token prefix drives four models toward 0 and two toward 1; the bidirectionality survives in-distribution starts. On this readout the unit of explanation is the prompt-model pair. The recurring error it caught in us has a name: a criterion with a shape applied to a quantity with no room to vary.

</details>


### [145] [Move by Move: Measuring and Steering How LLMs Conduct Psychotherapy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21325)
*Afonso Baldo,Hugo Pitorro,Areti Vassilopoulos,Anabela C. Areias,Maya D'Eon,Fabíola Costa,Ricardo Rei,Nuno M. Guerreiro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文引入了一个包含十种治疗性动作的本体论（基于MULTI-60清单），用于分析大语言模型如何开展心理治疗对话。研究发现模型过度使用询问（高达人类三倍）、忽视心理教育，且高度依赖人类咨询师开启的策略；将本体论作为工具集暴露给模型后，无需微调即可使对话动作分布与人类咨询师的平均偏差减半，回合级对齐提升7-9个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户越来越多地依赖大语言模型寻求情感支持，但尚不清楚这些模型实际如何进行心理治疗互动。缺乏系统性的分析框架来比较人类咨询师与模型的治疗行为差异，这限制了对模型在心理健康领域应用表现的理解与改进。

Method: 提出包含十个治疗性动作的本体论，这些紧凑的基于功能的类别源自MULTI-60清单；通过五位持证心理学家的标注活动进行验证，并采用基于裁判的方法进行规模化评估，达到与专家一致的水平。将该方法应用于真实咨询对话记录和模型主导的会话，比较人类临床医生与前沿模型面板之间的动作分布差异，并将本体论作为工具集暴露给模型。

Result: 模型过度使用询问动作（高达人类使用率的三倍），忽视了心理教育，且表现出强烈的上下文锚定效应——会延续人类临床医生发起的策略，但很少自主发起。将本体论作为工具集暴露后，模型与人类动作分布的平均偏差减少约一半，回合级与人类治疗师的对齐度提升7-9个百分点，且无需任何微调。

Conclusion: 大语言模型在心理治疗中的行为与人类治疗师存在系统性差异，但通过可解释的本体论工具引导，无需微调即可显著改善模型与人类治疗行为的一致性，为提升LLM在心理健康领域的临床应用提供了轻量级干预方案。

Abstract: Users increasingly turn to large language models for emotional support, yet little is known about how these models actually conduct a psychotherapy interaction. We introduce an ontology of ten therapeutic moves: compact, function-based categories grounded in the MULTI-60 inventory, validated through an annotation campaign with five licensed psychologists, and scaled with a judge-based approach that matches expert agreement. Applying it to real counseling transcripts and model-led sessions, we compare the move distributions between human clinicians and a panel of frontier models. Models over-use inquiry at up to three times the human rate, neglect psychoeducation, and are strongly context-anchored: they carry forward strategies initiated by a human clinician but rarely initiate them themselves. Exposing the ontology as a set of tools roughly halves the mean deviation from the human move distribution and improves turn-level alignment with human therapist by 7-9 percentage points, without any fine-tuning.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [146] [NeuroStrata: An Electroencephalographic Connectivity-Aware Deep Representation Learning Framework for Dynamic Brain Network Analysis of Mental Stress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20354)
*Sayantan Acharya,Hamzeh Asgharnezhad,Abbas Khosravi,Douglas Creighton,Roohallah Alizadehsani,U Rajendra Acharya*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出NeuroStrata框架，利用时变偏定向相干（TV-PDC）建模EEG有效连接的时间演化，结合预训练CNN和ViT提取深度连接嵌入，在SAM 40数据集上实现97.3%的峰值准确率（beta频段+LAION-CLIP-ViT-L14+SVM），并揭示了与精神压力相关的额叶驱动alpha影响和中央整合beta连接模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统EEG精神压力分类方法依赖静态特征，忽略了大脑区域间频段特异性定向连接的时变动态特性，难以充分捕捉压力相关的神经动力学变化。

Method: 从32通道SAM 40数据集的算术任务EEG信号生成TV-PDC连接图；使用预训练CNN和ViT（如LAION-CLIP-ViT-L14）提取深度连接嵌入；采用轻量级机器学习模型（如SVM）进行分类；对各频段（alpha、beta等）和时窗分别进行评估。

Result: beta频段连接判别能力最强，峰值准确率达97.3%（LAION-CLIP-ViT-L14+SVM）；alpha频段性能稳定；时序评估显示中后期时窗分类性能趋于稳定；连接分析揭示额叶驱动的alpha影响和中央整合的beta连接模式。

Conclusion: NeuroStrata将时变有效连接建模与深度表示学习相结合，为EEG精神压力分析提供了可解释且自动化的方法，验证了动态脑连接模式在压力识别中的有效性。

Abstract: This study introduces NeuroStrata, a connectivity-aware deep representation learning framework for EEG-based mental stress analysis using Time-Varying Partial Directed Coherence (TV-PDC). Unlike conventional EEG classification approaches based on static features, NeuroStrata models the temporal evolution of frequency-specific directed connectivity across distributed brain regions. EEG signals from the 32-channel SAM 40 dataset recorded during mental arithmetic tasks were used to generate TV-PDC connectivity maps. These maps were processed using pretrained Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and Vision Transformers (ViTs) to extract deep connectivity embeddings, which were subsequently classified using lightweight machine learning models. Experimental results demonstrate that beta-band connectivity provides the highest discriminative capability, achieving a peak accuracy of 97.3% using the LAION-CLIP-ViT-L14 backbone with a Support Vector Machine classifier, while alpha-band connectivity exhibits consistently stable performance across model configurations. Connectivity analysis revealed prominent frontal-driven alpha influences and centrally integrated beta connectivity patterns associated with stress-related neural dynamics. Temporal evaluation further indicated that classification performance stabilizes in mid-to-late temporal windows, suggesting progressive consolidation of stress-related connectivity signatures. The proposed framework integrates time-varying effective connectivity modelling with deep representation learning to provide an interpretable and automated approach for EEG-based mental stress analysis.

</details>


### [147] [Interpretable Information-Decomposed Brain Graph Learning for fMRI-based Disease Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20380)
*Dengyi Zhao,Zhiheng Zhou,Zihan Wang,Guiying Yan,Xingqin Qi*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出IID-GCN，一种基于部分熵分解（partial entropy decomposition）的可解释图学习框架，将rs-fMRI脑区交互分解为冗余、独特性和协同三类信息图，并通过多通道图卷积网络进行融合，在三项数据集上证明了其能捕获超越传统功能连接的互补诊断信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有rs-fMRI计算机辅助诊断方法大多将脑区间关系简化为基于相关性的边权重，这只能捕捉共波动强度，却无法揭示信息如何在脑区之间共享。脑疾病不仅可能破坏连接强度，还可能扰乱冗余、独特性和协同等信息的组织结构，因此传统功能连接可能遗漏疾病相关的信息结构。

Method: IID-GCN框架包含三个核心组成部分：(1) 使用部分熵分解（partial entropy decomposition）将rs-fMRI交互分解为冗余图、独特性图和协同图，分别表征共享、区域特异和联合涌现的脑活动成分；(2) 构建多通道图卷积网络，通过边重校准（edge recalibration）、跨信息交互（cross-information interaction）、ROI注意力读出（ROI-attention readout）和通道注意力融合（channel-attentive fusion）来整合三类分解图。

Result: 在三个数据集上，IID-GCN一致地捕获了超越传统功能连接的互补诊断信息。所学的信息画像揭示了疾病特异性的冗余、独特性和协同改变模式，表明脑疾病重塑的是功能信息组织而非仅仅是连接强度。

Conclusion: 信息分解的脑图（information-decomposed brain graphs）被确立为一种可解释的rs-fMRI诊断表示，为理解脑疾病的病理机制提供了新的视角，且代码已开源。

Abstract: Resting-state functional magnetic resonance imaging (rs-fMRI) has enabled non-invasive mapping of functional brain interactions for computer-aided diagnosis, yet most existing approaches reduce inter-regional relationships to correlation-based edge weights. Such representations capture co-fluctuation strength but obscure how information is shared across brain regions. Because brain disorders may disrupt not only connectivity strength but also the organization of redundancy, uniqueness and synergy, traditional functional connectivity may miss disease-relevant information structures. Here we introduce IID-GCN, an interpretable graph learning framework that decomposes rs-fMRI interactions into redundancy, uniqueness and synergy graphs using partial entropy decomposition. These information-specific graphs separately characterize shared, region-specific and jointly emergent components of brain activity. A multi-channel graph convolutional network then integrates the decomposed graphs through edge recalibration, cross-information interaction, ROI-attention readout and channel-attentive fusion. Across three datasets, IID-GCN consistently captures complementary diagnostic information beyond traditional functional connectivity. The learned information profiles reveal disorder-specific patterns of altered redundancy, uniqueness and synergy, suggesting that brain diseases reshape functional information organization rather than merely changing connection strength. These results establish information-decomposed brain graphs as an interpretable representation for rs-fMRI-based diagnosis. Our code is available at https://github.com/Zdy12/IID-GCN.

</details>


### [148] [Conscious Access as Continuous-to-Discrete Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20723)
*Tianming Yang*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个实用主义转向，将意识研究从形而上学的"困难问题"转向计算建模。作者引入C/D框架，认为大脑包含两个系统：System C（分布式连续流形上的感觉-运动网络）和System D（离散尺度不变符号的中央引擎），并主张"意识访问"是一种保持结构的翻译运算，将连续的局部状态映射为离散符号令牌，再通过逆投影回感觉-运动动力学。该框架在表征几何层面提出可测试的预测（从梯度相似结构到低维类别等价类的坍缩），以区别于现有主流意识理论，并为神经符号人工智能提供架构蓝图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 意识研究长期停滞于关于"困难问题"的本体论争论，缺乏实证可推进的路径。作者认为应放弃追问"意识在形而上层面是什么"，转而将意识访问建模为具体的计算变换，以解决神经科学和人工智能领域的现有瓶颈。

Method: 提出Continuous/Discrete (C/D)框架：大脑实现两种处理模式——System C（连续高维流形上的分布式感觉-运动网络）和System D（围绕离散、尺度不变符号组织的中央引擎）。意识访问被定义为这两个系统之间的结构保持翻译（continuous-to-discrete conversion），包括前向映射（连续状态→离散符号令牌）和逆投影（符号→感觉-运动动力学接地）。据此推导以表征几何为核心的一组统一可测试预测，涉及从分级相似结构到低维类别等价类的可测坍缩。

Result: 该框架推导出可操作的表征几何预测，明确将其与全局神经元工作空间理论、整合信息理论、预测加工和高阶理论等主流意识理论区分开来。此外，该框架为神经符号人工智能提供了原理性架构，表明意识访问作为翻译式计算具有实证可追踪性，无需解决现象学困难问题。

Conclusion: 意识访问可被功能性地理解为连续-离散翻译计算，这提供了一条务实且经验上可行的研究路径：在不必解决"困难问题"的前提下，澄清意识状态在功能层面完成了什么。

Abstract: The scientific study of consciousness frequently stalls on ontological debates regarding the "Hard Problem." This paper proposes a pragmatic pivot. Rather than asking what consciousness is metaphysically, we ask how modeling conscious access as a specific computational transformation may address existing bottlenecks in neuroscience and artificial intelligence. We introduce the Continuous/Discrete (C/D) framework, which holds that the brain implements two distinct processing regimes: System C, a distributed sensory-motor network operating over continuous, high-dimensional manifolds, and System D, a centralized engine structured around discrete, scale-invariant symbols. We argue that conscious access requires a structure-preserving translation between these regimes, which maps localized continuous states onto discrete symbolic tokens, coupled with an inverse projection that grounds those tokens back into sensorimotor dynamics. By formalizing conscious access as this continuous-to-discrete conversion, we derive a unified set of testable predictions centered on representational geometry, specifically, on a measurable collapse from graded similarity structures to low-dimensional categorical equivalence classes. These predictions explicitly differentiate our account from Global Neuronal Workspace Theory, Integrated Information Theory, Predictive Processing, and Higher-Order Theories, shifting the focus from ontological status to computational mechanism. Beyond neuroscience, the framework provides a principled architecture for neuro-symbolic artificial intelligence. We argue that treating conscious access as translational computation offers a pragmatic, empirically tractable pathway forward, clarifying what conscious states functionally accomplish without requiring resolution of the hard problem of phenomenology.

</details>

### [149] [PEN-STACK: A non-fabricating tool layer for language-model agents in genome writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20412)
*Anees Ahmed Mahaboob Ali,Radhakrishnan Delhibabu,Everette Jacob Remington Nelson*

Main category: q-bio.GN

TL;DR: PEN-STACK是一个开放工具层，为生物学语言模型智能体提供基因组编写所需的全部定量参数并保证可验证来源。该工具层提供十个设计阶段共22个工具，强制每个数值必须来自已验证工具，从而消除模型编造。测试表明无工具时三个模型家族编造了90.8%-98.8%的数值，而驱动工具后四目标审计中零编造；但在粗粒度分辨率下表达稳健性验证不通过，十个预注册声明中八个未通过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体在生物学中广泛应用，但它们报告数值时缺乏可验证的来源，且存在生物安全风险。基因组编写放大了这两个问题：编写计划必须指明位置、写作酶、载体和递送工具，这些参数都是量化且相互依赖的，没有集成的工具层，智能体只能凭编造提供这些数值。

Method: PEN-STACK提供十个基因组编写设计阶段，共22个范围感知工具，可通过SDK、模型上下文协议服务器和REST接口访问。系统采用类型强制不变式：每个数值必须来源于已验证工具。同时包含发射前生物安全筛查和表达稳健性评估轴，并返回机器可读的降级标志。

Result: 无工具情况下，三个模型家族在naive prompt下编造了90.8%-98.8%的240个所需数值；经指导后残差降至0-4个，但无模型达到零编造。在工具驱动下，同一批模型在四目标审计中零编造。发射前生物安全筛查对全部八个设计匹配了专家标签。表达稳健性轴在精确位点分辨率下有效（ρ=0.571），但默认粗分辨率下不通过（ρ≈0.16）。十个预注册声明中八个未通过审查。

Conclusion: 证据表明，消除编造的根源是"接地"（grounding）而非提示工程或模型规模，而接地需要一个底层支撑平台。四目标审计的接地实验值得在240字段规模上复制。PEN-STACK以开放可导入代码的形式为智能体基因组工程系统提供了这一平台。

Abstract: Background. Language-model agents are widely used in biology, but they report quantities without a verifiable source and pose unmanaged biosecurity risks. Genome writing sharpens both: a write plan must specify a location, writer enzyme, cargo, and delivery vehicle, all quantitative and interdependent, so without an integrated tool layer, the agent must supply them. We introduce PEN-STACK, an open tool layer that supplies them with guaranteed provenance. Results. PEN-STACK provides ten genome-writing design stages as twenty-two scope-aware tools, accessible via a software development kit, a Model Context Protocol server, and a Representational State Transfer interface, under a type-enforced invariant: every quantity must originate from a validated tool. Without tools, three model families fabricated 90.8% to 98.8% of the 240 required quantities under a naive prompt; coaching left a residual of 0 to 4, with no model certified at zero. Driving the tools, the same models fabricated nothing on a four-goal audit. A pre-emission biosecurity screen matched expert labels on all eight designs. The expression-robustness axis validated at exact-site resolution (ρ= 0.571, n = 1,506) but not at the coarser resolution served by default (ρ\approx 0.16), which returns a machine-readable downgrade flag. Eight of ten pre-registered claims did not pass, each flagged as machine-readable. Conclusions. On this evidence, grounding, not prompting or model scale, removes fabrication, and grounding requires a substrate; the grounded arm, a four-goal audit, warrants replication at the 240-field scale. PEN-STACK provides that substrate as open, importable code for agentic genome-engineering systems.

</details>


### [150] [BioFirewall: A genome-writing-native governance layer for design-stage biosecurity screening of agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20413)
*Anees Ahmed Mahaboob Ali,Radhakrishnan Delhibabu,Everette Jacob Remington Nelson*

Main category: q-bio.GN

TL;DR: 本文提出BioFirewall，一个规则驱动的中间件系统，在基因组编写的设计阶段拦截编辑计划并进行生物安全审查。该系统沿五个风险轴（货物、位点、编辑类型、生殖系和规模）返回允许、标记审查或拒绝三种决策，附有签名护照和防篡改审计日志。实验表明，函数感知的货物分类器在1%误报率下达到0.72的真阳性率，优于前沿和开源语言模型判断器；开源模型在提示注入下易被绕过，而确定性屏幕保持不变；288个合法计划无一被拒绝，假拒绝率95%上限为0.0103。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生物安全控制仅限于两个环节：基础模型上的拒答护栏和合成器端的序列身份筛查。而介于两者之间的设计阶段——即基因组编写计划被具体指定的关键环节——仅靠建议而非任何已部署的系统来治理，存在显著的安全漏洞。

Method: BioFirewall是一个规则治理的中间件，在基因组编写计划写入时拦截并沿五个风险轴（货物、位点、编辑类型、生殖系、规模）进行评估，输出允许、标记审查或拒绝。系统包含带引文证据的判定、签名设计护照、防篡改审计日志和分级访问控制。评估采用去环化的安全代理基准，与独立预言机对比货物分类器与语言模型判断器，并测试了提示注入抗性和跨调用分解攻击防御。

Result: 函数感知的货物分类器在1%误报率下真阳性率为0.72（95% CI 0.43-0.89），而前沿和开源语言模型无法可靠筛选相同序列。在提示注入下，开源权重模型在每通道6次试验中有3次和5次将阻止判定翻转为允许，而确定性屏幕保持不可侵犯。288个合法计划无一被拒绝，给出假拒绝率95%上限0.0103。会话监控器拦截了跨调用分解攻击。位点轴在持出基因集上富集了体内插入性肿瘤发生驱动因子（AUROC 0.605，OR 3.34）。

Conclusion: 设计阶段的生物安全治理在实践中是可行的。BioFirewall作为开源软件发布，附带预注册、开放数据、可复现的基准测试，为基因组编写流程的中间环节提供了首个可部署的安全基础设施。

Abstract: Background. Artificial-intelligence design tools now plan genome-scale edits, and agentic systems execute those plans with progressively less human oversight. Biosecurity controls are limited to two points: refusal guardrails at the foundation model and sequence-identity screening at the synthesiser. The design stage between them, where the plan is specified, remains governed by recommendations rather than any deployed system.
  Results. We present BioFirewall, a rule-governed middleware that intercepts a genome-writing plan and returns allow, flag-for-review, or refuse across five hazard axes native to genome writing: cargo, locus, edit type, germline and scale, with cited evidence, a signed design passport, a tamper-evident audit log, and tiered access. On a de-circularised benchmark of safe proxies scored against independent oracles, a function-aware cargo classifier reached a true-positive rate of 0.72 (95% CI 0.43 to 0.89) at a 1% false-positive rate, whereas frontier and open language-model judges did not screen the same sequences reliably. Under prompt injection, the open-weight judges flipped their blocking verdict to allow in 3 and 5 of 6 trials per channel, while the deterministic screen remained invariant. None of 288 legitimate plans from three templates was refused, yielding a certified 95% upper bound of 0.0103 on the false-refuse rate, and a session monitor intercepted cross-call decomposition attacks. On a held-out gene set, the locus axis was enriched for drivers of in vivo insertional oncogenesis (AUROC 0.605; odds ratio 3.34).
  Conclusions. Design-stage governance is achievable in practice. BioFirewall is released as open source with a pre-registered, open-data-reproducible benchmark.

</details>


### [151] [mLS-GKM: Efficient Multi-class Regulatory Sequence Classification with Gapped k-mer SVMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20576)
*Kieran Howard,Nathan Harmston*

Main category: q-bio.GN

TL;DR: 本文提出mLS-GKM，扩展了LS-GKM框架，增加了多分类支持、概率校准预测、并行化推理、内存高效的序列解释以及训练过程中的检查点功能。在322个ENCODE ChIP-seq数据集上验证，分类器结果与LS-GKM一致，gkmpredict和gkmexplain分别提速22倍和80倍（64线程），gkmexplain峰值内存减少超过65%。实际演示表明其能准确区分增强子、启动子和CTCF结合位点，并识别生物学相关的调控基序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的gkm-SVM方法（如LS-GKM）虽然高效，但局限于二分类任务且不提供校准的概率输出，难以满足现代大规模调控基因组学分析中多分类和概率解释的需求。

Method: 在LS-GKM的基础上进行四项关键扩展：（1）引入多分类支持，（2）增加概率校准输出，（3）实现并行化推理，（4）优化gkmexplain的内存效率并添加训练检查点功能。

Result: 在322个ENCODE ChIP-seq数据集上，mLS-GKM的分类器输出与LS-GKM完全一致，验证了正确性；gkmpredict和gkmexplain在64线程下分别提速22倍和80倍，gkmexplain峰值内存降低超过65%。实际应用中能准确区分增强子、启动子和CTCF结合位点并识别相关调控基序。

Conclusion: mLS-GKM将gkm-SVM扩展到了多分类问题，并大幅提升了可扩展性，使得在大规模和复杂数据集中高效解释调控DNA序列成为可能，为调控基因组学提供了更强大的工具。

Abstract: Gapped k-mer support vector machines (gkm-SVMs) are widely used for classifying regulatory DNA sequences and identifying the sequence features underlying those predictions. Although LS-GKM provides an efficient implementation of gkm-based kernels, it is restricted to binary classification and does not provide calibrated probability outputs. Here, we present mLS-GKM, an extension of LS-GKM that adds multiclass classification, probability-calibrated predictions, parallelised inference, memory efficient sequence interpretation and checkpointing during training. Across 322 ENCODE ChIP-seq datasets, classifiers trained using mLS-GKM are identical to those produced by LS-GKM, while gkmpredict and gkmexplain run 22x and 80x faster respectively at 64 threads, and gkmexplain peak memory is reduced by more than 65%. As a practical demonstration, mLS-GKM was able to accurately distinguish between enhancers, promoters and CTCF binding sites directly from sequence and identified biologically relevant regulatory motifs. Together, these improvements extend gkm-SVMs to multiclass problems and substantially improve their scalability, enabling efficient interpretation of regulatory DNA sequences in large and complex datasets

</details>

### [152] [Harmonic Torsional Diffusion for Protein-Ligand Flexible Docking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20366)
*Maksim Zhdanov,Pavel Strashnov,Vladislav Kurenkov*

Main category: q-bio.BM

TL;DR: Harmony提出了一种谐波扭转扩散框架，用于柔性蛋白质-配体对接。它将配体和侧链扭转评分场参数化为圆上学习到的谐波势的导数，利用方差爆炸扩散在环面上的热半群解析提供噪声依赖，从而显式建模周期性并赋予模型频率感知的旋转运动归纳偏置。在PDBBind和PoseBusters基准上，Harmony在配体姿态精度、口袋全原子重建和物理有效性方面优于现有柔性对接方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的对接模型使用通用的欧几里得头部预测扭转更新，忽略了角变量的周期性几何结构。这种不匹配在柔性对接中尤其受限，因为配体构象和口袋侧链需要协同适应以形成结合复合物。作者旨在通过对齐分数参数化与扩散过程的几何结构来改进柔性对接。

Method: Harmony采用谐波扭转扩散框架：将配体和侧链扭转的分数场参数化为圆上学习到的谐波势的导数；噪声级依赖由方差爆炸扩散在环面上的热半群解析提供；这种构造使周期性显式化，并赋予模型频率感知的归纳偏置（针对旋转异构运动）。

Result: 在PDBBind基准上，Harmony在配体姿态准确性和口袋全原子重建方面优于近期柔性对接方法；在PoseBusters上，提升了生成复合物的物理有效性；通过EBNA1（极性结合位点）和KRAS G12D（浅结合位点）的案例研究验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 将分数参数化与扩散过程的几何结构对齐是提升柔性对接性能的一个简单而有效的杠杆点。周期性的显式建模和频率感知的归纳偏置为扩散模型在扭转空间中的应用提供了更好的设计原则。

Abstract: Molecular docking requires reasoning jointly about ligand pose and protein flexibility. Most diffusion-based docking models predict torsional updates with generic Euclidean heads that ignore the periodic geometry of angular variables. This mismatch is especially limiting in flexible docking, where ligand conformations and pocket side chains co-adapt to form the bound complex. Here, we introduce Harmony, a harmonic torsional diffusion framework for flexible protein-ligand docking. Harmony parameterizes ligand and side-chain torsional score fields as derivatives of learned harmonic potentials on the circle, whose noise-level dependence is supplied analytically by the heat semigroup of variance-exploding diffusion on the torus. This construction makes periodicity explicit and gives the model a frequency-aware inductive bias over rotameric motion. On the PDBBind benchmark, Harmony improves ligand pose accuracy and pocket all-atom reconstruction over recent flexible docking methods. On PoseBusters, it improves the physical validity of generated complexes. Case studies on EBNA1 and KRAS G12D illustrate the method's behavior on a polar and a shallow binding site, respectively. Together, these results indicate that aligning the score parameterization with the geometry of the diffusion process is a simple and effective lever for improving flexible docking.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [Event-triggered Implicit Perturbation for Zeroth-Order Fine-Tuning of Spiking Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21223)
*Tengteng Lei,Prabodh Katti,Rashi Dutt,Houssem Sifaou,Tan Peng,Osvaldo Simeone,Kai Xu,Bipin Rajendran*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种隐式扰动零阶优化（IPZO）架构，用于在存内计算（IMC）加速器上高效微调脉冲神经网络（SNN）。通过事件触发的扰动生成单元（PGU）将扰动和与IMC阵列的加权和合并，消除了显式权重扰动带来的重复读-修改-写（RMW）操作和随机数生成器（RNG）的面积开销。并进一步提出地址驱动的XOR重组方案（PGU-XOR）来缓解直接RNG复用带来的空间相关性。实验表明，PGU-XOR在精度上接近软件RNG，且相比显式扰动可将扰动能耗降至0.46x-0.83x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零阶优化（ZO）仅需前向传播即可估计梯度，适合训练非可微的事件驱动SNN，但在IMC加速器上部署受到两个制约：显式权重扰动导致的重复RMW操作，以及为每个权重生成统计独立扰动所需的RNG硬件开销过大。

Method: 提出IPZO架构：事件触发的PGU利用脉冲稀疏性，仅对脉冲激活的权重行生成并累加扰动贡献，从而缩减RNG阵列的行维度；为缓解直接RNG复用（PGU-Reuse）带来的空间相关性，引入地址驱动XOR重组方案（PGU-XOR），通过XOR运算对复用的随机数进行去相关处理。

Result: (1) PGU-XOR在Spikingformer/CIFAR-10上准确率76.41% vs 软件RNG 76.53%，在SpikeGPT/WikiText-2上PPL 54.20 vs 53.23；PGU-Reuse则准确率下降9.56个百分点、PPL增加11.8。(2) 在TSMC 16nm工艺下，PGU-XOR相比PGU-Reuse每次矩阵-向量乘增加40.3%-46.0%面积和15.2%-48.9%能耗，但更快收敛使等精度下总扰动能耗仅为PGU-Reuse的0.51x。(3) 在B=64、T=4下，IPZO相较传统显式权重扰动将扰动能耗降至0.46x-0.83x，且优势随BT减小而增大。

Conclusion: IPZO架构通过隐式扰动和脉冲稀疏感知的PGU设计，在保持IMC权重驻留执行的同时消除了扰动诱导的RMW开销，PGU-XOR方案在精度和能效之间取得了良好平衡，为ZO在IMC上微调SNN提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization estimates gradients using only forward-pass evaluations, making it suitable for fine-tuning non-differentiable, event-driven spiking neural networks (SNNs). However, its deployment on in-memory computing (IMC) accelerators is constrained by the repeated read-modify-write (RMW) operations arising from explicit weight perturbation and the prohibitive hardware footprint of random number generators (RNGs) for statistically independent per-weight perturbations. To address these challenges, we propose an implicit-perturbation ZO (IPZO) architecture in which perturbation sums computed by an event-triggered perturbation generation unit (PGU) are combined with the weighted sums produced by the IMC array, eliminating perturbation-induced RMW operations while preserving weight-stationary execution of IMC. By exploiting spike sparsity, the PGU generates and accumulates perturbation contributions only for spike-activated weight rows, reducing the required row dimension of the RNG array. An address-driven XOR recombination scheme (PGU-XOR) is further introduced to mitigate the spatial correlations caused by direct RNG reuse (PGU-Reuse). The results show that (1) PGU-XOR matches software RNGs in accuracy on Spikingformer/CIFAR-10 (76.41% vs. 76.53%) and perplexity (PPL) on SpikeGPT/WikiText-2 (54.20 vs. 53.23), whereas PGU-Reuse degrades accuracy by 9.56 percentage points and increases PPL by 11.8; (2) implemented in a TSMC 16-nm CMOS technology, PGU-XOR incurs 40.3%-46.0% area and 15.2%-48.9% energy overhead per matrix-vector multiplication relative to PGU-Reuse, yet its faster convergence reduces the total perturbation energy to 0.51x that of PGU-Reuse at iso-accuracy; (3) IPZO reduces the perturbation energy to 0.46x-0.83x that of conventional explicit weight perturbation for a batch size of B=64 and T=4 time steps, with the advantage growing as BT decreases.

</details>


### [154] [SPICE: Speculative Prefetching with Low-Rank Expert Surrogates and Heterogeneous Orchestration for MoE Inference Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21240)
*Yongxiang Lyu,Ning Li,Bonian Jia*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: SPICE是一种面向MoE模型卸载的推测性预取框架，结合轻量级专家预测与置信度感知的CPU-GPU异构编排，在DeepSeek-V2-Lite和Qwen2-57B-A14B上实现最高3.12倍TPOT加速且质量损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型因稀疏激活将模型容量与计算成本解耦而被广泛用于LLM，但大量专家参数超出GPU显存容量，导致推理延迟主要由主机到设备的PCIe传输（专家加载）主导，现有方法缺乏对专家卸载场景下预测与编排的整体优化。

Method: SPICE包含两个核心组件：（1）构建与目标MoE架构对齐的轻量级草稿模型，采用置信度感知的自适应前瞻算法预取高置信度专家；（2）当推测未命中时，切换至成本感知的CPU-GPU异构编排——低置信度未命中由驻留共享专家配合低秩专家（LoRE）代理进行近似处理，精确残差工作则卸载至CPU，与GPU当前计算异步并行执行。

Result: 在DeepSeek-V2-Lite和Qwen2-57B-A14B模型以及多种GPU平台上，SPICE实现了最高3.12倍的Time Per Output Token（TPOT）加速，且质量损失极小。

Conclusion: 有效的MoE卸载不仅需要预测未来的专家，还需要决定哪些未命中值得近似处理、哪些需要精确恢复，以及精确残差工作应在何处执行——即"预测+近似决策+执行位置"三位一体的编排策略。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models are increasingly used in LLMs because sparse activation decouples model capacity from compute cost. However, the large expert parameter footprint often exceeds GPU memory capacity, making inference latency dominated by the host-to-device PCIe transfers for expert loading. To address these challenges, this paper presents SPICE, a speculative prefetching framework for MoE offloading that combines lightweight expert prediction with confidence-aware CPU-GPU orchestration. On one hand, SPICE builds a lightweight draft model aligned with the target MoE architecture, using a confidence-aware adaptive lookahead algorithm to prefetch high-confidence experts. On the other hand, when speculative predictions miss, SPICE switches to a cost-aware CPU-GPU heterogeneous orchestration: low-confidence misses are approximated by the resident shared expert with low rank expert (LoRE) surrogates, while exact residual work is offloaded to the CPU and executed asynchronously in parallel with ongoing GPU computation. Evaluated on DeepSeek-V2-Lite and Qwen2-57B-A14B across diverse GPU platforms, SPICE achieves up to 3.12 speedup in Time Per Output Token (TPOT) with minimal quality loss, showing that effective MoE offloading requires not only predicting future experts, but also deciding which misses deserve approximation, which require exact recovery, and where exact residual work should execute.

</details>


### [155] [Assessing Triple Modular Redundancy for Wide-Link, Low-Latency NoC Routers: Reliability and Physical Design Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21288)
*Chen Wu,Michael Rogenmoser,Luca Benini,Angelo Garofalo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文针对物理AI tile加速器中NoC路由器在恶劣环境下面临单粒子效应(SEE)的可靠性问题，在TSMC 7nm工艺下实现了2周期延迟、512位超宽链路的NoC路由器，并评估了三种粒度（粗粒度、仅状态、全）的三模冗余(TMR)方案。结果表明，仅全TMR能完全消除超过一百万次注入故障中的所有失效；虽然独立路由器面积开销高达7.04倍，但集成到完整AI加速器tile后仅增加16.8%面积和15.2%功耗，且关键路径完全不受影响，证明了在先进工艺节点下全TMR是一种实用可行的NoC保护方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可靠NoC研究主要聚焦于窄链路（如32位）、深流水线路由器以及单粒子翻转(SEU)，但技术演进已转向采用先进工艺节点、频率超过1GHz的超宽链路低延迟NoC路由器。针对这些更易受单粒子瞬态(SET)影响的新兴设计，缺乏系统性的可靠性评估，需要探索如何在保证可靠性的同时控制成本和功耗。

Method: 在TSMC 7nm工艺下对2周期延迟、512位宽链路的NoC路由器实现三种粒度的TMR（粗粒度、仅状态、全TMR），执行RTL到GDSII的物理设计流程，并开展RTL级和网表级的SEU与SET故障注入实验。评估从独立路由器扩展到完整AI加速器tile（含处理器和L1存储器）的系统级可靠性与开销。

Result: 粗粒度和仅状态TMR无法提供充分保护；全TMR在每项实验超过一百万次注入故障中消除了所有观测到的失效。独立全TMR路由器面积开销为7.04倍，但集成到完整AI加速器tile后仅增加16.8%面积和15.2%功耗（GEMM基准下），关键路径完全不受影响。

Conclusion: 先进工艺节点提供了足够的布线资源，使得全TMR成为保护物理AI系统中NoC免受SEE影响的实用且可部署的解决方案，尤其适用于运行在恶劣环境中的系统。全TMR在系统层面成本可控，具有良好的工程可行性。

Abstract: Protecting the Network-on-Chip (NoC) of physical-AI tile-based accelerators deployed in harsh environments against single-event effects (SEEs) is paramount for preventing NoC failures that can lead to deadlocks and silent data corruption (SDC). Prior work on reliable NoCs has largely focused on narrow links (e.g., 32-bit), deeply pipelined routers, and single-event upsets (SEUs). However, the state of the art has evolved toward low-latency NoC routers with ultra-wide links, implemented on advanced technology nodes and operating at frequencies above 1 GHz. We evaluate the cost and reliability trade-offs of implementing Triple Modular Redundancy (TMR) at three granularities (coarse, state-only, and full) for a 2-cycle-latency NoC router with 512-bit wide links. We carry out RTL-to-GDSII physical design in TSMC 7nm technology, as well as both RTL- and netlist-level SEU and SET fault injection campaigns. We evaluate the three TMR approaches in terms of reliability, cost, and physical design strategies, further extending the assessment from a standalone router to a full AI acceleration tile. Our results show that state-only and coarse-grained TMR do not provide sufficient protection against SEEs, whereas full TMR eliminates all observed failures across more than one million injected faults per experiment. Although the standalone full-TMR router incurs a 7.04x area overhead, this cost is drastically amortized once integrated into a complete AI accelerator tile with processors and local L1 memories: the same design adds only 16.8% area and 15.2% power consumption under a GEMM benchmark at the system level, with the critical path of the tile entirely unaffected. These results demonstrate that advanced technology nodes provide sufficient routing capacity to make full TMR a practical and deployable solution for protecting NoCs in Physical AI systems operating in harsh environments.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [Humanoid Musical Robots as Experimental Interfaces for Music-Evoked Emotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20433)
*Vincent K. M. Cheung,Jia-Yeu Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将音乐人形机器人作为研究音乐诱发情绪的受控实验界面，主张其相比传统预录音频刺激范式能在多模态、具身化和交互层面提供更加有效的实验控制，并通过早稻田萨克斯管机器人WAS-5的案例论证其技术可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音乐诱发情绪研究依赖于预录音频刺激，无法捕捉真实音乐体验中的多模态、具身性与交互性特征，限制了研究的生态效度与实验结果的有效性。

Method: 这是一篇立场论文（position paper）。作者基于音乐认知与情绪理论，识别需要同时操纵声学与非声学变量才能揭示的机制；论证人形机器人因其参数化表演控制、跨试验可复现性、以及可解耦和重组听觉/视觉/交互组件的能力而具备独特优势；并以WAS-5（早稻田萨克斯管演奏者机器人5号）为例进行了技术可行性演示。

Result: 通过WAS-5案例展示了机器人对声学变量和交互变量的可复现控制，证明了满足未来音乐-情绪实验的先决条件，确立了音乐人形机器人作为方法论平台的技术基础。

Conclusion: 音乐人形机器人能够作为方法论平台，支撑未来对音乐诱发情绪的受控研究，为克服当前范式局限提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Advances in technology have led to increasingly sophisticated musical humanoid robots. However, their use has largely been limited to performance and related research in human-robot interaction. In this position paper, we propose a novel perspective: musical humanoid robots as experimental interfaces for investigating music-evoked emotions. We argue that current research is constrained by paradigms relying on pre-recorded auditory stimuli, which fail to capture the multimodal, embodied, and interactive nature of real-world musical experience. Building on existing theories of music cognition and emotion, we identify mechanisms that require controlled manipulation of both acoustic and non-acoustic variables. We show that humanoid robots are well-suited as they enable parametric control of performance variables, reproducibility across trials, and the decoupling and recombination of auditory, visual, and interactive components. We illustrate the technical feasibility of this perspective through a case study of the WAseda Saxophonist Robot 5 (WAS-5), demonstrating reproducible control of acoustic and interaction variables that are prerequisites for future music-emotion experiments. Our work positions musical humanoid robots as a methodological platform that enables future controlled investigations of music-evoked emotions.

</details>


### [157] [EndoLIFT: Language-Disambiguated Latent-Conditioned Rectified Flow for Bidirectional Endoscopic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20478)
*Chi Kit Ng,Yidong Zhang,Lui Siu Hing,Jinsong Lin,Tianchun Wu,Ho Yin Chim,Zhiqing Tang,Tao Yang,Huxin Gao,Trevor Yeung,Raymond Shing-Yan Tang,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 EndoLIFT，一种视觉-语言-动作策略，通过显式语言意图条件化和潜在条件化整流流动作专家来解决双向内镜控制中的"意图别名"（intent aliasing）问题，即在视觉场景相同但动作方向相反的歧义场景下，利用语言指令选择轴向模式，实验显示其在导航方向准确率、反向推进减少和闭环节成功等指标上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规胃肠内镜检查本质上是双向的：器械前进到达目标解剖部位，后退或倒转进行观察，而外部指令可能要求提前反转方向。当请求的阶段变化早于视觉场景变化时，几乎相同的观测可能需要完全相反的轴向动作。这种"意图别名"（intent aliasing）歧义使得仅靠视觉信息无法正确判断动作方向，需要额外的意图信号（如语言指令）来消解歧义。

Method: EndoLIFT由三部分组成：(1) 接收RGB图像、语言指令和上一动作状态的策略输入；(2) 显式语言意图条件化（language-based intent conditioning），用于选择轴向模式（前进/后退）；(3) 32维变分轨迹潜变量（VTL）随机条件化的整流流（rectified-flow）动作专家，用于生成连续动作块。作者还设计了受控的相同观测指令交换实验（same-observation instruction swaps）以及一个1比特模式标志参考模型作为架构对照。

Result: 与无潜变量条件化的匹配模型相比，EndoLIFT在导航方向准确率上提升11.1个百分点，错误方向前进减少83%。在44个未见语言变体上保持82.8%的意图跟随准确率。闭环节评估中，在结肠模体（seen）以及肺和胃模体（unseen）上，EndoLIFT相比无VTL版本整体成功率提升30个百分点，并在10/10离体猪气管试验中全部成功。

Conclusion: 实验结果将语言驱动的意图选择与轨迹潜变量对方向正确性和稳健回缩的贡献分开验证：语言负责轴向模式选择，轨迹潜变量提升方向正确性和回缩鲁棒性。EndoLIFT证明了语言条件化在解决双向内镜控制中意图别名问题的有效性。

Abstract: Routine gastrointestinal endoscopy is intrinsically bidirectional: the instrument is advanced to reach target anatomy and later withdrawn or retroflexed for inspection, while an external cue may require earlier reversal. When the requested phase changes before the visual scene does, nearly identical observations can require opposite axial actions. We identify and formalize this ambiguity in bidirectional endoscopic control as intent aliasing. We propose EndoLIFT (Endoscopic Language-Instruction Flow with Trajectory Latents), a vision-language-action policy that combines explicit language-based intent conditioning with a latent-conditioned rectified-flow action expert. The policy receives RGB, a language instruction, and the previous-action state; a 32-D variational trajectory latent stochastically conditions continuous action-chunk generation. Controlled same-observation instruction swaps establish that language selects the axial mode, independently of whether the trajectory latent is present. Relative to the matched model without latent conditioning, EndoLIFT improves navigation-direction accuracy by 11.1 percentage points and reduces wrong-direction advance by 83\%. An architecture-controlled 1-bit mode-flag reference exhibits weaker canonical-anchor switching, while EndoLIFT retains 82.8\% intent-following accuracy across 44 held-out linguistic variants. In closed-loop evaluation, EndoLIFT improves overall success by 30 percentage points over EndoLIFT w/o VTL on both the seen colon phantom and the unseen lung and stomach phantoms, and completes 10/10 ex-vivo porcine-trachea trials. These results separate language-based intent selection from the trajectory latent's contribution to directional correctness and robust retraction.

</details>


### [158] [Koala Gripper: Co-designing Robotic Grippers and Data-Capture Devices for Scaling Dexterous Manipulation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20546)
*Amar Hajj-Ahmad,Zubin Kremer Guha,Tim Fofonoff,Zhi Ern Teoh,Ciarán T. O'Neill,Ben Thacher,Igor Fala,Vidullan Surendran,Murphy Wonsick,Peter Whitney,David Watkins*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种手部数据采集设备与机器人执行夹具的协同设计框架，并据此开发了Koala Gripper系统——一种兼具灵巧性、抓取能力、可扩展性和易用性的数据采集与机械手平台，其设计包含力优化的手指/扳机连杆机构、整体式双拇指和符合人体工学的设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大规模操作数据集的持续增长，手持式机器人夹具数据采集的重要性日益凸显。然而，现有数据采集设备设计趋向于匹配已有机器人夹具的形态，牺牲了人机工程学与操作性能。需要一种协同设计方法，同时考虑数据采集设备与机器人执行设备的平台约束，以兼顾操作灵巧性和采集效率。

Method: 提出协同设计框架，将数据采集设备和机器人执行设备的平台约束同时纳入设计过程。该框架指导Koala Gripper的设计：(1) 力优化的手指/扳机连杆机构，具有方向反射质量特性；(2) 独特的整体式双拇指设计；(3) 以用户为中心的人体工学设计；(4) 驱动手指具有反向可驱动性，有效质量在几十克量级。

Result: Koala Gripper相较于平行夹爪提升了灵巧性和抓取能力，同时保持可扩展性和易用性。实验表明该夹具能够对广泛物体实现可靠抓取、用力工具操作和精确分拣，并通过端到端数据采集与策略执行管线验证了其通过示教学习（LfD）的性能。

Conclusion: 协同设计框架有效解决了数据采集设备与执行设备形态失配的问题，Koala Gripper证明了在保持数据采集易用性的同时提升机器人操作性能是可行的，为大规模操作数据集的采集与部署提供了新的平台方案。

Abstract: As the demand for larger manipulation datasets grows, handheld robotic gripper data collection and the associated gripper designs become more vital. Current data collection device designs trend towards matching the morphologies of existing robotic grippers, sacrificing ergonomics and manipulation performance. In this paper, we propose a co-design framework that guides the simultaneous development of both data collection and robotic execution devices by weaving both platform constraints into the design process. Through this workflow, we present the Koala Gripper system, a data capture device and robotic gripper platform that improves dexterity and grasp capability compared to parallel jaw grippers while preserving scalability and ease-of-use. The design introduces a novel force-optimized finger/trigger linkage mechanism with directional reflected mass characteristics, a unique monolithic dual-thumb, and user-centered ergonomic design. The design's actuated robotic fingers are backdrivable, with effective mass on the order of tens of grams. We show that these grippers are capable of secure grasps over a wide range of objects, forceful tool use, and precise singulation. We further validate the platform by deploying it with an end-to-end data collection and policy execution pipeline that highlights its capabilities through learning from demonstration. More information available at http://koalagripper.rai-inst.com

</details>


### [159] [Logic-VLA: A Temporal Logic Conditioned Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20556)
*Celina Shiyu Wang,Yiqi Zhao,Junjie Ye,Yue Wang,Jyotirmoy V. Deshmukh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Logic-VLA是一种形式化需求感知的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，通过语法图STL编码器和两阶段策略适应（STL条件监督微调+轨迹级偏好优化），在保持自然语言任务性能的同时显著提升信号时序逻辑（STL）规范的满足率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型虽然能跟随自然语言指令执行任务，但自然语言指令难以精确表达安全关键或时空约束需求。这导致在需要严格满足形式化逻辑规范（如时序、空间约束）的任务中，传统VLA模型可能产生不符合要求的行为。

Method: Logic-VLA包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于语法图的STL编码器，通过预训练捕获时序逻辑语义；(2) 第一阶段：STL条件监督微调（STL-conditioned SFT），在满足规范的演示数据上训练；(3) 第二阶段：基于流匹配代理的Identity Preference Optimization（IPO）进行轨迹级偏好优化，在匹配的满足-违反轨迹对上进行训练。

Result: 在闭环四旋翼导航仿真（随机的逼真环境）中，相比不考虑STL的基线策略，Logic-VLA将STL满意度提高了24.8到40.7个百分点，而自然语言任务成功率最多仅下降1.8个百分点。模型还能泛化到训练中未见过的STL公式。

Conclusion: Logic-VLA展示了一个单一的VLA模型能够根据不同的形式化需求自适应调整行为，无需为每种规范单独训练策略，在满足复杂形式化约束的同时保持自然语言任务的完成能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models can follow natural-language (NL) task instructions, but such instructions may not precisely specify safety-critical or spatiotemporal requirements on the resulting behavior. We introduce Logic-VLA, a formal-requirement-aware VLA that conditions on Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specifications supplied at inference time. Logic-VLA uses a syntax-graph-based STL encoder pre-trained to capture temporal logic semantics. Policy adaptation proceeds in two stages: STL-conditioned supervised fine-tuning on satisfying demonstrations is followed by trajectory-level preference optimization over matched satisfying-violating rollout pairs using a flow-matching surrogate for Identity Preference Optimization. This formulation improves formal requirement satisfaction while preserving the nominal NL task. We evaluate Logic-VLA in closed-loop quadcopter navigation simulation across randomized photorealistic environments and test generalization to STL formulas unseen during training. Across the evaluation benchmarks, Logic-VLA improves STL satisfaction rate over an STL-blind base policy by 24.8 to 40.7 percentage points (pp) while reducing nominal NL task success by at most 1.8 pp, showing that a single VLA can adapt its behavior to varying formal requirements without requiring a separate policy for each specification.

</details>


### [160] [Nonlinear Model Predictive Control for Trajectory Tracking of Differentially Flat Fixed-Wing Aerial Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20655)
*Nishanth Bobbili,Pratyaksh Rao,Luca Morando,Luca Masci,Giuseppe Loianno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，将基于微分平坦性的轨迹生成与非线性模型预测控制(NMPC)相结合，用于固定翼无人机(UAV)的规划与控制。该框架引入了风感知采样策略嵌入NMPC中，在强风条件下生成主动考虑风扰的动态可行参考轨迹，同时严格满足气动和输入约束。通过大量仿真和真实飞行实验验证，证明了其在高跟踪精度和鲁棒性方面的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定翼无人机由于非线性动力学、气动极限和环境扰动，其规划与控制极具挑战性。微分平坦性虽能有效生成快速可行的轨迹，但以往多用于无模型控制器，缺乏预测能力且需要大量调参。需要一种兼具计算效率和预测性、约束感知能力的统一框架来提升大翼无人机的轨迹跟踪性能，特别是在强风环境下。

Method: 提出了一个统一框架，核心包括：(1) 基于微分平坦性的轨迹生成模块，保证规划计算高效；(2) 非线性模型预测控制(NMPC)作为底层控制器，提供预测和约束感知能力；(3) 在NMPC框架内嵌入风感知采样策略，生成动态可行的参考轨迹，主动补偿风扰；(4) 严格控制气动约束和控制输入约束。

Result: 通过大量仿真和真实飞行实验验证，该框架在复杂轨迹上表现出改进的跟踪精度和鲁棒性；特别在强风条件下，所提出的风感知采样策略显著提升了系统性能。

Conclusion: 该统一框架有效结合了微分平坦性的高效规划与NMPC的预测约束控制优势，风感知采样策略进一步增强了在强风等扰动环境下的鲁棒性和可行性，为固定翼无人机在实际复杂环境中的高精度轨迹跟踪提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: Planning and control of fixed-wing Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) are challenging due to nonlinear dynamics, aerodynamic limits, and environmental disturbances. Differential flatness offers a principled way to generate fast, feasible trajectories, but its use has largely been confined to model-free controllers, which lack predictive capabilities and demand tuning. In this paper, we propose a unified framework that integrates differential flatness-based trajectory generation with Nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC), combining computationally efficient planning with predictive, constraint-aware control. To further improve robustness, we introduce a wind-aware sampling strategy embedded within the NMPC framework, enabling the generation of dynamically feasible reference trajectories that proactively account for wind disturbances while strictly enforcing aerodynamic and control input constraints. We validate the proposed framework through extensive simulations and real-world flight experiments, demonstrating improved tracking accuracy and robustness for complex trajectories, particularly when using the proposed wind-aware sampling strategy under strong wind conditions.

</details>


### [161] [Rethinking Demonstration Unlearning in Imitation Learning for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20784)
*Jiazhuo Li,Yu Zhang,Yiming Fei,Kangkang Dong,Xiaojun Zhu,Houde Liu,Jinze Tao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向机器人模仿学习的"重训练校准审计"框架，用于评估演示数据遗忘（unlearning）操作的有效性。该框架沿行为（behavior）和证据（evidence）两个轴衡量编辑后的策略是否等同于未接触被删除演示的重训练策略，并通过一致性检验在统计显著性层面验证。实验在多个真实机器人策略类别和仿真环境中验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习的机器人策略训练依赖人类演示数据，用户可能事后要求删除某些演示。重新训练（retrain）是自然基准但成本高昂，因此需要更廉价的策略编辑（editing）算子。然而，现有从机器遗忘领域继承的指标（如遗忘损失或单一成员攻击）无法证明编辑到底从闭环运行的策略中移除了什么，亟需一个能够真正评估编辑结果与重训练一致性的审计框架。

Method: 该方法提出基于重训练校准的双轴审计框架：(1) 行为轴：度量编辑后策略与重训练策略在匹配状态下的动作发散度，并以独立重训练之间的差异作为基线（floor）进行校准；(2) 证据轴：针对每个演示施加成员攻击，以重训练作为零假设（null），同时报告秩（rank）和绝对成员损失水平，避免仅看秩而接受人为抬高损失的算子。最后通过一致性检验（conformal test）将两轴合并为联合重训练一致性假设，并对多个独立重训练组成的"舰队"进行检验以达到常规显著性水平。

Result: 在五个预注册条件下，覆盖三个真实机器人策略类别和两个仿真环境，结果显示两轴可双向解耦：在某些检查点上，编辑可修复任务行为但证据保持不变，或降低证据但行为偏离重训练。在ACT（Action Chunking with Transformers）分支上，重定向编辑（redirect edit）将盲评机器人成功率恢复至20次试验中的18次。

Conclusion: 现有机器遗忘指标不足以评估闭环机器人策略的遗忘效果，所提出的重训练校准审计能从行为和证据两个维度全面刻画编辑算子的实际效果，为机器人演示遗忘提供了更可靠且统计严谨的评测手段。

Abstract: Imitation learning for robotics depends on human demonstrations, some of which people may later ask to remove. Retraining without them is the natural reference, but its cost grows with policy and dataset scale, motivating cheaper operators that edit a trained policy. Metrics inherited from machine unlearning, such as forgetting loss or a single membership attack, do not establish what an edit removed from a policy acting in closed loop. We therefore introduce a retrain-calibrated audit that reads demonstration unlearning along two axes: behavior, whether the edited policy acts like one retrained without the removed demonstrations, and evidence, whether an auditor can still detect it was trained on them. The behavior axis measures action divergence to that retrain at matched states, calibrated by a floor built from independent retrains, so a policy at the floor is as close to a retrain as retrains are to each other. The evidence axis applies a per-demonstration membership attack against a retrain null, reporting both its rank and its absolute member-loss level, since rank alone accepts operators that inflate member losses past the null. A conformal test then combines both axes into one hypothesis of joint retrain consistency, against a fleet of independent retrains large enough to reject at conventional significance. Across five preregistered conditions on three real-robot policy classes and two simulation suites, the axes dissociate in both directions on one checkpoint, as an edit may repair task behavior while leaving evidence unchanged, or reduce evidence while moving behavior away from retraining. On the ACT arm, a redirect edit restores blind-scored robot success to 18 of 20 trials.

</details>


### [162] [Natural Sit-to-Stand Motion Synthesis For Humanoids via Guided Assistance Curricula and Staged Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20823)
*Meet Pal Singh,Vyankatesh Ashtekar,Ashish Dutta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需示范或参考轨迹的强化学习方法，让类人机器人实现从坐姿到站姿（STS）的平滑、类人运动。通过耦合的力/椅子高度课程学习、基于逆运动学生成的姿态采样和生物力学启发奖励设计，单一PPO策略在八个椅子高度上实现了超过97%的平衡站立成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类人机器人从坐姿站起是极具挑战性的控制问题，因为存在无穷多种保持平衡的站起来方式。现有方法依赖示范数据或参考轨迹，限制了泛化能力，且难以应对不同椅子高度的变化。

Method: 采用三个互补组件：(i) 耦合的力/椅子高度课程——垂直骨盆辅助力在训练初期辅助轨迹探索并随训练衰减，椅子高度随辅助力衰减逐步解锁；(ii) 利用逆运动学随机生成覆盖八个椅子高度的初始和目标姿态，增强运动鲁棒性；(iii) 基于生物力学和最优控制研究的奖励函数——塑造离座时的角动量，并通过质心压力吸引函数实现支撑区域转换，确保平滑低能耗驱动。

Result: 在确定性无外力评估器上，策略在八个椅子高度下取得超过97%的平衡站立成功率；运动跨椅子高度平滑泛化，且能从比现有技术更深的坐姿姿态站起。

Conclusion: 该工作证明无需示范或参考轨迹，仅通过精心设计的课程学习、姿态多样性和生物力学启发的奖励塑造，即可端到端学习出自然、鲁棒且可泛化的类人STS运动，为无监督的复杂全身运动控制提供了有效范式。

Abstract: A humanoid has infinitely many ways to stand up from sitting while maintaining balance, making sit-to-stand (STS) a challenging control problem. We synthesise natural humanoid STS motion from scratch using reinforcement learning, without demonstrations or reference trajectories. A single Proximal Policy Optimisation policy learns smooth, human-like rising driven by three complementary components. (i) A coupled force/chair-height curriculum is used. A vertical pelvis-assist force aids early trajectory exploration and decays over training. Taller chairs are unlocked with decaying assisting force. This ensures that the policy masters a viable STS trajectory at each chair height before being exposed to harder ones, avoiding the premature distribution shift that otherwise collapses generalisation. (ii) Motion robustness is achieved by randomly sampling from a large number of inverse kinematics-generated initial and target poses spanning over eight chair heights. (iii) A set of rewards is defined inspired from biomechanics and optimal control studies. They shape the robot's angular momentum for seat-off, and enable support-region transition via centre of pressure attraction function to ensure smooth low-effort actuation. On a deterministic force-free evaluator, the policy attains more than 97% balanced-standing success across eight chair heights. The policy generalises smooth motion across chair heights and enables the robot to rise from substantially deep-seated postures as compared to the state of the art.

</details>


### [163] [Demonstration-Guided Humanoid Stand-Up on an Emulated Deformable Surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20852)
*Aniruddh Kushwah,Vyankatesh Ashtekar,Ashish Dutta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种参考引导的强化学习框架，使29自由度Unitree G1人形机器人在可变形的软地面上完成起身任务，利用硬地面录制的人类演示数据，通过调整MuJoCo的solref/solimp参数实现地形柔度建模，并结合参考跟踪与显式恢复目标训练策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在跌倒后起身是一项关键但具有挑战性的技能，而真实场景中地面并非刚性，软地面上的支撑力延迟生成使得基于硬地面演示的策略难以直接迁移。现有方法缺乏在可变性地面上生成起身动作的有效解决方案。

Method: 该方法包含三要素：(1) 使用MuJoCo的solref和solimp参数建模地形柔度；(2) 奖励函数由参考运动跟踪（残差关节位置控制）和显式恢复目标（骨盆高度、躯干直立度、最终姿态）组成；(3) 两阶段训练：先在硬地上训练，再降低地面刚度并扩大名义表面穿透区域进行后续训练，使策略适应接触密集阶段因显著穿透导致的支撑力延迟，同时保持原始演示模式。

Result: 在仿真中成功完成从倒地到站立的任务，达到目标骨盆高度和直立度，最大接触穿透约40mm。方法在两个起身序列上验证，在硬地和软地上均成功达到最终恢复目标。消融研究表明仅靠参考跟踪不足以实现起身，显式恢复奖励必不可少。

Conclusion: 参考引导结合显式恢复奖励的强化学习框架能够有效使人形机器人在硬地和软地上完成起身任务，为解决可变性地面上的人形机器人运动生成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a reference-guided reinforcement learning framework to generate stand-up motion for a 29-DOF Unitree G1 humanoid on deformable soft ground, using a human demonstration recorded on hard ground. The terrain compliance is modelled using solref and solimp parameters from MuJoCo's rigid body soft-contact model. The rewards consists of (i) reference motion tracking through residual joint-position control and (ii) explicit recovery objectives such as pelvis height, torso uprightness, and the final posture. First, the policy is trained with the specified rewards considering hard ground. Next, the terrain stiffness is lowered by updating solref and the nominal surface penetration zone is expanded using solimp. Subsequent training enables the policy to adapt to the delayed support force generation due to significant surface penetration during contact-intensive phases while preserving the original demonstration pattern. The learned policy successfully completes the fallen-to-standing task in simulation, reaching the targeted pelvis height and uprightness, with a maximum contact penetration of approximately 40 mm during the process. The proposed method is demonstrated on two stand-up sequences and successfully achieves the final recovery objective on both hard and soft ground. Ablation studies show that reference tracking alone is insufficient for successful stand-up, and that explicit recovery rewards are essential.

</details>


### [164] [IMU-Free Body-Frame State Estimation with Sparse Scene Flow for Quadcopters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20891)
*Daniel Grønhaug,Sofie Markeset,Mathias Kolberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅依赖立体视觉（无IMU、无GPS）的X构型四旋翼状态估计系统。系统在机体坐标系中运行，利用同步立体图像和电机推力指令，通过连续-离散扩展卡尔曼滤波器（在复合流形状态上）估计机体位姿、速度、角速度、重力及扰动。特征点检测与跟踪结合4视角全束调整生成稀疏3D点云（含逐点位置、速度与联合协方差），点云密度可空间自适应分配，最终输出机体坐标系下的状态估计、标定位姿变化和稀疏场景流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统四旋翼状态估计高度依赖IMU和GPS，在GPS拒止或IMU失效环境中鲁棒性差。本文旨在构建一个不依赖任何惯性传感器和外部基础设施的纯视觉状态估计系统，利用静止场景点作为隐式惯性参考，为下游机体坐标系世界模型提供测量来源，同时保持架构对未来集成GPS/IMU的可扩展性。

Method: 系统采用连续-离散EKF，状态定义在复合流形上与SE(3)及相关向量空间上，以电机推力作为控制输入驱动滤波器。视觉前端使用FAST/Shi-Tomasi特征检测、SSD/Lucas-Kanade时序跟踪及NCC左右相机匹配，搜索区域由滤波器预测的位姿与点不确定性引导。卡方门控基于标准化新息仅接纳静止特征点。对匹配的四视角（两立体对x两时刻）执行全束调整，联合估计特征点位置与速度，利用立体视差和时间视差求解，以滤波器提供的相对位姿作为先验。点云分配由外部焦点引导，实现空间自适应密度。

Result: 系统能够在不使用IMU和GPS的情况下，输出机体坐标系下的状态估计（位姿、速度、角速度、重力、扰动）、标定姿态变化以及带速度和协方差的稀疏3D点云。EKF特征信息通过位姿先验反映到束调整中，避免了特征点进入求解器的计算负担，同时保证了估计的一致性。点云在关注区域实现致密覆盖，其余区域稀疏覆盖。

Conclusion: 该系统展示了纯视觉四旋翼状态估计的可行性，无需任何惯性传感器或外部基础设施即可维持状态估计并提供场景流信息。架构设计允许多种传感器模态的扩展集成，为GPS拒止环境下的无人机自主导航和世界模型构建提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: We present a vision-only state estimation system for X-configuration quadcopters equipped with a canonical stereo camera pair and no inertial sensors. The system operates entirely in the body frame, requiring only synchronised stereo images and motor thrust commands. A continuous-discrete extended Kalman filter on a composite manifold state $\langle SE(3), \mathbb{R}^3, \ldots \rangle$ maintains estimates of body-frame pose, velocity, angular velocity, gravity, and disturbances, using stationary scene points as implicit inertial references. Feature points are detected (FAST, Shi-Tomasi), tracked temporally (SSD, Lucas-Kanade) and matched across cameras (NCC), with search regions predicted from filter-derived pose and point uncertainty. Chi-squared gating on the normalised innovation admits only stationary points to the filter. The system also produces a sparse 3D point cloud carrying per-point position, velocity and joint covariance. These come from a 4-view (two stereo pairs at two timestamps) full bundle adjustment that jointly estimates position and velocity from stereo disparity and temporal parallax, with the filter-derived relative pose as a prior. Feature points in the EKF do not enter the solver; their information is reflected through the pose prior. Point cloud density is spatially adaptive: an external focus point directs allocation, producing dense coverage in the region of attention and sparse coverage elsewhere. The output is a body-frame state estimate, a calibrated pose change, and a sparse scene flow. It is intended as a measurement source for a downstream world model anchored in the current body frame, without dependence on GPS, IMU, or any world-frame infrastructure, though the architecture accommodates their future integration.

</details>


### [165] [Fast Coordinated Bimanual Motion Planning With Hard Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20946)
*Borna Paro,Luka Petrović,Ivan Marković*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种快速双臂运动规划管线，通过领导者-追随者参数化方式将硬性变换约束（双末端执行器相对位姿固定）在整条路径上连续施加，相比先前方法提速19.4倍，并在仿真和真实Kinova Gen3双臂平台上验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂操作虽然能完成复杂任务，但大量自由度引入了额外复杂度。当操作刚性物体时，两个末端执行器之间的相对变换需在运动全程保持固定，这表现为非线性等式约束，将可行配置空间限制在零测度流形上，给传统运动规划器带来重大挑战。

Method: 采用领导者-追随者参数化：领导者的构型作为自由变量，追随者的构型通过逆运动学求解以满足约束条件。该方法在整个规划路径上连续强制执行硬性变换约束，而非仅约束离散路径点。

Result: 在多种仿真环境、约束条件和双臂平台上进行了广泛评估，规划速度比先前方法快19.4倍，同时保证了约束的连续满足。在双臂Kinova Gen3平台上进行了真实世界实验，包括托盘搬运和长物体操作，验证了规划轨迹可直接迁移到物理硬件。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了通过领导者-追随者参数化可以有效处理双臂刚性物体操作中的硬约束，实现大幅提速的同时确保约束全程连续满足，且规划结果可直接部署到真实机器人上，具备良好的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Bimanual manipulation enables complex tasks but introduces added complexity from the high number of degrees of freedom involved. When handling rigid objects, the relative transformation between the two end effectors must remain fixed throughout the motion, manifesting as a nonlinear equality constraint that confines the feasible configuration space to a measure-zero manifold and challenges conventional motion planners. We propose a fast bimanual motion planning pipeline that enforces this hard transformation constraint continuously along the entire path, using a leader-follower parameterization: the leader's configuration is treated as a free variable, while the follower's is determined via inverse kinematics to satisfy the constraint. We extensively evaluate the method in simulation across diverse environments, constraints and bimanual platforms, achieving 19.4x faster planning than prior work while guaranteeing continuous constraint satisfaction. Real-world experiments on a bimanual Kinova Gen3 setup, involving tray transport and elongated-object manipulation, validate direct transfer of planned trajectories to physical hardware.

</details>


### [166] [Neural-Primitive: An Efficient End-to-end Local Planner with Primitive-based Imitation Learning for Autonomous Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20948)
*Zhitao Liu,Guangtong Xu,Zihan Wang,Jialiang Hou,Chao Xu,Fei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模仿学习的端到端局部规划器，通过轻量级离线原语数据集收集框架生成高质量轨迹原语，并利用紧凑神经网络将传感器输入直接映射为多项式系数，实现低于1ms的超快计算、低内存占用（<1.5MiB）及鲁棒的零样本虚实迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知杂乱环境中，自主飞行的机载在线轨迹生成面临计算复杂度、轨迹质量和内存占用之间的三难困境（computation-quality-memory trilemma），亟需高效且资源友好的局部规划方案。

Method: 设计轻量级基于离线原语的数据集收集框架，在非凸环境中生成安全且高质量的轨迹原语；训练一个紧凑神经网络，直接将传感器输入映射为蕴含高阶动力学信息的多项式系数，实现无后端求解的实时轨迹生成。

Result: 在标准桌面上计算时间低于1ms，机载飞行平均耗时3.68ms，内存占用小于1.5MiB；仿真基准测试表明规划延迟和目标到达进度质量均优于对比方法，真实世界实验零样本部署验证了强大的sim-to-real迁移能力。

Conclusion: 该端到端学习方法有效解决了计算-质量-内存三难问题，实现了超快、轻量且可安全部署的自主飞行局部规划，并具备良好的仿真到现实迁移鲁棒性。

Abstract: Autonomous flight in unknown cluttered environments is hindered by the computation-quality-memory trilemma of onboard trajectory generation. In this paper, we propose an efficient end-to-end local planner via imitation learning. A lightweight offline-primitive-based dataset collection framework is designed to produce safe and high-quality trajectory primitives in non-convex environments. A compact neural network directly maps sensory inputs to polynomial coefficients that inherently encode higher-order dynamical information. The learned policy generates smooth, empirically collision-free and dynamically feasible trajectories in real time without back-end solving. It achieves ultra-fast computation (below 1ms on a standard desktop and average 3.68ms during onboard flight), while maintaining low onboard memory requirements (less than 1.5MiB). Extensive simulation benchmarks demonstrate superiority in both planning latency and target-reaching progress quality. Zero-shot deployment in real-world experiments further validates the robust sim-to-real transfer capability of the proposed method.

</details>


### [167] [Hybrid Roller-Jamming Gripper for Object Acquisition and Retention Under Pose Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20962)
*Yijie Ren,Guillaume Gourmelen,Hiroyasu Iwata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合滚轮-颗粒阻塞（hybrid roller-jamming）夹持器，将主动卷入物体与捕获后锁定功能集成于单一夹持器，解决家庭操作中因姿态不确定性导致的偏心/部分初始接触问题。840次抓取试验中成功812次（平面偏移215/216，姿态偏移597/624），消融实验表明滚轮与颗粒阻塞各自贡献不同，验证了该策略在初始接触不理想时提升抓取可靠性的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭操作中物体姿态不确定常导致夹持器初始接触偏心或不完整，使可靠抓取变得困难。现有方案中，滚轮式夹持器能主动卷入物体但捕获后稳定性有限；颗粒阻塞式夹持器则需要在阻塞前具备充分接触才能实现强保持力。二者各有不足，需要一种既能主动收集物体又能稳定保持的夹持方案。

Method: 设计混合滚轮-颗粒阻塞夹持器：首先通过内向滚轮旋转增加接触并将物体拉向夹持器中心，随后通过真空诱导颗粒阻塞使滚轮硬化以稳定抓取。文中还提供了夹持器的简化几何分析及原型力能力的台架表征。将原型安装于7自由度机械臂上，使用8个测试物体进行评估，施加受控的平面位置偏移和姿态偏移，每个条件重复三次。

Result: 主要评估共840次抓取试验（216次平面偏移、624次姿态偏移），整体成功812/840次：平面偏移215/216，姿态偏移597/624。消融评估162次（3个物体），滚轮单独模式54/81、颗粒阻塞单独模式24/81成功，显示两种机制的不同贡献。

Conclusion: 混合滚轮-阻塞策略在机制层面被证明是提升不完美初始接触后目标获取与保持能力的有效方案，为后续家庭操作中不确定姿态下的可靠抓取提供了新的设计思路。

Abstract: In household manipulation, pose uncertainty often results in off-centre or partial initial contact, making reliable object acquisition difficult. Roller-based grippers can actively draw objects inward but often provide limited post-capture stability, whereas granular-jamming grippers require sufficient contact before jamming to achieve strong retention. This paper presents a hybrid roller-jamming gripper that integrates active object intake and post-capture retention within a single gripper. The proposed gripper uses inward roller rotation to increase contact and draw the object toward the gripper centre, followed by vacuum-induced granular jamming to stiffen the rollers and stabilise the grasp. The paper also presents a simplified geometric analysis of the gripper and a bench-level characterisation of the prototype's force capability. The gripper prototype was mounted on a 7-DoF robotic arm and evaluated using eight test objects. Furthermore, controlled planar position and orientation offsets were applied, with each condition repeated three times. The main evaluation comprised 840 grasp trials, including 216 planar-offset trials and 624 orientation-offset trials. Overall, the gripper succeeded in 812/840 trials: 215/216 planar-offset trials and 597/624 orientation-offset trials. The ablation evaluation comprised 162 trials on three objects. The roller-only and jamming-only conditions achieved 54/81 and 24/81 successes, respectively, showing their different contributions. These results provide initial mechanism-level evidence that hybrid roller-jamming is a promising strategy for improving acquisition and retention after imperfect first contact.

</details>


### [168] [PhysCaP: Grounding Code-as-Policy Agent with Physics-Informed Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21031)
*Chen-Yu Lin,Jing-Wen Chen,Hsueh-En Chang,Hung-An Chen,Sheng-Hsun Chang,Chi-Pin Huang,Fu-En Yang,Min-Hung Chen,Yi-Ting Chen,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang,Shao-Hua Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PhysCaP，一种面向机器人操作主动感知的物理信息代码即策略（Code-as-Policy）智能体，通过物理信息驱动的探索层显式获取物理属性（质量、刚度），采用Planner与Prioritizer双智能体设计平衡探索成本与信息效率，在真实桌面操作任务和LIBERO仿真任务中超越被动与朴素交互基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略虽然擅长模仿演示，但依赖于被动观测，无法推断对操作至关重要的潜在物理属性，导致在物理属性隐藏时任务失败。

Method: PhysCaP在Code-as-Policy框架中引入物理信息探索层，包含：1）训练无关的物理属性提取模块，仅利用机器人本体感觉估计物体质量与刚度，无需额外传感器；2）双智能体设计——Planner决定何时探索、何时停止，Prioritizer过滤不合理交互并按启发式优先级评分排序，实现高效精准的探索。

Result: 在真实桌面操作任务（搜索隐藏物体、检测空罐、寻找成熟牛油果）和LIBERO仿真任务中，PhysCaP以更少的交互次数和更短的执行时间达到与基线相当的性能；现有被动基线和朴素交互基线分别在物理属性隐藏时失败或过度探索。消融实验验证了物理属性提取模块的有效性。

Conclusion: PhysCaP通过物理信息驱动的主动探索，使操作策略能够显式获取潜在物理属性，在保证任务性能的同时显著减少交互成本，为机器人操作中的主动感知提供了有效范式。

Abstract: We present PhysCaP, a Physics-Informed Code-as-Policy agent for active perception in robotic manipulation. While vision-language-action policies excel at imitating demonstrations, they rely on passive observation and fail to infer latent physical properties critical for manipulation. PhysCaP augments code-as-policy frameworks with a physics-informed exploration layer that enables explicit information-seeking through interaction. It introduces training-free physical property extraction modules that estimate object mass and stiffness from robot proprioception without additional sensors. To balance exploration costs and the efficiency of information obtained, PhysCaP employs a dual-agent design: a Planner that decides when to explore and when to stop, and a Prioritizer that filters implausible interactions and ranks the remainder using a heuristic priority score, enabling efficient, targeted exploration. We evaluate PhysCaP on real-world tabletop manipulation tasks (searching for hidden objects, detecting empty cans, and finding ripe avocados) and a simulated task in LIBERO. The results show that existing passive and naive interactive baselines either fail when physical properties are hidden or over-explore, whereas PhysCaP achieves comparable performance with fewer interactions and reduced execution time. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of the proposed physical property extraction modules. Project page: https://physcap.github.io

</details>


### [169] [Roadside-Cooperative Autonomous Driving: From Data Platform to Vision-Language End-to-End Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21032)
*Yitao Xu,Tong Wu,Yiyan Wu,Guoji Xu,Yanbo Jiang,Jiahao Wang,Zehong Ke,Junkai Jiang,Fang Zhang,Jianqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出V2XBench仿真平台与Chat-V2XBench数据集，并在此基础上构建AURORA端到端协同驾驶框架，通过跨视角查询对齐融合模块与LoRA适应的视觉语言模型，在重度遮挡场景下实现SOTA性能（路线完成率98.21%，驾驶得分76.02）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有V2X基准缺乏闭环评估和语言监督支持，阻碍了视觉语言模型在端到端协同驾驶中的发展。

Method: 提出V2XBench仿真平台（支持同步自车-路侧感知与闭环评估）和Chat-V2XBench递进式VQA数据集；在此基础上构建AURORA框架，采用双视角感知架构，通过查询级Cross-View Query Alignment and Fusion（CQAF）模块缓解自车与路侧视角间的空间和语义差异，再利用LoRA适应的VLM桥接语义推理与生成式轨迹规划。

Result: 在V2XBench上的闭环评估中，AURORA在重度遮挡场景下达到SOTA性能，路线完成率98.21%，驾驶得分76.02，且所需路侧通信带宽较低。

Conclusion: 该工作开创了可扩展的V2X-VLM范式，为下一代协同自动驾驶铺平道路。

Abstract: Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) cooperation enables beyond-line-of-sight perception, mitigating occlusions in single-vehicle sensing. However, existing V2X benchmarks provide limited support for closed-loop evaluation and language-grounded supervision, hindering the development of vision-language models (VLMs) for end-to-end cooperative driving. To address these limitations, we introduce V2XBench, a simulation platform featuring synchronized ego--roadside sensing and closed-loop evaluation, together with Chat-V2XBench, a progressively structured VQA dataset for cooperative reasoning. Building upon this benchmark infrastructure, we propose AURORA, an end-to-end cooperative driving framework. Equipped with a dual-view perception architecture, AURORA mitigates spatial and semantic discrepancies across ego and roadside viewpoints through a query-level Cross-View Query Alignment and Fusion (CQAF) module. Leveraging the resulting unified tokens, a LoRA-adapted VLM bridges semantic reasoning and generative trajectory planning. Extensive closed-loop evaluations on V2XBench demonstrate that AURORA achieves state-of-the-art performance in heavily occluded scenarios, with a Route Completion rate of 98.21% and a Driving Score of 76.02, while requiring low roadside communication bandwidth. Ultimately, this work pioneers an extensible V2X--VLM paradigm, paving the way for next-generation cooperative autonomous driving.

</details>


### [170] [Teaching is a Process: The TOSS Framework for Modeling Human Teaching Decisions in Human-Interactive Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21083)
*Bernhard Hilpert,Kim Baraka,Joost Broekens*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过一项N=34的探索性研究，分析了204条人类在机器人强化学习场景中的直觉教学响应，揭示了教学决策由触发因素、目标、信号和策略构成的内在逻辑网络，并提出TOSS框架将人机教学建模为触发-信号响应循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成功的人机教学需要机器人处理需求与人类教学意图之间的对齐，但人类在教授机器人时直觉采用的内在逻辑尚不清楚。理解这一逻辑对于设计更好的机器人学习算法和教学界面至关重要。

Method: 采用自下而上的探索性研究设计，招募34名参与者观察两种不同的机器人强化学习场景，收集204条跨越早期、中期和晚期学习阶段的人类直觉教学响应，并进行归纳分析以识别教学决策中的结构模式。

Result: 教学决策由触发因素（情境催化剂）、目标（主观教学标的）、信号（沟通行为）和策略（高层治理）组成的相互关联网络构成；教师会自发扮演教练、工程师或设计师等不同角色，并优先考虑不同教学目标。基于此提出了TOSS框架。

Conclusion: TOSS框架将人机教学概念化为机器人行为与人类教学动作之间的程序化循环，其中人类教学决策被建模为由教学目标与策略调节的触发-信号响应。该框架提供了开放数据集和理论基础，可用于理解教学决策、模拟真实教学预言机，以及设计超越当前机器人学习局限的人本教学设置和新型学习算法。

Abstract: Successful Human-Robot Teaching assumes alignment between robot processing needs and human teaching intent. To better understand this alignment, this work seeks to uncover the underlying logic that humans intuitively apply when teaching. Through an exploratory, bottom-up study with N=34, participants observing two distinct robot Reinforcement Learning (RL) scenarios, we analyze 204 intuitive teaching responses across early, middle, and late learning phases. Results reveal that teaching decisions consist of a nuanced, interconnected network of Triggers (situational catalysts), Objectives (subjective teaching targets), Signals (communicative acts), and Strategies (high-level governance) in which teachers spontaneously adopt diverse roles, acting as coaches, engineers, or designers and prioritize different objectives. Based on these results, we introduce the TOSS Framework, which conceptualizes Human-Robot teaching as a procedural loop between robot behavior and human teaching actions, in which human teaching decisions are modeled as Trigger-Signal responses modulated by teaching Objectives and Strategies. It provides future research with an openly accessible dataset and a theoretical foundation for a) understanding teaching decisions and b) simulating realistic oracles as well as c) designing human-centered teaching settings and novel robot learning algorithms that go beyond the constraints of current robot learning settings.

</details>


### [171] [SRL-MPC: Shape-Aware Reinforcement Learned Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21175)
*Ruihua Han,Rui Gao,Zhe Liu,Xinyi Wang,Chang Chen,Shuai Wang,Qi Hao,Jia Pan,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SRL-MPC，一种结合高阶控制障碍函数(HOCBF)与强化学习(RL)的形状感知导航方法，用于异构人群与机器人编队中的安全高效导航，无需几何简化即可实时适应周围人群几何形状。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人群导航方法通常假设机器人同质、工作空间稀疏、几何简化、离线计算或手工调参，难以部署在密集人群场景中，缺乏对异构形状的适应性。

Method: 基于支持函数变换的几何分离特征(GSFs)构造高阶控制障碍函数(HOCBF)约束以编码形状感知安全；利用强化学习框架学习读取GSF的神经策略，实时输出MPC参数更新，使MPC求解器适应邻近人群几何；结合MPC的安全结构与泛化性以及RL的适应性与智能性。

Result: 在随机人群场景及任意形状机器人编队实验表明SRL-MPC在有效性、可扩展性和鲁棒性上表现良好，在安全性和适应性上显著优于代表性基线。

Conclusion: SRL-MPC通过融合MPC与RL实现了异构形状人群中的安全、高效且自适应的导航，保留了MPC的安全保证并增强了RL的适应能力，为密集人群导航提供了一种无需几何简化的可行方案。

Abstract: Safe and efficient shape-aware navigation in heterogeneous crowds and robot fleets remains challenging. Traditional approaches often assume homogeneous robots, sparse workspaces, simplified geometry, offline computation, or handcrafted parameters to make the problem tractable, which limits their deployment in dense crowd scenarios. Toward this end, we propose Shape-Aware Reinforcement Learned Model Predictive Control (SRL-MPC), a method for safe, efficient, and adaptive navigation in crowds with heterogeneous shapes without geometry simplification. To encode shape-aware safety, we formulate high-order control barrier function (HOCBF) constraints from geometric separation features (GSFs) based on support function transformation. A reinforcement learning (RL) framework then learns a neural policy that reads GSFs and outputs real-time MPC parameter updates, enabling the MPC solver to adapt to neighboring crowd geometries. The key advantage of SRL-MPC is that it preserves the safety structure and generalizability of MPC while integrating the adaptability and intelligence of RL. Experiments in randomized crowd scenarios with arbitrary shaped robot fleets demonstrate the effectiveness, scalability, and robustness of SRL-MPC. The results show that SRL-MPC substantially outperforms representative baselines in safety and adaptability. Project website: https://hanruihua.github.io/srl_mpc_project/

</details>


### [172] [Beyond Imitation: Self-Improving Robot Policies via Off-Policy Q-Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21204)
*Varun Giridhar,Anant Khandelwal,Jeremy A. Collins,Ignat Georgiev,Animesh Garg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-Planning方法，为大规模视觉运动BC策略配备一个小型离策略Q函数，通过价值引导的动作选择实现推理阶段的单步Q加权平均，并可在保持BC权重不变的情况下仅微调Q函数完成在线自我改进。在LIBERO、RoboTwin及真实双臂接触密集任务上均稳定提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆（BC）虽然在机器人操作中取得了显著进展，但本质上无法自我改进——策略失败后无法从该失败中学习，除非提供额外的人类示范。强化学习微调虽能实现自我改进，但在支撑现代机器人策略的数十亿参数模型上难以扩展。

Method: Q-Planning将大型视觉运动BC策略与一个小型离策略Q函数结合。关键创新是利用Q函数估计价值而非模仿动作的对称性：Q函数可以与BC策略使用相同的成功示范训练，随后还能吸收部署中成功和失败的rollouts。推理时通过BC采样结果的单步Q加权平均进行价值引导动作选择；在线自我改进时仅微调Q函数，BC权重保持冻结。

Result: 在LIBERO上十次自我改进使分数从93%提升至99%，RoboTwin从83.8%提升至91.4%。在近天花板套件（LIBERO-Object、LIBERO-Goal）上缩短成功回合时长。在两个接触密集的双臂真实机器人任务中，五轮迭代内stack-cups从40%提升至90%，insert-wallet从25%提升至80%，而仅用成功rollouts的SFT分别停滞在55%和30%。在相同在线预算下，Q-Planning是唯一一种能够在无需训练辅助actor的情况下从失败中稳定改进的方法。

Conclusion: Q-Planning证明了利用Q函数的非对称性，可以在冻结大规模BC策略权重的前提下实现高效在线自我改进，为大规模机器人策略的自我提升提供了可扩展且稳定的新路径。

Abstract: Behaviour Cloning (BC) has driven remarkable progress in robot manipulation, yet it is fundamentally limited by its inability to self-improve: a policy that fails cannot learn from that failure without additional human demonstrations. Reinforcement Learning fine-tuning offers a path to self-improvement but has proven difficult to scale to the multi-billion-parameter models underpinning modern robot policies. We propose Q-Planning, which equips a large visuomotor BC policy with a small off-policy Q-function. Because a Q-function estimates value rather than imitates actions, it can be trained on the same successful demonstrations as the BC policy and later absorb both successful and failed deployment rollouts, an asymmetry BC does not have. We exploit this asymmetry to enable value-guided action selection at inference (a single-step Q-weighted average over BC draws) and online self-improvement that fine-tunes only the Q-function, leaving the BC weights untouched. On LIBERO and bimanual RoboTwin, ten iterations of self-improvement lift every benchmark score we tested (LIBERO-10 93% to 99%, RoboTwin 83.8% to 91.4%) and shorten successful episodes on the near-ceiling suites (LIBERO-Object, LIBERO-Goal). On two contact-rich bimanual real-robot tasks, the same loop (BC frozen, no human intervention) improves purely from its own deployment rollouts: stack-cups 40% to 90% and insert-wallet 25% to 80% in five iterations, whereas SFT on successful rollouts alone stalls at 55% and 30%. Under an identical online budget Q-Planning is the only method, among Best-of-N, filtered SFT, IBRL, DSRL, and DAWR, that improves stably from failures without training an auxiliary actor.

</details>


### [173] [VT-MUSE: Multimodal Unified Sequential Visuotactile Representation Learning for Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21290)
*Congsheng Xu,Qiaochu Yang,Fangyuan Shi,Yifan Han,Baijun Chen,Yiming Wang,Haonan Zhao,Daolin Ma,Xiaokang Yang,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VT-MUSE，一种用于视觉-触觉操作的多模态统一序列表示学习框架，通过两阶段学习（跨模态时间对齐与掩码视图一致性、条件变分潜模型）捕获细粒度跨模态依赖与接触的时序演化，在仿真基准上超越最强基线11个百分点并在真实实验中取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常在融合前独立编码视觉和触觉观测，限制了捕获细粒度跨模态依赖的能力；且多数方法只关注当前时间步的观测，忽视了接触信息随时间演化的动态过程。

Method: 采用两阶段表示学习框架。Stage I中，模态特定编码器通过跨模态时序对齐和掩码视图一致性联合适配；Stage II中，条件变分潜模型处理掩码视觉序列与完整触觉历史，辅助解码器重建掩码的近期视觉观测并预测触觉深度变化，使潜在表示同时保留全局视觉上下文和局部接触动力学。学习到的表示通过门控交叉注意力整合进轻量级Transformer策略。

Result: 在仿真基准上，VT-MUSE在所有任务上超越最强基线11个百分点；在真实世界实验中同样取得了显著改进。

Conclusion: 通过统一的序列级多模态表示学习，同时捕获跨模态依赖与触觉时序演化，VT-MUSE有效提升了视觉-触觉操作策略的性能，验证了全局视觉上下文与局部接触动力学联合建模的有效性。

Abstract: We propose VT-MUSE, a Multimodal Unified SEquential representation learning framework for visuotactilemanipulation. Existing approaches often encode visual and tactile observations independently before fusion, limiting their ability to capture fine-grained cross-modal dependencies. Moreover, most methods focus on observations at the current time step and overlook the temporal evolution of contact. VT-MUSE addresses both limitations through a two-stage representation learning framework. In Stage I, modality specific encoders are jointly adapted via cross-modal temporal alignment and masked-view consistency. In Stage II, a conditional variational latent model processes masked visual sequences together with full tactile histories. Auxiliary decoders reconstruct the masked recent visual observations and predict tactile depth changes, encouraging the latent representation to retain both global visual context and local contact dynamics.
