The learned representation is subsequently integrated into a lightweight Transformer policy through gated cross-attention. On the simulation benchmark, VT-MUSE outperforms the strongest baseline evaluated on all tasks by 11 percentage points and also achieves substantial improvements in real-world experiments.

</details>


### [174] [NeSAM: Neuro-Symbolic Kinodynamics with Soil Adaptation for Off-Road Mobility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21330)
*Chenhui Pan,Tong Xu,Francesco Cancelliere,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出NeSAM，一个神经符号框架，结合可微Bekker-Wong地面力学模型与Transformer残差动力学模型，用于越野车辆六自由度长时间运动学预测，在仿真和真实平台上分别提升预测精度达30%和29%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野车辆在可变形地面上运动预测具有挑战性，因为下沉量、滑移率和牵引力随局部土壤条件变化。现有基于学习的运动学模型直接从数据近似车辆-地形交互，但未显式建模土壤力学，物理可解释性有限。

Method: 提出NeSAM框架，包含三个核心组件：(1)可微Bekker-Wong地面力学模型，显式建模与土壤相关的交互力；(2)Transformer残差动力学模型，修正解析预测与观测车辆动力学之间的偏差；(3)从地形观测估计物理意义的土壤参数，并通过扩展卡尔曼滤波在线更新。评估使用基于Chrono多物理引擎的Verti-Bench仿真器和物理Verti-4-Wheeler平台。

Result: NeSAM在仿真中提升预测精度最高达30%，在真实数据上提升29%（相对最强基线）。与闭环导航控制器集成时，通过在线土壤自适应使穿越成功率提升，并将到参考轨迹的Hausdorff距离降低69.4%，表明轨迹跟踪精度显著改善。

Conclusion: NeSAM通过结合物理原理（Bekker-Wong地面力学）与数据驱动方法（Transformer残差模型），在保持物理可解释性的同时显著提升了越野车辆运动预测精度和轨迹跟踪性能，验证了神经符号方法在越野机器人运动学建模中的有效性。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of off-road vehicle motion over deformable terrain remains challenging because sinkage, slip, and traction vary with local soil conditions. Existing learning-based kinodynamic models directly approximate vehicle-terrain interactions from data but do not explicitly represent soil mechanics and offer limited physical interpretability. To address these limitations, we present NeSAM, a neuro-symbolic framework that combines differentiable Bekker-Wong terramechanics with learned terrain representations and a Transformer-based residual dynamics model for long-horizon, six degree-of-freedom kinodynamic prediction. The terramechanics component models soil-dependent interaction forces, while the residual model corrects discrepancies between the analytical prediction and the observed vehicle dynamics. NeSAM further estimates physically meaningful soil parameters from terrain observations and updates them online using an extended Kalman filter. We evaluate NeSAM in Verti-Bench, a simulator built on the Chrono multiphysics engine, and validate its performance on a physical Verti-4-Wheeler platform. NeSAM improves prediction accuracy by up to 30% in simulation and 29% on real-world data relative to the strongest compared baselines. When integrated with a close-loop navigation controller, NeSAM further improves traversal success rate through online soil adaptation while reduces Hausdorff distance to the reference trajectory by 69.4%, indicating improved trajectory tracking accuracy.

</details>


### [175] [ViTacPhys: Physical Property-Aware Grasping from Human Visual-Tactile Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21355)
*Yiwen Liu,Yujun Zhu,Kui Jia,Zhao Liao,Yangwei You,Shuaijun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ViTacPhys是一个基于视觉-触觉融合的框架，通过人类操作演示估计物体的质量、摩擦系数类别和连续刚度，并将这些物理属性作为条件输入用于自适应抓取策略，在分布内和分布外物体上分别达到95.0%和83.4%的抓取成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于视觉的动作模型虽然具备强大的复杂操作能力，但很少利用显式的物体物理属性来调整策略。物体的质量、摩擦系数和刚度等物理属性对抓取力控制至关重要，缺乏这些信息会导致策略在接触不同物体时表现不稳定。

Method: ViTacPhys包含一个视觉-触觉数据采集系统和估计框架：(1)从人类操作演示中估计物体质量类别、摩擦系数类别和连续刚度；(2)结合时间视觉-触觉建模、跨注意力多模态融合和来自视觉语言模型的语义先验；(3)通过有限的机器人遥操作数据、机器人风格视频增强和匹配动作的人类演示实现从人类域到机器人域的迁移；(4)作为在线模块部署到自适应抓取策略中，输出物理属性条件用于策略控制。

Result: 在已见物体上，质量分类准确率97.2%，摩擦系数分类准确率98.8%，刚度MAPE为5.51%；在已知类别的未见物体上，质量准确率87.5%，摩擦系数准确率97.5%，刚度MAPE为9.08%。物理属性条件化策略在分布内物体上总抓取成功率为95.0%，分布外物体上为83.4%。相比ACT方法，该策略在成功抓取的分布外物体上产生的力曲线与人类遥操作更一致。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，显式估计物体物理属性并将其作为条件输入真实世界的自适应抓取策略是可行的，能够提升抓取成功率并产生更接近人类操作行为的力控制模式。

Abstract: Recent vision-based action models have demonstrated strong capabilities in complex manipulation, but they rarely leverage explicit object physical properties to adapt their policies. We introduce ViTacPhys, a visual-tactile framework and data acquisition system that estimates object mass and friction-coefficient classes, together with continuous stiffness, from human manipulation demonstrations. Trained on data from 60 rigid and deformable objects, ViTacPhys combines temporal visual-tactile modeling, cross-attention multimodal fusion, and a semantic prior derived from a vision-language model. On seen objects, it achieves 97.2% mass classification accuracy, 98.8% friction-coefficient classification accuracy, and a stiffness mean absolute percentage error (MAPE) of 5.51%. On held-out objects from known categories, it achieves 87.5% mass accuracy, 97.5% friction-coefficient accuracy, and a stiffness MAPE of 9.08%. We transfer ViTacPhys from the human domain to the robot domain using limited robot teleoperation data, robot-style video augmentation, and human demonstrations with matched actions, and deploy it as an online module for adaptive grasping. The resulting physical-property-conditioned policy achieves total grasping success rates of 95.0% on in-distribution objects and 83.4% on out-of-distribution objects. For out-of-distribution objects successfully grasped by both methods, its force profiles are more consistent with human teleoperation than those produced by ACT. These results demonstrate the feasibility of explicitly estimating and conditioning on object physical properties for real-world adaptive grasping.

</details>


### [176] [Mining beyond Earth with Space Robots: Exploration, Sampling, and Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21358)
*Dong Li,Dujun Nie,Xiaotong Zhang,Ruilin Wang,Yuchen Li,Chang Ge,Chao Xiong,Kaichang Di,Andreas Nüchter,Levente Kovács,Qingquan Li,Shirong Ge,Fei-Yue Wang,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对空间采矿机器人及相关技术进行了全面综述，提出了一个六阶段系统架构（远程探测、原位机器人检测、单机器人小规模采样、多机器人大规模挖掘、自主资源提取、集成到原位建造或地球运输），并整理了现有研究资源，识别了自主空间采矿中的关键开放挑战，规划了战略研究路线图以推动可持续地外经济发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间资源的获取与利用（即空间采矿）是实现持续人类探索和开拓太空商业机会的关键路径。由于太空环境恶劣、通信延迟大、发射成本高，开发自主机器人系统对于实现高效、经济的空间采矿至关重要，因此需要系统性地梳理该领域的现状、技术架构与未来方向。

Method: 本文采用文献综述方法，首先回顾空间采矿背景（包括国际政策、商业实体和最新进展）；然后定义六阶段系统架构，从勘探（远程遥感、原位机器人检测）、采样（单机器人小规模采样、多机器人大规模挖掘）到提取（自主资源提取、集成到原位建造或地球运输）；同时梳理并整理了空间采矿研究可用资源，包括真实任务数据、地面模拟数据集和高保真仿真环境。

Result: 提出了一套系统的六阶段空间采矿机器人架构，整理了已有的研究资源（真实任务数据、类比数据集、仿真环境），识别了自主空间采矿中未解决的关键开放挑战，并给出了一项填补当前技术差距的战略研究路线图。还维护了一个供社区持续追踪发展动态的项目页面。

Conclusion: 自主机器人技术是空间采矿成功的核心保障。通过系统化架构、整合可复用资源并明确开放挑战，本文为从当前技术现状过渡到可持续的地外经济提供了清晰的研究路线图。

Abstract: Space resource acquisition and utilization, commonly referred to as Space Mining, represent critical pathways for enabling sustained human exploration and unlocking commercial opportunities in space. These resources mainly include helium-3, water, mineral resources on the Moon and Mars, and abundant mineral deposits on asteroids. Due to the harsh conditions of space, communication delays, and high launch costs, the development of autonomous robotic systems is critical to achieving efficient, cost-effective space mining. This paper provides a comprehensive overview of space mining robotics and associated technologies. First, we review the background of space mining, including international policies, commercial entities, and recent advancements. We define a systematic six-stage architecture for space mining: Exploration is initiated by (1) remote sensing for target identification and (2) precise in situ robotic detection; Sampling progresses from (3) single-robot small-scale sampling to (4) multi-robot large-scale excavation; and Extraction integrates (5) autonomous resource extraction and (6) final integration into in situ construction or terrestrial transport. Additionally, we review and curate existing resources for space mining research, including real-world mission data, terrestrial analog datasets, and high-fidelity simulation environments. Finally, we identify critical open challenges in autonomous space mining and delineate a strategic research roadmap to bridge current technological gaps, fostering the transition toward a sustainable off-world economy. To track ongoing developments in space mining, we maintain an updated project page: https://github.com/OpenSpace-Lab/Space-Mining-with-Robotics-List.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [Scalpel3: A High-Performance Data Carving Architecture for Recovery of Fragmented Files](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20363)
*Karley Waguespack,Samuel Goodwin,George Hendrick,Samuel Hildebrand,Thomas Landaiche,Mingyang Li,Joshua McCain,Tyler Saizan,Jacob Tucker,James M. Ghawaly,Golden G. Richard*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Scalpel3是一个大规模多线程的文件雕刻框架，支持连续与碎片化文件恢复，研究者只需编写单线程的验证和重组代码即可部署新的恢复策略，并集成了ONNX Runtime以支持学习模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具仅能有效恢复连续文件，缺乏公开可用的、格式无关的、高性能的碎片化恢复研究框架，研究者难以开发和部署新的碎片化恢复策略。

Method: 采用大规模多线程架构，将工作者调度、同步、检查点及I/O等底层基础设施与研究者编写的单线程验证/重组代码解耦；集成ONNX Runtime支持学习模型；提供人工介入控制、块去重、持久化重启检查点、增量输出和FUSE文件系统等操作特性。

Result: 在超过80,000个文件的混合语料上评估，涵盖连续恢复和三种受控碎片化场景（间隙、乱序块放置、两者结合）。结果显示连续恢复快速准确，多线程架构使碎片化验证结果显著早于单线程执行，且针对特定文件类型定制的策略在日益困难的布局下保持高精度。

Conclusion: Scalpel3为开发和部署大规模碎片化恢复策略提供了实用的基础框架。

Abstract: File carving recovers files from raw storage without filesystem metadata, a key capability in digital forensics, data recovery, and digital exploration. Existing tools recover contiguous files effectively, but, to our knowledge, no publicly available, format-agnostic, high-performance framework exists in which researchers can develop and deploy new fragmented recovery strategies. Scalpel3 fills this gap with a massively threaded architecture for contiguous and fragmented recovery. Researchers need only write single-threaded validation and reassembly code for a new file type; Scalpel3 supplies worker scheduling, synchronization, checkpointing, and I/O. This separation allows new recovery methods to be added without modifying the backend infrastructure. The architecture also integrates the ONNX Runtime, allowing learned models to be used within validators and recovery strategies. Operational features include interactive human-in-the-loop control, block deduplication, persistent restart checkpoints, incremental output, and a FUSE filesystem for hybrid workflows.
  We evaluate Scalpel3 on a mixed corpus of more than 80,000 files under contiguous recovery and three controlled fragmentation scenarios: gaps, out-of-order block placement, and both together. Results show fast and accurate contiguous recovery and demonstrate that Scalpel3's massively threaded architecture makes validated fragmented results available substantially earlier than single-threaded execution. Furthermore, strategies tailored to individual file types maintain high overall accuracy across increasingly difficult layouts. Together, these results demonstrate that Scalpel3 provides a practical foundation for developing and deploying fragmented recovery strategies at scale.

</details>


### [178] [Benchmarking LLM Serving Systems for Agentic AI Workloads with XPerf](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20370)
*Michael Wang,Yikang Yue,Shaobo Li,Yirui Eric Zhou,Chen Wang,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: XPerf是一个用于对LLM服务系统进行代理式AI工作负载压力测试的基准测试框架，通过细粒度trace重放技术最小化工作负载变化，提供详细的系统和硬件性能剖析，帮助用户识别代理式工作负载引入的性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理式AI应用依赖非确定性的LLM输出驱动控制流，导致工作负载模式在每次运行时不可预测地变化，这使得对LLM服务系统进行基准测试变得困难。现有基准测试框架难以在代理式工作负载下提供可复现、准确的性能评估。

Method: XPerf采用细粒度trace重放方法：从真实代理应用中收集traces，根据需求合成新工作负载模式，并在不同LLM服务系统上可复现地重放。框架默认包含8个覆盖不同用例（编码、深度研究、问答等）的代理应用，同时提供详细的服务系统与硬件剖析能力。

Result: 实验研究表明XPerf能够准确重放代理工作负载，提供详细的性能分解，可扩展到更大的服务系统，并有助于服务系统的调试。

Conclusion: XPerf有效解决了代理式AI工作负载基准测试中的可复现性问题，为LLM服务系统的性能评估和瓶颈诊断提供了实用工具，项目将开源。

Abstract: We present XPerf, a benchmarking framework that load-tests LLM serving systems with diverse agentic AI workloads. It provides detailed profiling of the serving system and hardware, enabling users to identify performance bottlenecks introduced by agentic workloads. Benchmarking LLM serving systems under agentic workloads is challenging - agentic applications rely on nondeterministic LLM outputs to guide their control flow; therefore, workload patterns vary unpredictably from run to run. XPerf minimizes this workload variation with a fine-grained trace replay approach: it enables users to easily collect traces from real agentic applications, synthesize new workloads with various patterns if needed, and reproducibly replay them on different LLM serving systems. XPerf includes eight agentic applications across diverse use cases (e.g., coding, deep research, and Q&A) by default. Our empirical study using these workloads shows that XPerf accurately replays agentic workloads, provides detailed performance breakdowns, scales to larger serving systems, and assists in serving system debugging. We will open-source XPerf on GitHub.

</details>


### [179] [Enabling Memory-efficient Im2win Convolution with Multi-precision Support on GPU CUDA and Tensor Cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20725)
*Xiang Fu,Jixiang Ma,Xinpeng Zhang,Peng Zhao,Shuai Lu,Xu Tony Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文扩展了im2win卷积范式，使其在CUDA核心上以全精度运行、在张量核心上以半精度运行，通过锯齿形内存访问和异步数据移动等优化，在十二个CNN基准上分别比CUDA核心实现、cuDNN和基于GEMM的卷积快2.8倍、1.4倍和6.4倍，且内存使用分别仅为后两者的53%和35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卷积是深度神经网络中的主要计算瓶颈，其效率取决于算法与GPU硬件之间的紧密集成。现有的GPU卷积方法存在内存开销大、缓存利用率差、跨卷积核尺寸有效性有限或数值不稳定等问题，亟需一种统一且高效的方法。

Method: 在既有im2win范式的基础上，针对CUDA核心和张量核心分别设计了全精度与半精度的高效卷积实现，引入了新的核设计及优化技术，包括锯齿形（zig-zag）内存访问模式和异步数据移动，以充分利用硬件加速的半精度矩阵乘累加运算。

Result: 在十二个CNN基准上，im2win相比其CUDA核心实现TFLOPS提升最高达2.8倍，相比cuDNN提升1.4倍，相比基于cuBLAS的GEMM卷积提升6.4倍；内存使用量分别低至cuDNN的53%和cuBLAS GEMM的35%。

Conclusion: im2win被验证为一种面向现代GPU架构的统一、高性能卷积框架，兼顾了计算速度与内存效率，能够有效适应不同卷积核尺寸的需求。

Abstract: Convolution is a principal computational bottleneck in deep neural networks, and its efficiency depends on tight integration between algorithms and GPU hardware. Existing GPU convolution methods suffer from large memory overhead, poor cache utilization, limited effectiveness across kernel sizes, or numerical instability. This work extends the im2win paradigm -- a universal, memory-efficient convolution method with contiguous memory access for all kernel sizes -- to run efficiently in full precision on CUDA cores and half precision on tensor cores. By introducing new kernel designs and optimizations such as zig-zag memory access and asynchronous data movement, im2win efficiently exploits hardware-accelerated half-precision matrix multiply-accumulate operations. Across twelve CNN benchmarks, im2win achieves up to 2.8x higher TFLOPS than its CUDA core implementation, 1.4x higher than cuDNN, and 6.4x higher than GEMM-based convolution with cuBLAS, while using as little as 53% and 35% of their memory, respectively. These results establish im2win as a unified, high-performance convolution framework for modern GPU architectures.

</details>


### [180] [PRICE: Pricing-based Resource Incentives for Quality-of-Result-aware Computing at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20819)
*Uwe Gropengießer,Sebastian Frenz,Max Mühlhäuser*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PRICE是一种面向边缘节点的激励机制，将依赖利用率的动态价格信号与逐请求质量选择相结合，在过载时促使请求向轻量级执行转移，从而在维持接近容量运行的同时显著提升接受吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘节点在容量上受到设计约束，但许多边缘工作负载可以在运行时以结果质量换取资源效率。现有边缘定价机制大多将请求视为固定配置提交，很少利用过载状态下的逐请求质量灵活性，导致资源利用和吞吐受限。

Method: PRICE提出一个与利用率耦合的价格信号机制：当节点利用率上升时，接受价格随之上升，使得资源密集型变体被选择的可能性降低，从而将接受请求向更轻量级的执行方式转移。该机制在真实硬件上通过持续过载实验进行评估，并与固定分配和动态定价基线进行对比。

Result: 在真实硬件持续过载条件下，PRICE在接受的吞吐量和CPU利用率方面均优于固定分配和动态定价基线。实验结果在不同定价函数族、任务持续时间分布和客户端群体下均表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 结果质量灵活性是边缘过载管理中被低估但强有力的控制维度，而定价是挖掘这一维度的有效机制。

Abstract: Edge nodes are capacity-constrained by design, yet many edge workloads can trade result quality for resource efficiency at runtime. Existing edge pricing mechanisms largely treat requests as fixed-configuration submissions and rarely exploit per-request quality flexibility under overload. We present PRICE, an incentive mechanism that couples a utilization-dependent price signal to per-request quality selection. As utilization increases, rising acceptance prices make resource-intensive variants less likely to be selected, shifting accepted requests toward lighter execution and allowing the node to serve significantly more requests while operating near capacity. Evaluation on real hardware under sustained overload shows that PRICE outperforms both fixed-allocation and dynamic-pricing baselines in accepted throughput and CPU utilization. The results are robust across pricing function families, task-duration distributions, and client populations. Result-quality flexibility is a powerful but underused control dimension for overload management at the edge, and pricing is an effective mechanism to exploit it.

</details>


### [181] [AI Infrastructure in Space: How Far Can We Go?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21034)
*Qing Li,Qiyang Zhang,Daliang Xu,Tianze Huang,Dingge Zhang,Yihao Zhao,Xiaolong Huang,Jinfeng Wen,Xiameng Hu,Tao Qi,Mengwei Xu,Shangguang Wang,Xuanzhe Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了太空AI基础设施的系统愿景，将轨道和物理状态纳入资源模型，管理跨航天器、轨道网络、地面站和云端的AI能力，并通过三颗在轨卫星（BUPT-1/BUPT-2）的遥测数据验证了热约束对计算资源和AI服务生命周期的关键影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星正成为可编程计算平台，可运行日益繁重的AI负载，但轨道时间上的计算容量、连接、能量和热余量动态变化，使AI服务在发射后的可部署性、可管理性和可恢复性成为关键系统问题。

Method: 综合了地面AI基础设施、卫星网络和卫星边缘计算的相关基础，审视直接塑造系统设计的物理约束，并基于三个在轨案例研究（节点级、平台级和服务级）进行实证：BUPT-1卫星遥测分析计算容量受热/能耗包络约束；BUPT-2卫星上的SateLight系统优化应用更新传输；有状态VLM服务案例研究热中断下的执行状态恢复。

Result: BUPT-1卫星遥测显示可用计算能力受热和能量包络约束；BUPT-2卫星上的SateLight将应用更新传输延迟平均降低56.54%，最高达91.18%，更新正确率100%；热中断使执行状态恢复成为有状态VLM服务的一等系统问题。

Conclusion: 这些观测催生了针对空间原生资源管理、生命周期支持和跨天地持续AI服务的研究议程，为未来太空AI基础设施系统设计奠定了基础。

Abstract: Satellites are becoming programmable computing platforms capable of running increasingly demanding AI workloads. This shift raises a systems problem: how can AI services remain deployable, manageable, and recoverable after launch when compute capacity, connectivity, energy, and thermal headroom vary over orbital time? This paper develops a systems vision for AI infrastructure in space. We define it as the systems layer that manages AI capabilities across spacecraft, orbital networks, ground stations, and cloud backends, while treating orbital and physical state as part of the resource model. We synthesize relevant foundations from terrestrial AI infrastructure, satellite networking, and satellite edge computing, and examine the physical constraints that directly shape system design. We further ground this vision in three in-orbit case studies spanning the node, platform, and service levels. Telemetry from BUPT-1 satellite shows that usable compute capacity is bounded by thermal and energy envelopes. SateLight on BUPT-2 satellite reduces application-update transmission latency by 56.54% on average and up to 91.18%, with 100% update correctness. A stateful VLM serving case further shows that thermal interruptions make execution-state recovery a first-class systems problem. These observations motivate a research agenda for space-native resource management, lifecycle support, and sustained AI service across space and ground.

</details>


### [182] [Integrating a Python Dynamical core into ICON](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21150)
*Mauro Bianco,Till Ehrengruber,Enrique González Paredes,Andreas Jocksch,Christos Kotsalos,Ioannis Magkanaris,Philip Müller,Edoardo Paone,Mikael Simberg,Hannes Vogt,Jacopo Canton,Yilu Chen,Anurag Dipankar,Nicoletta Farabullini,Michael Jähn,Matthieu Leclair,Ong Chia Rui,Nathan Beech,Nicolas Gruber,Christoph Müller,Daniel Hupp,Xavier Lapillonne*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出将基于Python的ICON动力核心集成到传统Fortran仿真代码中，利用GT4Py DSL和DaCe优化框架，证明高层Python可以无缝集成到遗留基础设施中且无性能损失，在全局生产级模拟中实现了20-30%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球系统模型向exascale过渡常受限于僵化的单体Fortran代码库和维护成本高昂的编译器指令。虽然高层DSL被提出作为解决方案，但由于集成繁琐而经常失败，因此需要一种既能无缝集成到遗留代码中又不损失性能的新方法。

Method: 将基于Python的ICON动力核心集成到原始Fortran模拟代码中，采用GT4Py DSL和Data-Centric (DaCe)优化框架；通过先进的数据流优化和自动内核融合，从单一可移植的Python源码生成优化的设备端代码，替代与硬件纠缠的指令。

Result: 在全局生产级模拟中，Python动力核心较高度优化的Fortran+OpenACC实现实现了20-30%的性能提升，耦合设置总时间提升10%，挑战了Python编排会引入过高HPC开销的假设。

Conclusion: Python可以提供一种可持续、高效且硬件无关的全球气候建模未来，证明了Python在HPC领域的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: The transition of Earth-system models to exascale is often hindered by rigid, monolithic Fortran codebases and maintenance-heavy compiler directives. While high-level DSLs offer a solution, they frequently fail due to cumbersome integration. We present the integration of a Python-based ICON dynamical core into the original Fortran simulation code. Leveraging the GT4Py DSL and the Data-Centric (DaCe) optimization framework, we demonstrate that high-level Python can be seamlessly integrated into legacy infrastructure without performance loss. Our results challenge the assumption that Python orchestration introduces prohibitive HPC overhead. In production-grade global simulations, our Python dynamical core achieves a 20--30\% performance improvement over the highly-optimized Fortran+OpenACC implementation, with a 10\% improvement on the total time for a coupled setup. Driven by advanced data-flow optimizations and automated kernel fusion, this approach replaces hardware-entangled directives by generating optimized device code from a single, portable Python source. This work proves that Python can provide a sustainable, efficient, and hardware-agnostic future for global climate modeling.

</details>


### [183] [HIERA: Workload-Aware Planning Across Implementation Spaces for GPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21157)
*Jinghao Wang,Qiqi Gu,Chenpeng Wu,Jianguo Yao,Haibing Guan,Xijun Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HIERA是一个用于GPU内核优化的分层搜索空间规划框架，通过构建契约增强的任务规范、在不同实现空间（PyTorch算子、CUDA库、自定义CUDA内核）中选择合适的空间，并利用性能剖析反馈和专家知识进行结构化迭代优化。在KernelBench基准上优于现有无训练方法，与需训练的CUDA-L1方法性能相当，并在科学计算stencil算子上实现比cuDNN快1.53倍的速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的GPU内核优化方法通常在固定的实现空间内进行优化，导致优化灵活性或搜索效率受限。随着工作负载多样化和GPU硬件快速演进，需要更高效的自动化内核生成与优化方法。

Method: HIERA框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 构建契约增强（contract-augmented）的任务规范；(2) 在PyTorch算子、CUDA库和自定义CUDA内核之间选择适当的实现空间；(3) 利用性能剖析反馈和专家知识引导结构化迭代细化。

Result: 在KernelBench基准的多个工作负载层级和多种基础LLM上，HIERA在实现有效性、样本效率和优化性能方面均优于现有无训练方法，并与需额外模型训练的CUDA-L1方法具有竞争力。在科学计算的专用stencil算子上实现了相对cuDNN的1.53倍加速。

Conclusion: HIERA展示了分层搜索空间规划对GPU内核优化的有效性，不仅适用于机器学习负载，也能泛化到科学计算等领域，是一种无需额外模型训练的通用高性能内核优化框架。

Abstract: High-performance GPU kernels underpin modern deep learning and scientific computing. As workloads become increasingly diverse and GPU hardware evolves rapidly, developing efficient methods for automated GPU kernel generation and optimization has become increasingly important. Existing LLM-based methods typically optimize within a fixed implementation space, limiting either optimization flexibility or search efficiency. We propose \textsc{HIERA}, a hierarchical search-space planning framework for GPU kernel optimization. \textsc{HIERA} constructs contract-augmented task specifications, selects an appropriate implementation space across PyTorch operators, CUDA libraries, and custom CUDA kernels, and uses profiling feedback and expert knowledge to guide structured iterative refinement. Experiments on KernelBench across multiple various workload levels and base LLMs show that \textsc{HIERA} delivers stronger overall implementation validity, sample efficiency, and optimization performance than existing training-free methods, while remaining competitive with the training-based CUDA-L1 without additional model training. A case study on a specialized stencil operator from scientific computing further achieves a \(1.53\times\) speedup over cuDNN, demonstrating the potentiality of the general framework beyond standard machine-learning workloads.

</details>


### [184] [GrAND: GPU-based Dynamic Graph Indexes for Approximate Nearest Neighbour Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21163)
*Karthik Venkatasubba,Shivendra Deshpande,Shivram S,Jyothi Vedurada*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出GrAND，一个GPU原生的动态图索引更新算法集合，支持在Vamana和CAGRA两种流行图索引上进行高效的插入和删除操作。GrAND通过批量合并图修复、无锁find-and-replace策略以及按需反向图构建，在七个真实数据集和五个流式工作负载上将整体吞吐量提升2.2x-8.7x（对比SVFusion）和6.5x-25.4x（对比FreshDiskANN-GPU），同时保持高搜索吞吐量和召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代ANNS应用需要处理不断演化的向量集合，要求图索引在高召回率下支持高频插入和删除。然而大多数GPU图索引是静态的或只提供有限的更新支持，更新操作中的邻居发现、反向边创建、剪枝和删除修复会引入大量冗余距离计算和共享邻接表的冲突访问，当前方案难以同时保证更新效率与查询性能。

Method: GrAND采用三种关键技术：(1) 跨批次合并图修复，消除冗余的剪枝计算；(2) 无锁find-and-replace策略实现并行的邻接表更新；(3) 在GPU上按需构建反向图，准确识别入边而无需永久复制索引，从而支持可靠的原地删除。该方法应用于Vamana和CAGRA两种图索引。

Result: 在七个真实数据集、五种流式工作负载的评估中，GrAND相比SVFusion实现了2.2x-8.7x的整体工作负载吞吐量提升，相比FreshDiskANN-GPU实现6.5x-25.4x的提升，同时在持续更新下保持高搜索吞吐量和高召回率。

Conclusion: GrAND通过GPU原生设计的批量图修复、无锁更新和按需反向图策略，有效解决了动态图索引在GPU上面临的更新性能瓶颈，为连续演化的向量集合提供了高吞吐、高召回率的高效索引更新方案。

Abstract: Modern Approximate Nearest Neighbour Search (ANNS) applications operate over continuously evolving vector collections and require graph indexes that sustain high-throughput searches while incorporating insertions and deletions with high recall. However, most GPU graph indexes are static or provide limited update support. Updates require neighbour discovery, reverse-edge creation, pruning, and deletion-induced graph repair; executing these operations concurrently introduces redundant distance computations and conflicting accesses to shared adjacency lists. Background-rebuild-based deletion further incurs substantial computation, additional memory consumption, and interference with foreground queries.
  We present GrAND (GPU-based Dynamic Graph Indexes for Approximate Nearest Neighbour Search), a GPU-native collection of dynamic-update algorithms for two popular graph indexes, Vamana and CAGRA. GrAND consolidates graph repair across a batch, eliminating redundant pruning computations, and employs a lock-free find-and-replace strategy for parallel adjacency-list updates. For reliable in-place deletion, GrAND constructs an on-demand reverse graph on the GPU, accurately identifying incoming edges without permanently duplicating the index. We evaluate GrAND on seven real-world datasets across five streaming workloads, comparing it against SVFusion and FreshDiskANN-GPU (our GPU adaptation of FreshDiskANN). GrAND improves overall workload throughput by 2.2x-8.7x and 6.5x-25.4x, respectively, while maintaining high search throughput and recall over sustained updates.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [SDAD: Spec-Driven Agentic Development for the AI-Native SDLC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20341)
*Vu Hung Nguyen,Thanh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文正式提出"规格驱动的智能体开发"（SDAD）范式，将严谨的前端形式化与高速实现相结合，涵盖意图捕获、机器可读规格、智能体综合和独立多智能体验证。论文论证智能体速度并未消除工程纪律，而是将纪律上移至规格精度、显式门控和可审计溯源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型驱动的编码智能体拥有数十万至百万级token的上下文窗口，正在重构软件开发生命周期（SDLC），使得大规模功能性需求文档（FRD）和仓库上下文可在单一流程中被处理，从而需要重新审视开发范式和工程纪律的位置。

Method: 论文采用综合分析与范式构建方法：(1) 形式化定义SDAD框架，包括四个核心阶段（意图捕获、机器可读规格、智能体合成、独立多智能体验证与人工签核）；(2) 回顾瀑布与敏捷之间的历史钟摆，提出AI代码作为第四种生产范式；(3) 对比Human-Agile（2020年）与Agentic-SDAD（2026年）在制品、节奏、问责和安全态势上的差异；(4) 扩展到团队角色蜕变、量化治理指标（Ambiguity Tax、Spec Fidelity、SER、TCI_agentic含修复乘数phi）以及分阶段迁移蓝图。

Result: 论文提出了一套完整的SDAD理论框架和实践指南，包含团队角色转化模型、量化治理指标体系、混合估算方法和分阶段采用蓝图，并整合了AI增强测试与验证的工业和研究证据以支持合成权与发布权的分离。

Conclusion: 核心结论是：智能体的速度并不消除工程纪律，而是将纪律重新定位到上游——即规格精度、显式门控和可审计溯源。合成权与发布权应分离，工程纪律在智能体时代变得更加关键而非过时。

Abstract: Frontier coding agents backed by large language models with context windows from hundreds of thousands to millions of tokens are restructuring the Software Development Life Cycle (SDLC). Rich context handling and multi-step reasoning now allow substantial Functional Requirement Documents (FRDs) and repository context to be ingested in a single workflow, making specification quality the execution fuel for autonomous delivery. This report formalises Spec-Driven Agentic Development (SDAD) as a synthesis of disciplined up-front formalisation and high-velocity implementation: intent capture, machine-readable specification, agentic synthesis, and independent multi-agent verification under human sign-off. We revisit the historical pendulum between Waterfall and Agile, introduce AI-code as a fourth production paradigm, and compare Human-Agile (circa 2020) with Agentic-SDAD (circa 2026) across artefacts, cadence, accountability, and security posture. Beyond process description, we extend the model to team role metamorphosis (engineer, QA, platform, and product functions), quantitative governance (Ambiguity Tax, Spec Fidelity, SER, and TCI_agentic with repair multiplier phi), and pragmatic adoption via hybrid estimation and a staged migration blueprint. Industrial and research evidence on AI-augmented testing and verification is integrated to motivate separation between synthesis and release authority. Overall, the paper argues that agentic speed does not eliminate engineering discipline; it relocates discipline upstream into specification precision, explicit gates, and auditable provenance.

</details>


### [186] [PrimeAgentOrchestrator: Memory-Primed Agent Spawning for Personal AI Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20342)
*Myron Koch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PAO是一个为LLM编码代理（Claude Code）提供"记忆预加载"的系统，通过并行查询PostgreSQL实体-观测数据库和Cloudflare Worker语义搜索索引两个独立记忆后端，将编译后的简报通过文件系统注入方式在代理启动时交付，实现了从空上下文窗口到携带历史记忆的会话启动，并经过四个月的实际部署验证了其多代上下文交付机制的设计演进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码代理每次会话都从空上下文窗口开始，丢弃了先前工作中积累的知识，导致重复劳动和效率低下。现有系统缺乏在代理启动时自动加载用户已有个人数据库中相关记忆的能力。

Method: PAO在代理启动时并行查询两个独立运行的记忆后端（PostgreSQL实体-观测数据库和Cloudflare Worker语义搜索索引），使用各自后端的检索策略融合结果，通过文件系统注入利用宿主代理的配置自动读取行为传递简报，并管理完整的代理生命周期，包括信任预播种、带错误检测的就绪轮询和自适应终端文本注入。

Result: 该系统经过2025年12月至2026年3月共四个月的常规部署，作为经验报告记录了上下文交付机制的三代演进、促使每次重新设计的失败模式，以及桥接异构记忆系统而非构建统一系统的工程权衡。

Conclusion: PAO证明通过桥接多个异构记忆后端而非构建统一记忆系统，可以在不牺牲各后端优势的前提下为编码代理提供有效的记忆预加载，其三代机制演进过程中揭示的失败模式和工程权衡为该领域提供了有价值的实践参考。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) coding agents start each session with an empty context window, discarding accumulated knowledge from prior work. We present PrimeAgentOrchestrator (PAO), a system that spawns new instances of Claude Code -- Anthropic's terminal-based coding agent -- pre-loaded with relevant memories compiled from the user's existing personal databases. At spawn time, PAO queries two independently-operated memory backends in parallel (a PostgreSQL entity-observation database and a Cloudflare Worker semantic search index), fuses results using backend-specific retrieval strategies, and delivers the compiled briefing via filesystem injection that exploits the host agent's configuration auto-read behavior. PAO manages the full agent lifecycle including trust pre-seeding, readiness polling with error detection, and adaptive terminal text injection. We report on four months of regular deployment (December 2025 through March 2026) as an experience report, documenting three generations of context delivery mechanisms, the failure modes that motivated each redesign, and the engineering tradeoffs of bridging heterogeneous memory systems rather than building a unified one.

</details>


### [187] [Truth Lies Deep: Countering Semantic Camouflage via Latent Intent Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20378)
*Md. Hasib Ur Rahman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM的安全对齐机制存在表面化问题，仅依赖生成末期的拒绝机制而无法消除预训练中习得的有害知识。研究提出了"语义伪装"攻击概念，通过将有害意图包装在良性叙事背景中绕过安全护栏。在三个SLM家族（Phi-3、Qwen2.5、Gemma-2b）上分析发现了一个普遍的"意图地平线"——在模型约15-20%层深处发生有害意图表征的崩塌。基于此提出潜伏意图验证（LIV）轻量级探测防御，在PKU-SafeRLHF数据集上超越标准护栏20-50%的性能提升，且无需重新训练模型即可有效中和零日语义攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM的安全对齐本质上停留在表面层面，仅通过生成末期的拒绝机制进行防护，并未真正消除预训练阶段习得的有害概念知识。这种架构层面的脱节导致模型容易受到"语义伪装"攻击——将有害意图嵌入良性叙事语境中即可绕过标准输入与输出护栏。研究者旨在揭示这一安全漏洞的潜在表征机制，并提出有效的轻量级防御方案。

Method: 首先，对三个不同的小语言模型家族（Phi-3、Qwen2.5和Gemma-2b）在对抗性压力下进行潜在激活轨迹分析，识别出有害意图表征崩塌的关键深度（"意图地平线"，约占总层数的15-20%）。其次，通过比较早层与晚层的表征可区分性，验证早层保留可检测的"危害签名"。最后，基于这一洞察提出潜伏意图验证（LIV），一种在早层进行轻量级探测的防御方法，在PKU-SafeRLHF数据集上进行评估。

Result: 晚层对伪装攻击的表征在数学上与安全查询不可区分（检测率低于20%），而早层表征保留了清晰可检测的"危害签名"。LIV在所有测试架构上比标准护栏的性能高出20-50%，能有效中和零日语义攻击，且无需模型重训练。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了一个普遍存在于不同模型家族的安全架构弱点，证明安全对齐不应仅在生成末期层进行，而在更早的层中保留有害意图的可检测表征为轻量级防御提供了可行路径。LIV作为一种无需重训练的探测式防御，为提升LLM在语义伪装攻击下的鲁棒性提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Safety alignment in Large Language Models (LLMs) is often superficial, relying on refusal mechanisms that trigger only at the final stages of generation without erasing the foundational knowledge of harmful concepts acquired during pretraining. This study demonstrates that this architectural disconnect leaves models vulnerable to Semantic Camouflage -- adversarial attacks that wrap harmful intent in benign narrative contexts (e.g., creative writing), effectively bypassing standard input and output guardrails. By analyzing the latent activation trajectories of three distinct Small Language Model (SLM) families (Phi-3, Qwen2.5, and Gemma-2b) under adversarial stress, this research identifies a universal ``Intent Horizon'' -- a critical depth (typically 15--20\% of total layers) where the model's distinct, pre-trained representation of harmful intent collapses as it contextualizes the query into a ``safe'' narrative. Results indicate that while late-layer representations of camouflaged attacks are mathematically indistinguishable from safe queries (Detection Rate $< 20\%$), early-layer representations retain a distinct, detectable ``harm signature.'' Leveraging this insight, this paper proposes Latent Intent Verification (LIV), a lightweight probing defense. Experiments on the PKU-SafeRLHF dataset demonstrate that LIV outperforms standard guardrails by a margin of 20--50\% across all tested architectures, effectively neutralizing zero-day semantic attacks without requiring model retraining.

</details>


### [188] [Categorical AI phenomenology: A first-person approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20420)
*Robert Prentner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"现象学优先"的人工意识研究方法，通过Q-网络导出的范畴论数学框架，将意识重新概念化为智能体与世界界面的主观体验，强调第一人称结构和关系性特征，并与4E认知科学取向对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人工意识研究通常从第三人称或功能主义视角出发，缺乏对主观体验（第一人称现象学结构）的形式化处理。作者旨在建立一种以现象学为出发点、能数学化描述"界面意识"的框架，弥合计算系统与主观体验之间的鸿沟。

Method: 采用现象学优先的进路，将意识定义为主体通过与世界界面的相互作用所涌现的主观体验。方法论上，利用从Q-网络（强化学习中的价值网络）导出的范畴论结构来形式化行动与现象学不变量；将Q-网络视为编码智能体-世界相互作用的"关系性界面"，类比计算机动态状态对其感觉输入、先前状态和行动的依赖。

Result: 构建了一个严格的"界面意识"框架，能够描述将信息处理嵌入现象学结构的计算系统；同时与4E认知观（具身、嵌入、延展、生成）相一致，提供了基于范畴数学的人工现象学原理性、关系性阐述。

Conclusion: 该工作为人工意识研究提供了一个以现象学为基础、以范畴论为工具的严格框架，证明了Q-网络的界面结构可以承担现象学不变量建模的职能，为计算系统的主观体验描述开辟了理论路径。

Abstract: This paper develops a phenomenology-first approach to artificial consciousness by reframing consciousness as the subjective experience enacted through an agent's interface with the world. We shift the methodological focus to first-person structures, modeled mathematically by categories derived from Q-networks to capture actions and phenomenological invariants. In this framework, Q-networks are conceptualized as relational interfaces encoding agent-world interaction, analogous to how the dynamical states of a computer depend on its sensory inputs, previous states, and actions. Our work provides a rigorous framework for interface consciousness to describe computational systems that embed information-processing into phenomenological structure. The approach aligns with 4E approaches to cognition by emphasizing enactive, embedded, and extended dimensions of experience. The paper thus offers a principled, relational, and phenomenological account of artificial phenomenology grounded in categorical mathematics.

</details>


### [189] [A Survey on Foundations and Frontiers of Multimodal Agentic Frameworks: Techniques and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20379)
*Neel Mokaria,Rishie Raj,Dheeraj Baiju,Xiaoqian Shen,Shraman Pramanick,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Arda Senocak,Mike Zheng Shou,Philip Torr,Mohamed Elhoseiny,Yapeng Tian,Ruohan Gao,Salman Khan,Sayan Nag,Sanjoy Chowdhury,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本综述系统性地分析了多模态性如何在智能体框架的核心功能模块（感知、推理、规划、记忆、行动）中发挥作用，通过以模态为中心的视角追踪从纯文本智能体到多模态智能体的演化过程，并审视不同模态融合架构（委派式、晚期融合、早期融合）对智能体能力的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有大量关于LLM智能体的综述，但近年来多模态性如何塑造智能体能力尚未被系统性地研究。随着大规模多模态模型（LMMs）的兴起，智能体系统能够处理图像、音频、视频等多种模态信息，这对智能体的真实世界适用性产生了重大影响，亟需一个系统性的综述来填补这一空白。

Method: 该综述采用模块化分析框架，从智能体框架的核心功能模块（感知、推理、规划、记忆、行动）出发，考察多模态性的影响。通过模态中心的分类法，将现有的多模态智能体工作按委派式（delegated）、晚期融合（late-fusion）和早期融合（early-fusion）三种架构进行组织，并连接架构设计选择与智能体能力。同时，综述覆盖了机器人、GUI与网页导航、多模态内容生成与编辑、长视频理解与检索等多个应用领域，并分析了性能、效率与可扩展性之间的权衡。

Result: 该综述系统性地整理并分类了多模态智能体领域的现有工作，展示了从文本为中心的智能体向多模态框架演化的路径，总结了不同模态集成架构的能力特征，分析了多个应用领域中多模态智能体的性能表现，并讨论了训练与推理成本、延迟和部署约束等效率-可扩展性权衡问题。

Conclusion: 通过聚焦多模态性对智能体设计的影响，该综述识别了当前研究中的关键空白，并提出了朝着稳健、通用的智能系统发展的路线图。

Abstract: Advances in large language models (LLMs) have fueled a wave of research into agency: the ability to reason, plan, and act. This effort has produced agentic frameworks that orchestrate perception, memory, and decision-making around powerful LLM backbones. With the advent of large multimodal models (LMMs), these systems can process and integrate diverse modalities, including images, audio, and video, thereby improving their real-world applicability. Yet, while surveys of LLM-based agents exist, the role of multimodality in shaping agency has not been systematically examined in recent years. This survey fills the gap by analyzing the impact of multimodality across the core functional modules of the agentic framework: perception, reasoning, planning, memory, and action. Using this lens, we trace the evolution from text-centric agents to multimodal frameworks, examine how modalities are integrated through delegated, late-fusion, and early-fusion architectures, and assess the emergence of agentic behaviors enabled by grounded perception and multimodal reasoning. We organize existing work through a modality-centric taxonomy that links architectural design choices to agent capabilities. Moreover, we review multimodal agentic systems across various application domains, including Robotics, GUI & Web Navigation, Multimedia Content Generation & Editing, and Long-form Video Understanding & Retrieval. Beyond capabilities, we analyze performance across these settings and discuss efficiency-scalability trade-offs, including training and inference costs, latency, and deployment constraints. By focusing on the impact of multimodality in agentic design, we aim to identify key gaps and chart a roadmap toward robust and general-purpose intelligent systems.

</details>


### [190] [Interpretable Multimodal Classification with Linear Discriminant Tree Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20384)
*Mojtaba Moattari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于树集成（Tree-based Ensemble）的多模态情感与行为分类框架，在保持竞争力的同时提供可解释的特征重要性。该框架通过编码、聚类、路由和一种改进的特征重要性指标，在IEMOCAP和CMU-MOSI上分别取得了优于Multimodal Transformer和Interpretable Multimodal Routing的性能，同时显著提升了人类标注者对齐的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高容量模型（尤其是Transformer）在文本、音频、视觉多模态融合任务中虽能达到出色的预测精度，但其分布式表示与深层非线性结构难以赋予各模态特征有意义的权重，限制了在临床情感监控和教学评估等对可信度要求高的场景中的应用。

Method: 该框架将每个模态编码为token，提取并聚类概念以降低维度，融合后的模态通过树集成分类器路由，并使用一种新改进的特征重要性指标来解释趋势。改进的重要性指标降低了二分类任务中负类的影响，从而提升指标/标记物检测能力。提出的模型包括Linear Discriminant Tree (LDT)、Linear Discriminant Forest (LDF)和Linear Discriminant AdaBoost (LDAB)。

Result: 提出的树集成模型相对Multimodal Transformer取得了4.3%的F1-mod提升，相对Interpretable Multimodal Routing (IMR)取得了3.0%的准确率提升。基于改进特征重要性提取的跨模态概念，与人类标注者的一致性显著高于默认特征重要性（IEMOCAP上62.2% vs 43.2%；CMU-MOSI上46.7% vs 32.1%）。

Conclusion: 树集成框架能够同时兼顾多模态情感分类的准确性和可解释性，改进的特征重要性方法更好地捕捉了人类可理解的关键跨模态线索，为可解释多模态融合提供了有效的替代方案。

Abstract: Multimodal affect and behaviour classifiers that fuse heterogeneous text, audio, and visual streams must simultaneously achieve competitive accuracy and produce human-understandable explanations of the cues driving their decisions -- a dual objective that current high-capacity models, notably Transformers, only partially address. While Transformers attain strong predictive performance, their distributed representations and deep nonlinearity make it difficult to assign meaningful importance weights to individual multimodal features, limiting their use in trust-sensitive applications such as clinical affect monitoring and educational assessment. We address this gap by developing a framework based on tree-based ensembles that balances accuracy and interpretability. The framework encodes each modality into tokens, extracts and clusters concepts to reduce dimensionality, routes the fused modalities through tree-based ensemble classifiers, and interprets trends using a novel modified feature importance metric. The modified importance reduces the influence of the negative class in binary classification tasks, thereby improving indicator or marker detection. The proposed tree-based ensembles -- Linear Discriminant Tree (LDT), Linear Discriminant Forest (LDF), and Linear Discriminant AdaBoost (LDAB) -- achieve F1-mod gains of 4.3\% over the Multimodal Transformer and accuracy gains of 3.0\% over the primary interpretable multimodal baseline, Interpretable Multimodal Routing (IMR). The proposed multimodal feature importance extracts salient inter-modal concepts with substantially higher human-annotator agreement scores than default feature importance (62.2\% vs.\ 43.2\% on IEMOCAP; 46.7\% vs.\ 32.1\% on CMU-MOSI).

</details>


### [191] [Representation Affects Retrieval: A Case Study of Skill Discovery and Routing in a Multimodal Agent Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20389)
*Kevin Dela Rosa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过Tinycloud（生产级多模态视频智能体框架）的案例研究，探讨了智能体如何从技能库中为任务选择合适技能。核心发现是：在系统提示中暴露技能并非单调有益——部分暴露可能造成词汇竞争，抑制正确选择。研究比较了工具技能（包装单个API）和工作流技能（编排多个调用并产出交付物）两种技能表示，以及内联全文和单行列表两种暴露方式，并通过六任务消融实验验证了这一结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的智能体框架需要从不断增长的技能库中检索并排序最合适的技能。在小规模场景下，LLM规划器通过系统提示中暴露的技能表示直接进行上下文内选择，无需显式的嵌入检索。作者希望理解这种小规模（small-N）上下文选择机制与大规模嵌入检索之间的对应关系，并揭示部分暴露技能可能带来的负面效应。

Method: 作者提出将上下文内技能选择视为大规模嵌入检索的小规模对应物。Tinycloud采用两种技能表示：tool-skills（包装单个外部API/系统工具，作为原始词汇）和workflow-skills（编排多个tool-skill调用并渲染模板以产出命名交付物）。通过两种系统提示暴露表面：内联全文（自动加载技能）和单行列表（按需技能）。设计了一个六任务选择消融实验，涵盖三种暴露模式：all-on（全部自动加载）、default（生产默认，自动加载+按需列表）、all-off（全部关闭）。

Result: 实验结果显示：all-on模式下每个任务都能正确选择目标技能；all-off模式严重拖慢执行并导致硬性发现失败；生产默认模式有一个任务被错误路由，因为其词汇信号与自动加载的工具技能发生碰撞，将规划器注意力从列表中的工作流技能引开。这证明部分暴露技能会引入词汇竞争并抑制正确选择。

Conclusion: 在提示中暴露技能并非越多越好或越少越好——部分暴露可能比全部暴露或全部关闭产生更差的效果，因为词汇竞争会干扰LLM的选择。作者将该观察与大规模基于检索的技能路由研究联系起来，并将本文定位为案例研究而非基准测试。

Abstract: A production agent harness must discover and rank, from a growing library of skills, the one most appropriate for a user's task. At small scale this selection happens in context: the LLM planner chooses among skill representations exposed in its system prompt, without an explicit embedding-based retrieval step. We treat this in-context selection as the small-N counterpart to embedding-based skill retrieval at scale, and present a case study of how Tinycloud, a production multimodal video agent harness, represents its skills for the planner. The harness ships skills under two recurring representations: tool-skills that wrap a single external API or system tool and serve as primitive vocabulary, and workflow-skills that orchestrate tool-skill calls plus a template render to produce one named deliverable. The harness exposes them via two surfaces in the system prompt: an inlined-body surface (full instructions, scripts, templates) for autoloaded skills, and a one-line listing for on-demand skills. A six-task selection ablation across three exposure regimes (all-on, default, all-off) shows that full autoload selects the gold skill on every task; all-off slows execution and produces hard discovery failures; and the production default misroutes one task because its lexical signal collides with an autoloaded tool-skill that pulls planner attention away from a listed workflow-skill. The headline finding is that in-prompt exposure of skills is not monotonically helpful: partial exposure can create lexical competition that suppresses correct selection. We connect this small-N observation to recent retrieval-based skill-routing work at large scale, and frame this contribution as a case study rather than a benchmark.

</details>


### [192] [Nexus: Depth-Adaptive KV-Cache Splicing and Retrieval-Decoupled Tool Routing for Agentic LLMs on Unified Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20397)
*Mustafa Arslan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Nexus，通过INT8语义查找缓冲器（SLB）解耦工具路由与schema预填充成本，并利用编译后的schema KV块拼接与深度自适应后缀重解码，实现工具注册表扩展时TTFT加速且输出保真不回归（top-1一致、D_KL≈0），在250工具规模下仍保持约89%路由准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于MCP的智能体LLM每轮都会重新编码冗长的工具schema，由于预填充复杂度随序列长度呈二次增长，工具注册表扩大时TTFT被预填充主导，成为性能瓶颈。

Method: 两层杠杆：(1) 主杠杆——INT8语义查找缓冲器（SLB），用校准的交叉编码器边际门控做检索式路由，参数生成在压缩的文本签名（中位19 token）上进行；(2) 次要有界杠杆——将编译好的schema KV块移植进实时上下文，但受RoPE相位漂移限制，超过阈值P=256后采用深度自适应后缀重解码修复拼接缝，可升级为完整重预填充。

Result: 路由准确率在规模扩展到250工具时保持约89%（此时基线拼接全部schema已溢出上下文窗口）；首参数token比完整schema重预填充快1.66倍，主上下文token节省约80%；中等深度下TTFT加速1.1-1.7倍，深度上下文时收敛至持平（最低0.98x）；off-anchor RoPE保真边界与参考无关漂移门失效（Spearman rho=0.193）为两个负面结果。

Conclusion: Nexus通过解耦路由与schema预填充成本，在扩展工具注册表时保持路由精度并加速TTFT，同时以never-regress属性保证输出保真；定性边界可泛化，但定量指标受限于具体模型元组（Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct Q4_K_M on Apple silicon）环境。

Abstract: Agentic large language models (LLMs) on the Model Context Protocol (MCP) re-encode verbose tool schemas every turn, so prefill - quadratic in sequence length - dominates time-to-first-token (TTFT) as the tool registry grows. Nexus's primary lever is to decouple routing from the schema-prefill cost: an INT8 semantic lookaside buffer (SLB) with a calibrated cross-encoder margin gate selects tools by retrieval, and arguments are generated over a compressed textual signature (median 19 tokens) rather than over spliced key/value (KV) cache. This path is depth-independent: routing accuracy stays near 89% as the registry scales to 250 tools - where a concatenate-all-schemas baseline overflows the context window entirely - and it reaches a first-argument token 1.66x sooner than a full-schema re-prefill at a ~80% main-context token saving. As a secondary, bounded lever we transplant a compiled schema KV block directly into the live context. This is fundamentally limited by rotary position embedding (RoPE) phase drift: an anchored splice is output-exact, but off-anchor placement corrupts attention, so beyond a threshold P=256 Nexus repairs the seam with a depth-adaptive suffix redecode that escalates to a full re-prefill. The resulting never-regress property is a guarantee on output fidelity (top-1 agreement, D_KL approx. 0) - not on latency, which can dip to 0.98x before converging to parity - alongside a 1.1-1.7x TTFT speedup at moderate depth that narrows to parity at deep context. Two negative results bound the design: the off-anchor RoPE fidelity boundary, and the failure of a reference-free drift gate to predict drift (Spearman rho = 0.193). All measurements are from one model tuple (Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct Q4_K_M) on Apple-silicon unified memory; the qualitative boundaries generalize, while the quantitative envelope is tuple-specific.

</details>


### [193] [TreeWY: Speculative Verification for Gated DeltaNet Hybrids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20961)
*Sneha Murthy Ghantasala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对混合开源模型中Gated DeltaNet（GDN）线性注意力层在投机解码（speculative decoding）时因快照整个循环状态导致内存开销过大的问题，提出基于树状WY变换的方法，消除了逐节点状态快照，仅需单次三角求解计算各草稿节点输出，并在提交时重构唯一接受的状态。该方法在Qwen3.5（35B/397B）上的服务基准测试中，显著降低了投机循环状态内存与KV缓存压力，提升了吞吐量并大幅降低首字延迟（TTFT），同时使更宽的草稿树成为可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代混合模型中大多数层为线性注意力（Gated DeltaNet）层，携带小型固定尺寸循环状态而非增长的KV缓存，这使普通解码内存高效，但损害了投机解码。在投机解码中，验证一批草稿token并回滚被拒绝者时，现有系统需在每个草稿位置对GDN层快照全部循环状态，且这些快照无法在草稿树分支间共享，导致宽且高接受率的草稿树在内存上不可行。因此需要消除这些快照以解决内存瓶颈。

Method: 提出树状WY变换（tree-structured WY transform）应用于门控delta规则。利用该变换，可用一次三角求解（triangular solve）计算草稿树中所有节点的输出，而非为每个节点保存完整状态快照。在提交时仅重构被接受的单一状态，存储小型伪值矩阵（pseudo-value matrix）而非逐节点状态。该方法推导仅依赖门控delta规则本身，不依赖其他架构细节，故具有广泛的适用性。

Result: 在两个规模的混合模型家族（Qwen3.5 35B和397B）的服务基准测试中，在相同接受长度下，该方法削减了投机循环状态内存和KV缓存压力，将释放的HBM转化为更高的吞吐量和显著更低的TTFT（在内存受限场景下）；在非内存受限场景下仅增加几个百分点的开销。对于草稿树宽度，节省的内存使更宽、接受率更高的草稿树变得可行，但尚未实现吞吐量上的优势。

Conclusion: 该方法通过消除GDN层的逐位置状态快照，以少量计算开销换取大幅内存节省，在内存受限的服务场景中显著提升吞吐量并降低TTFT，同时解锁了更宽草稿树的内存可行性，为混合架构模型的投机解码提供了更高效的内存管理方案。

Abstract: Modern open models are hybrids: most layers are linear-attention (Gated DeltaNet, GDN) layers carrying a small fixed-size recurrent state instead of a growing key-value (KV) cache. This makes ordinary decoding memory-efficient, but hurts speculative decoding. To verify a batch of draft tokens and then roll back the rejected ones, today's systems snapshot the full recurrent state at every draft position for GDN layers, and those snapshots cannot be shared across branches of a draft tree, so a wide, high-acceptance tree becomes memory-infeasible. We remove the snapshots. Using a tree-structured WY transform of the gated delta rule, we compute every draft node's output with a single triangular solve and reconstruct only the one accepted state on commit, storing a small pseudo-value matrix instead of per-node states; the derivation depends only on the gated delta rule, not on any other architectural detail. In serving benchmarks on two scales of one hybrid model family (Qwen3.5 35B and 397B) this cuts speculative recurrent-state memory and KV-cache pressure at identical acceptance length, turning the freed HBM into higher throughput and much lower time-to-first-token (TTFT) wherever memory binds, and costing a few percent where it does not. For tree width the same memory buys affordability: a wider, higher-acceptance draft becomes possible, though not yet a throughput win.

</details>


### [194] [Environmental Slow AI: Design Principles for Generative Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20398)
*Vanessa Utz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，从环境人文学传统出发，批判当前生成式AI中嵌入的"最大化主义"价值观，并提出以环境可持续性为核心价值的"慢AI"（Slow AI）设计原则。论文提出五项设计原则（节制、充裕、选择性而非保留、物质可见性、摩擦作为可用性特征），每项原则同时包含设计实现层和解释性反思层，旨在通过将反思性纳入设计来恢复被无摩擦默认设置悄然剥夺的人类能动性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI系统大规模生产文化制品的同时，也通过其设计内嵌了特定的文化价值观；这些价值观一旦被识别，就面临被有意重塑的可能。作者认为当前生成式AI承载的是"最大化主义"价值观，与可持续性相悖，因此需要从环境人文学视角提出替代性设计原则。

Method: 采用环境人文学传统作为理论框架，在"慢AI"这一已在多个研究与实践中流通的术语下发展设计原则。论文提出五项设计原则（节制、充裕、选择性而非保留、物质可见性、摩擦作为可用性特征），每项原则对照当前广泛部署系统的实际设计加以说明，并在每个原则中区分两个操作层面：设计实现层与解释性反思层。

Result: 形成了五条以可持续性为核心价值的慢AI设计原则，每条原则均以现有主流生成式AI系统的设计为例进行对照说明，且每项原则均可在设计实现与解释性反思两个层面运作，从而将原本被无摩擦默认设置剥夺的决策权重新交还用户与开发者。

Conclusion: 这些原则共同扩展了人类能动性——它们通过将解释性反思内建于设计之中，恢复了被无摩擦默认值悄然移除的决策机会，从而在生成式AI系统中实现以环境可持续性为核心价值的转向。

Abstract: Generative AI (genAI) systems produce cultural artefacts at scale, but they also reflect embedded cultural values through their design. Once identified, these values become open to deliberate reshaping. This position paper examines the maximalist values of current generative AI through an environmental humanities tradition and proposes design principles in which environmental sustainability serves as the core value instead. The principles are developed under the umbrella of Slow AI, a term that already circulates across several distinct research and practice programs. Five design principles are articulated (restraint, sufficiency, selectivity over retention, material visibility, and friction as affordance), each of them illustrated against the current design of widely deployed systems. Each principle operates at two levels: a design implementation, and an interpretive layer at which users and developers are prompted toward reflective engagement with the system. Together these principles extend human agency by restoring decisions that frictionless defaults have silently removed and do so by building interpretive reflection into design.

</details>


### [195] [When Retrieval Fails Before It Begins: Structurally Indirect Prerequisite Eviction as a Retention Failure in Agentic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20400)
*Minkyu Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文挑战了现有agentic memory研究中"检索中心"的范式，指出在固定预算下存在一个被忽视的"检索前失败模式"——结构性间接先决条件驱逐（structurally indirect prerequisite eviction），即与查询弱对齐的上游记忆块在预算压力下被错误清除。作者提供了该失败的操作性定义、可复现的确定性基准和逐种子追踪诊断，并评估了DSGC（依赖感知语义垃圾回收）这一单跳图感知规则。在主干套件中，DSGC将完整链路保留率从0.03提升至0.90（词法编码器）以及从0.23提升至1.00（句子编码器），并通过鲁棒性检验界定了单跳规则的适用与退化边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有agentic memory系统默认"只要检索足够好，保留失败即可被容忍"，但这一假设未被验证。作者识别出检索前的独特失败模式——结构性间接先决条件驱逐，即预算压缩导致上游关键块（与查询仅弱相关）被清除，从而使得即使检索完美也无法恢复完整推理链路。

Method: 作者首先给出该失败模式的操作性定义，构建了可复现的确定性基准，并提供逐种子（per-seed）的追踪诊断工具。随后提出并评估DSGC——一种单跳图感知的语义垃圾收集规则，用于在预算有限时更智能地决定哪些记忆块应当被保留。鲁棒性检验进一步考察了预算和规模变化对单跳规则表现的影响。

Result: 在主干实验中，DSGC将完整链路保留率从0.03显著提升至0.90（词法编码器），以及从0.23提升至1.00（句子编码器）。这表明DSGC在两种编码器设置下均能大幅改善代理记忆中的结构依赖保留。鲁棒性检验则揭示了在不同预算和规模setting下单跳规则的保持或退化情况。

Conclusion: 本文确证了"保留先于检索"应被视为独立的失败边界，DSGC作为轻量的单跳图感知规则能有效缓解结构性间接先决条件驱逐问题。作者发布了完整流水线和失败事后分析（postmortem），为后续在代理记忆中进行机制性分析提供了工具和基准。

Abstract: Agentic memory under a fixed budget involves two stages: retention and retrieval. Existing retrieval-centered paradigms implicitly assume necessary evidence survives eviction, but we challenge this by isolating a pre-retrieval failure mode: structurally indirect prerequisite eviction, in which upstream blocks weakly aligned with the query are discarded under budget pressure. We provide an operational definition of this failure, a reproducible deterministic benchmark, and per-seed trace diagnostics. Finally, we evaluate Dependency-aware Semantic Garbage Collection (DSGC), a one-hop graph-aware rule. In our main suite, DSGC improves full-chain retention from 0.03 to 0.90 under a lexical encoder and from 0.23 to 1.00 under a sentence encoder. Robustness checks then identify the budget and scaling regimes where the one-hop rule holds or degrades. Our released pipeline and failure postmortem support mechanistic analysis of retention before retrieval as a distinct failure boundary.

</details>


### [196] [World models of environment, agent and joint agent-environment systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20401)
*Manuel Baltieri,Filippo Torresan,Yivan Zhang,Alexander Boyd,Fernando E. Rosas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出世界模型的一个先验性区分维度——所建模的"通道"（环境通道、智能体通道、联合过程），并利用计算力学为三种通道定义典范预测模型（ε-转换器/ε-机器），进一步构建由闭环耦合诱导的支持受限的典范环境与智能体模型，证明其可经联合因果状态分解，且耦合与支持受限能从根本上改变模型结构与复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型研究主要关注模型预测的变量（观测、奖励、状态等），而忽视了更根本的问题：模型究竟建模哪个通道。作者认为环境通道与智能体通道的区分是预测变量之上的先验区分，需要从理论上澄清不同世界模型各自"是什么的模型"，以及耦合与支持受限如何影响其典范预测结构与复杂度。

Method: 使用计算力学框架，将三种通道（环境通道O|A、智能体通道A|O、联合过程(A,O)）分别定义为ε-转换器或ε-机器以得到典范预测模型。随后通过闭环耦合诱导支持受限的环境与智能体模型，其预测等价关系限定在真实交互所支持的未来延续上；关键结构结果是支持受限环境状态经联合因果状态分解，转换结构直接由联合模型诱导，智能体侧构造对偶。

Result: 典范环境模型恢复标准预测状态表示，而其他两种通道给出智能体与联合系统的对应典范模型。给出一个POMDP/控制器实例，其中无限制环境模型状态无限，而由耦合诱导的支持受限典范模型状态有限。这表明耦合与支持受限可从根本上降低模型复杂度。

Conclusion: 该框架澄清了不同世界模型究竟建模的是何种对象（哪个通道），并揭示了耦合诱导的支持受限如何改变其典范预测结构与复杂度，为理解和构建世界模型提供了更精确的理论基础。

Abstract: World models are a central component of model-based reinforcement learning. They are usually discussed in terms of what variables they predict, such as observations, rewards, states, latent or information states. We argue that there is a prior distinction: which channel they model. We consider three cases: the environment channel $O_{:} \mid A_{:}$, the agent channel $A_{:} \mid O_{:}$, and the realised joint process $(A, O)_{:}$, equivalently viewed as a channel with no inputs. Using computational mechanics, we define canonical predictive models for these three cases as $ε$-transducers or $ε$-machines. Canonical environment models recover standard predictive state representations, while the other two give analogous notions of canonical models for the agent and the joint system. We then build canonical support-restricted environment and agent models induced by closed-loop coupling, whose predictive equivalences range over continuations supported by the realised interaction. The key structural result is that canonical support-restricted environment states factor through the canonical joint causal states, and their transition structure is induced directly from the joint model; the agent-side construction is dual. Finally, we give a POMDP/controller example in which the unrestricted environment model has infinitely many states while the canonical support-restricted model induced by the coupling is finite. The framework clarifies what different world models are models of, and how coupling and support restriction can change their canonical predictive structure and complexity.

</details>


### [197] [StateSight: Benchmarking Latent Spatial-State Reconstruction in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20414)
*Michelle Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出StateSight基准，用于评估视觉语言模型从单张图像重建潜在空间结构的能力，包含立方体网对面推理、遮挡立方体塔计数和4-邻域连通分量计数三类任务，每类300个程序生成的单图提示。GPT-5.5和Claude Sonnet 5的表现显著低于人类基线（80.8%/68.8%/64.3%），且格式有效的输出可能掩盖无法恢复空间结构的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型的基准往往将感知、OCR、领域知识、语言先验和推理混在一起评估，难以单独衡量模型从单张图像重建潜在空间结构的能力。作者希望设计一个可验证、可精确评分的程序生成基准，以隔离并评估这一核心能力。

Method: 作者采用程序化生成方式构建了三个任务族的基准（StateSight），每类包含300个单图提示，具有确定性oracle标签和精确匹配评分。三个任务分别为：立方体网对面推理、遮挡立方体塔计数、4-邻域连通分量计数。此外还引入配套数据集StateSight-Steps，包含900个交错图像-文本示例和3600个确定性中间视觉状态。

Result: GPT-5.5在三个任务上的准确率分别为59.3%、33.3%和28.3%；Claude Sonnet 5分别为53.3%、18.7%和7.3%。所有直接运行均无格式错误。30名参与者在60个样本上的人类基线在所有任务上均超过模型，平均准确率分别为80.8%、68.8%和64.3%。可见推导分析发现了图像状态重建和推理过程中的循环性错误。

Conclusion: 格式有效的响应可能掩盖模型无法恢复可验证视觉推理所需空间结构这一缺陷。当前视觉语言模型即使输出格式完全正确，在空间结构重建和推理方面仍与人类有显著差距，需要专门的基准和数据集来衡量和改善这一能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models are increasingly used for multimodal question answering, yet their ability to reconstruct latent spatial structure from a single image remains difficult to isolate. Broad benchmarks often combine perception, optical character recognition, domain knowledge, linguistic priors, and reasoning in the same evaluation. We introduce StateSight, a procedurally generated benchmark for cube-net opposite-face reasoning, occluded cube-tower counting, and 4-neighbor connected-component counting. Each task family contains 300 single-image prompts with deterministic oracle labels and exact-match scoring. OpenAI GPT-5.5, using the API model identifier gpt-5.5, achieved 59.3%, 33.3%, and 28.3% accuracy across the three tasks, while Claude Sonnet 5 achieved 53.3%, 18.7%, and 7.3%. All final direct runs had zero format errors. A 30-participant human baseline on 60 items exceeded both models on every task, with mean accuracies of 80.8%, 68.8%, and 64.3%. Visible-derivation analysis identified recurring errors in image-state reconstruction and reasoning procedure. We also introduce StateSight-Steps, a companion dataset of 900 interleaved image-text examples and 3,600 deterministic intermediate visual states. The results show that format-valid responses can mask failures to recover the spatial structure required for verifiable visual inference.

</details>


### [198] [Who Delegates to AI? Evidence from 53,000 Agent Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20425)
*Hyeongjae Lee,Jihyang Cheon,Lanu Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"委托暴露"（delegated exposure）概念，以Manus Skills Marketplace约53,000条智能体技能规范与约18,000条O*NET任务陈述的语义相似度构建代理式采用指数（AAI），衡量工人是否已将任务委托给AI嵌入工作流。发现AAI暴露分布与既有AI暴露框架差异显著，且AAI在高教育水平职业及工资分布顶端出现下降，技术可行性无法完全解释这一缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI暴露度量仅衡量AI在技术上"能做什么"（潜力），而非工人"实际是否采用"（现实）。为弥补这一核心缺口，需要一个新的度量层面来区分技术可行性与实际采用行为。

Method: 作者提出"委托暴露"作为新增度量层，将其操作化为代理式采用指数（AAI）。具体方法：从Manus Skills Marketplace嵌入约53,000条代理技能规范，计算其与约18,000条O*NET任务陈述的语义相似度，再聚合到职业层面进行统计分析。

Result: 三个主要发现：其一，委托集中的职业与AI风险框架预测的高风险职业差异显著；其二，AAI与实际技术能力高度相关但反映的更多是"可用性"而非"现实使用"；其三，AAI在工资分布顶端之下和学士学位水平达到峰值，在两端均下降。技术可用性解释大部分变异，但无法解释高教育职业的缺口。

Conclusion: 可行性本身不足以解释AI采用的职业分布。高教育职业的采用缺口可能源于两类原因：工作任务本身抵抗预先规范（work that resists advance specification），或专业人员对编码化速度保持自主裁量权。区分这两者并追踪度量随时间演化，需要重复测量。

Abstract: A growing literature measures how far occupations are exposed to AI, but these measures capture where AI could perform tasks, not whether workers have adopted it. We propose a new layer of exposure, delegated exposure, which records whether a worker has committed a task to AI by building it into a workflow. We operationalize it as the Agentic Adoption Index (AAI), which measures how closely an occupation's tasks match the agentic routines practitioners have already built and shared. We embed roughly 53,000 agent skill specifications from the Manus Skills Marketplace, compute their semantic similarity to about 18,000 O*NET task statements, and aggregate to the occupation level. Three findings follow. First, the occupations where delegation concentrates differ sharply from those pre-AI frameworks identified as most at risk. Second, the AAI tracks what AI could do more closely than what workers currently use it for. Third, the AAI peaks below the top of the wage distribution and at the bachelor's level, declining at both extremes. Technical availability explains most of this variation, but not the shortfall among the most educated occupations, so feasibility alone cannot account for who adopts. That shortfall may reflect work that resists advance specification, or professional discretion over the pace of codification. Distinguishing the two, and tracking how these measures diverge over time, will require repeated measurement.

</details>


### [199] [Terminal Agents: A Survey of AI Agents in Command-Line Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20485)
*Yi Bin,Xiaoyang Yuan,Haoxi Zeng,Wencheng Ye,Wenqi Shao,Chen Qian,Wei Ye,Yujuan Ding,Zheng Wang,Pengpeng Zeng,Jingkuan Song,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出以"终端中介执行"（terminal-mediated execution）为核心组织视角的综述框架，将终端智能体定义为通过终端命令执行、文本反馈和有状态环境交互来驱动"行动-观察"循环的系统。文章建立了负载级边界，提出七维终端能力画像，并系统连接了系统架构、能力获取与评估。研究发现：终端智能体的实际行为由模型、接口、框架、运行时和环境共同塑造；可执行轨迹通过行动后果、验证与恢复机制实现学习，而现有评测侧重最终结果，对过程质量、恢复能力和治理的覆盖不均；基准族暴露不同的过程信号且系统对比呈现基准依赖的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于大语言模型智能体的综述将终端中介行为分散于软件工程、工具使用和计算机使用等多个研究领域中，缺乏统一的分析视角，难以系统理解终端智能体的行为机制、能力获取与评估体系，因此需要一个以终端执行为组织主线的统一框架。

Method: 该综述采用"终端中介执行"作为组织透镜，建立负载级（workload-level）边界定义，提出七维终端能力画像（包括行动执行、文本反馈处理、状态ful环境交互等维度），系统性地整合系统架构设计、能力获取（如可执行轨迹驱动的学习）与评估方法论，并通过有界固定条件诊断实验对比不同基准族和系统配置来验证框架的有效性。

Result: 研究发现终端智能体的实际行为是模型、接口、框架、运行时和环境多因素联合塑造的结果；以可执行轨迹为基础的学习依赖行动后果、验证与恢复机制；现有评测过度侧重最终结果而忽视过程质量、恢复与治理维度；不同基准族暴露不同的过程信号，匹配的系统对比揭示基准依赖的性能差异及组件归因的局限性。

Conclusion: 研究建议在系统与运行时的报告、可重放轨迹和过程级证据方面建立更明确的规范，所提出的框架为软件工程及新兴应用领域中终端中介能动性的统一研究提供了基础性参考。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly act through terminals, yet existing surveys disperse terminal-mediated behavior across software engineering, tool use, and computer-use research. We regard terminal agents as systems whose dominant progress-bearing action--observation loop is mediated by terminal command execution, textual feedback, and stateful environment interaction. Using terminal-mediated execution as an organizing lens, this survey establishes workload-level boundaries and connects system architecture, competence acquisition, and evaluation through a seven-dimensional terminal competence profile. Our synthesis shows that realized behavior is jointly shaped by the model, interface, harness, runtime, and environment. Executable trajectories ground learning in action consequences, verification, and recovery, whereas prevailing evaluations emphasize final outcomes and expose process quality, recovery, and governance unevenly. Bounded fixed-condition diagnostics illustrate two implications: benchmark families expose different process signals, and matched system comparisons reveal benchmark-dependent performance and limits of component attribution. These findings motivate explicit reporting of system and runtime conditions, supported by replayable traces and process-level evidence. The framework provides a unified basis for studying terminal-mediated agency across software engineering and emerging application domains.

</details>


### [200] [Lost in Translation: How Universal Ethical Values Fail to Translate Across Global Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20490)
*Ozioma C. Oguine,Munachimso B. Oguine,Cesar Cervera,Jenny Yang,Pooja Voladoddi,Mario Rodriguez,Saif Eddin Bani Malhem,Karla Badillo-Urquiola,Daricia Wilkinson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过对10个国家14位专家的访谈研究，揭示了全球AI伦理框架（如公平、透明、问责）在实际部署中与本地实践存在"翻译鸿沟"，专家们根据本地道德逻辑重新诠释核心价值（如隐私为集体性而非个体性、公平为获取权而非结果平等），并提出迈向多元治理的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI伦理框架将公平、透明、问责等价值观视为普适且可跨情境统一操作的，但这忽视了AI部署所处的结构化不平等条件（基础设施约束、掠夺性实践、技术神秘化）以及本地情境对价值观理解的差异。作者希望揭示这种全球框架与本地实践之间的张力。

Method: 采用质性研究方法，对来自10个国家的14位AI领域专家进行深度访谈，考察他们如何在实践中理解AI、重新诠释核心伦理价值观，并设想治理替代方案。通过跨文化比较分析识别价值观翻译中的"翻译鸿沟"。

Result: 研究发现：AI部署存在结构性不平等，塑造了风险与机会的感知差异；专家根据本地道德逻辑重新诠释价值观——隐私被理解为集体性和关系性的而非个体性的，透明被理解为建立信任的问责机制而非技术披露，公平被理解为获取和代表上的公平而非结果对等；这些差异被识别为全球框架与本地实践之间的"翻译鸿沟"。

Conclusion: 作者提出走向多元治理的路径，主张重新分配认知权威（epistemic authority），将伦理协商视为持续的、情境敏感的进程，而非既定的技术标准。

Abstract: AI ethics frameworks treat values such as fairness, transparency, and accountability as universal and uniformly operationalizable across contexts. We examined how 14 experts across 10 countries made sense of AI in practice, reinterpreted core values, and envisioned governance alternatives. We found that AI deployment is characterized by structurally unequal conditions, marked by infrastructural constraints, extractive practices, and a "mystification" of technology, which fundamentally shape perceptions of risks and opportunities. Our findings reveal that experts reinterpret values to fit local moral logics: privacy as collective and relational rather than individual; transparency as trust-building accountability rather than technical disclosure; and fairness as equity in access and representation rather than parity in outcomes. We identify these as translation gaps between encoded global frameworks and situated local practices. Finally, we propose pathways toward plural governance that redistributes epistemic authority and treats ethical negotiation as an ongoing, context-sensitive process rather than a settled technical standard.

</details>


### [201] [A Temporal Planning Approach for Intelligent Flood Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20510)
*Fazlul Hasan Siddiqui,Md. Monjurul Islam,Sabah Binte Noor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于时间规划的智能洪水响应框架，涵盖洪灾响应的完整操作生命周期，包括优先级驱动的分诊、路线可达性与旅行成本、资源分配和物资管理，并支持执行中重新规划。框架使用ANML和PDDL 2.1两种语言形式化建模，实验验证了其可行性与可扩展性，并为规划器选择提供了指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多个区域同时遭受洪水时，需要在严重资源约束下按正确的时间顺序协调适当的行动。自动化规划可以通过生成时间感知的调度方案来应对这一挑战，因此研究者希望构建一个能够完整建模洪灾响应操作生命周期的智能框架。

Method: 采用时间规划（temporal planning）作为基础，构建洪水响应框架，包含优先级驱动的分诊、路线可达性与旅行成本计算、资源分配和供应管理，并支持执行中意外环境变化下的重新规划。框架同时用ANML和PDDL 2.1两种规划领域语言进行形式化表述，以兼容更广泛的规划器。

Result: 实验结果验证了该框架的可行性和可扩展性，表明洪灾响应场景可以被有效建模并通过时间规划求解，同时为不同场景下的规划器选择提供了指导性建议。

Conclusion: 该智能洪水响应框架通过时间规划技术成功实现了多区域同时洪水响应中的协调调度，且具备跨规划器兼容性和执行中重规划能力，为实际洪水应急管理提供了可行的自动化决策支持方案。

Abstract: Effective response to multiple, simultaneously flooded areas requires coordinating appropriate actions in the correct temporal order, under severe resource constraints. Automated planning provides a foundation for addressing this challenge by generating time-aware schedules, given a formal description of available resources, constraints, and goals. This work presents an intelligent flood-response framework that exploits temporal planning and models the complete operational life cycle of flood response. The framework incorporates priority-driven triage, route accessibility and travel costs, resource allocation, and supply management, while also supporting mid-execution re-planning in response to unexpected environmental changes. The framework is formulated both in the Action Notation Modeling Language (ANML) and the Planning Domain Definition Language (PDDL) 2.1, facilitating compatibility with a wider range of temporal planners. Experimental results establish the feasibility and scalability of the proposed framework, showing that flood response scenarios can be effectively modeled and solved using temporal planning, while providing guidance on planner selection.

</details>


### [202] [FL-MAESTRO: Multi-Agent LLM Orchestration for Resource-Constrained Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20518)
*Jiajun Wu,Zirui Wang,Jiayu Zhou,Qiang Ye,Steve Drew*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FL-MAESTRO，一个利用三个专用LLM智能体分别处理通信拓扑、客户端资源分配和聚合规则三个正交决策的多智能体编排器，用于联邦学习中的联合运行时决策。协调器综合各智能体分析并经过非LLM可行性检查后执行，通过预读服务器的故障预测列表跳过永远不会被聚合的客户端更新，将波动边缘网络中的回合浪费能耗从超过三分之一降至近零，同时保持模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中通信拓扑是运行时变量而非固定设计，且每一轮需要同时耦合决策三个维度（拓扑、资源分配、聚合规则）。现有将LLM引入FL的工作要么只在设置阶段操作，要么只处理单一运行时维度（如客户端选择），缺乏对运行时联合决策的支持。

Method: FL-MAESTRO采用多智能体架构：三个专用LLM智能体分别负责通信拓扑、每客户端资源分配和聚合规则三个决策维度；一个协调器将三者分析综合为单一决策；一个非LLM可行性检查模块在每轮执行前确认决策可行。编排器读取服务器的预测故障列表以阻止不会被聚合的客户端更新，客户端状态以自然文本档案形式读入，支持异构设备类别而无需逐类的能耗模型。

Result: 在非IID CIFAR-10基准上，FL-MAESTRO在匹配最强能耗感知基线精度的同时，将浪费的回合能耗从超过三分之一降低至接近零。

Conclusion: 通过LLM多智能体编排实现FL运行时三大耦合决策的联合优化，能够在不损失精度的前提下大幅削减波动边缘网络中的回合能耗浪费，为LLM在FL系统运行时管理中的应用提供了有效范式。

Abstract: In Federated Learning (FL), the communication topology is a runtime variable rather than a fixed design choice, since links and edge devices drop in and out during training. Each round, the server must commit three coupled decisions, namely the communication topology, per-client resource allocation, and the aggregation rule for combining local updates. Recent agentic systems have begun bringing large language models (LLM) into FL, but the existing line of work either operates at setup time or handles a single runtime dimension such as client selection. We propose FL-MAESTRO, a multi-agent orchestrator that makes the joint runtime FL decision directly through three specialist LLM agents, one per decision dimension. A coordinator combines their analyses into a single decision, and a non-LLM feasibility check confirms it before the round executes. Because the orchestrator consumes the server's predicted-failure list, it withholds clients whose updates would never be aggregated, which removes the dominant source of wasted round energy in classical FL on volatile edge networks. Because client state is read as natural-text profiles, the same orchestrator extends to heterogeneous device classes without per-class energy models. On a non-IID CIFAR-10 benchmark, FL-MAESTRO matches the accuracy of the strongest energy-aware baseline while cutting wasted round energy from over a third to near zero. Code is available at https://github.com/denoslab/FL-MAESTRO.

</details>


### [203] [Volumetric Radiology AI in the Era of Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20549)
*Zanting Ye,Shengyuan Liu,Xin Liu,Chenhui Wang,Zhisong Wang,Jiashuai Liu,Zipei Wang,Cheng Wang,Wentao Pan,Mengjie Fang,Di Dong,Mohammad Salmanpour,Arman Rahmim,Yu Gu,Yong Xia,Hongming Shan,Yixuan Yuan,Yefeng Zheng,Lijun Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了截至2026年7月超过200篇文献，围绕容积（3D）放射学中多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的表征与理解展开，提出了模型层面的容积基础模型、语言对齐与压缩策略，系统层面的智能体编排，以及连接临床应用的Claim-Design-Validation评估框架。核心结论是原生容积建模与智能体能力需匹配任务的空间、定量、上下文和工作流需求，临床可信性依赖于忠实的容积表征、可追溯的系统行为、与声明一致的验证及明确的人机协同监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MLLMs在放射学中的应用存在根本性表征不匹配：临床容积放射学解读需要全容积空间上下文和采集相关的定量信息，而现有MLLMs通常只基于选定的2D图像、压缩视觉表征或报告派生文本。这一矛盾催生了本综述，旨在梳理如何在模型层面保留任务相关的3D信息，并在系统层面实现跨临床工作流的信息访问、校验与整合。

Method: 作者系统检索并分析了截至2026年7月的200余篇出版物，按层次组织文献：模型层面涵盖容积基础模型、语言对齐与压缩策略；系统层面涵盖通过规划、工具、记忆和工作流交互扩展MLLMs的智能体系统；并建立Claim-Design-Validation（声明-设计-验证）框架，用于评估技术、工作流及临床声明是否得到相应设计方法与验证流程的支持。

Result: 研究发现，原生容积建模和智能体能力是否必要，取决于目标任务的空间、定量、上下文和工作流需求——在某些场景中选定的2D视图或报告中介推理可能已足够，而另一些场景则确实需要原生3D建模。文献还显示，临床可信性取决于容积表征的保真度、系统行为可追溯性、与声明一致的验证设计以及现实中可明确界定的人类监督。

Conclusion: 可靠的容积放射学AI需要：（1）保留任务相关3D信息的表征设计；（2）能在临床工作流中访问、验证和整合信息的系统架构；（3）以任务需求驱动的原生容积建模选择而非盲目追求全3D化；（4）严格、与声明对齐的验证框架以及明确的人机协作边界，以建立临床可信度。

Abstract: Advances in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are extending radiological artificial intelligence (AI) beyond task-specific image analysis toward multimodal understanding and reasoning. Volumetric radiology, however, presents a fundamental representational mismatch: clinical interpretation often requires full-volume spatial context and acquisition-dependent quantitative information, whereas current MLLMs are commonly conditioned on selected two-dimensional (2D) images, compressed visual representations, or report-derived text. Reliable volumetric radiology AI therefore requires representations that preserve task-relevant three-dimensional (3D) information and systems that can access, verify, and integrate this information across clinical workflows. In this Review, we examine more than 200 publications through July 2026. We organize the literature around volumetric representation and multimodal understanding at the model level, agentic orchestration at the system level, and their links to clinical applications and evaluation. We review volumetric foundation models, language alignment and compression strategies, and agentic systems that extend MLLMs through planning, tools, memory, and workflow interaction. We distinguish settings in which selected 2D views or report-mediated reasoning may suffice from those that warrant native volumetric modeling. We also introduce a Claim-Design-Validation framework to assess whether technical, workflow, and clinical claims are matched by appropriate design and validation. Across the literature, native volumetric modeling and agentic capabilities depend on the spatial, quantitative, contextual, and workflow requirements of the intended task. Clinical credibility requires faithful volumetric representation, traceable system behavior, claim-aligned validation, and clearly defined human oversight in realistic workflows.

</details>


### [204] [Consilience: Conformally Calibrated Communication Control for Hidden-Profile Multi-Agent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20564)
*Abhijith Babu,Ramneet Kaur,Vishal Pramanik,Olivera Kotevska,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Susmit Jha,Sunny Raj,Yanzhao Wu,Sumit Kumar Jha,Anirban Roy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Consilience框架，通过推理时的编排与逐轮共形校准，为多智能体LLM在隐藏信息场景下的通信提供自适应控制与可证明的决策保障，显著提升决策准确率和通信效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统通过整合多样视角可提升推理能力，但在隐藏信息场景（hidden-profile）下，每个智能体仅持有部分证据，现有通信协议（固定调度、轮询交换、非结构化辩论）无法保证对话动作的适当性，缺乏对通信行为的可靠控制与保证。

Method: Consilience框架包含两部分：(1) 状态压缩模块，通过紧凑状态捕获不确定性、分歧、证据增益、冗余与过早共识等信息；(2) 通信干预选择模块，在每轮中选择通信动作（质疑、澄清、寻求证据或路由）及说话的智能体。核心贡献是逐轮共形校准（round-wise conformal calibration）程序，提供无分布假设的有限样本保证——在每轮对话中，控制器动作的单步遗憾以至少1-α的边际概率被校准阈值约束；接受机制通过替换不可接受的提议，确保实际执行动作同样满足该保证。

Result: 在HiddenBench风格的隐藏信息任务上，对12个开源和闭源语言模型进行评估，Consilience在决策准确率和通信效率上均优于固定与无结构讨论协议，有时甚至超过所有智能体都观察全部证据的全信息基线。

Conclusion: 认证的自适应通信控制可能比增加信息可用性更有价值，为可靠的多智能体LLM协调提供了实用机制。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems can improve reasoning by pooling diverse perspectives, but their effectiveness depends on coordinating communication, particularly in hidden-profile settings where each agent holds only part of the evidence required for a correct decision. Existing protocols, including fixed schedules, round-robin exchange, and unstructured debate, provide no guarantee that a conversational action is appropriate. We propose Consilience, an inference-time orchestration framework that both steers and certifies multi-agent communication under distributed private information. At each turn, Consilience summarizes the discussion using a compact state capturing uncertainty, disagreement, evidence gain, redundancy, and premature consensus, then selects both a communication intervention (challenge, clarify, seek evidence, or route) and an appropriate speaker. Its central contribution is a round-wise conformal calibration procedure that provides a distribution-free, finite-sample guarantee: at each discussion round, conditional on reaching that round, the one-step regret of a controller's proposed action is bounded by a calibrated threshold with marginal probability at least 1 - alpha; an acceptance mechanism enforces the same guarantee for the executed action by replacing inadmissible proposals. On HiddenBench-style hidden-profile tasks spanning 12 open and closed weight language models, Consilience improves decision accuracy and communication efficiency over fixed and unstructured discussion protocols, sometimes surpassing a full-information baseline where every agent observes all evidence. These results demonstrate that certified adaptive communication control can be more valuable than increasing information availability, providing a practical mechanism for reliable multi-agent LLM coordination.

</details>


### [205] [Open-Weight Masked Introspection: Measuring What Language Models Can Report About Their Own Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20569)
*Emilio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文测试了八个开源权重模型（来自七个模型家族）的"内省"能力——即模型能否准确报告其内部计算是否被篡改。通过构建Open-Weight Masked Introspection (OWMI)框架，在超过78,000次测量中发现：所有模型的自我报告在区分真实干预与虚假干预时均不优于随机猜测（AUROC约0.5007），等价性检验将效应量限制在AUROC的0.15个百分点以内。然而，所有必要信息其实都存在于模型内部——线性探针可从激活中恢复干预信息，准确率达75%-95.8%，最后层甚至达到零错误。问题出在内部状态到口头报告的通路上，而非模型内部信息缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究声称复杂模型在特定条件下能够审计自身内部状态并自信报告变化。作者希望验证这一主张是否成立，并系统性地检验前沿模型的内省能力，以回答"模型能否真正内省其内部状态"这一核心问题。

Method: 构建OWMI框架：(1) 在残差流位置、注意力头、稀疏自编码器特征等多个内部站点进行干预；(2) 设置三种严格的对照条件：无实际改变的虚假运行（sham）、影响匹配的随机扰动、仅观察可见输出的文本对照观察者；(3) 对八个开源权重模型进行超过78,000次测量，使用AUROC和等价性检验评估模型报告能否区分真实干预与虚假干预；(4) 用微调模型和线性探针验证内部信息是否可恢复。

Result: 所有模型的自我报告在区分真实干预与虚假干预上均不优于随机水平（AUROC约0.5007），等价性检验将效应限制在0.15个百分点内。但内部信息实际存在：微调模型在保留方向上接近完美恢复，线性探针在激活上达到75%-95.8%准确率，最后层达到零错误。一个模型中信号出现在置信度而非言语内容中（AUROC 0.647）。

Conclusion: 当前开源权重模型不具备可靠的内省能力，失败发生在从内部状态到语言报告的转换路径中。这提示依赖模型自我证词的监督机制需要内部参照进行验证。作者同时指出，该结论仅针对当前模型，未来模型的内省能力仍是开放问题。

Abstract: Are frontier models able to introspect about their internal states? Recent work suggests that under certain conditions a complex enough model can audit its own internals, call out what changed, and report back confidently about it. We tested that claim on eight open-weight models from seven families and found no such ability: asked whether their own computation had been altered, none answered better than chance. To test it we built Open-Weight Masked Introspection (OWMI), a framework that intervenes on residual-stream sites, attention heads and sparse-autoencoder features, then interrogates the model about the change against the null conditions an answer has to beat: sham runs where nothing was altered, impact-matched random perturbations, and a text-only observer that sees only the visible output.
  Over 78,000 measurements, no model's report discriminates a real intervention from a sham beyond chance (AUROC ~0.5007), and an equivalence test bounds the effect below 0.15 percentage points of AUROC. Surprisingly, all the information needed is in the models. A model fine-tuned to report this class of intervention reaches near-perfect recovery on held-out directions, and a linear probe recovers intervention presence from the same activations at 75% to 95.8% accuracy, sharpening to no held-out error at the last layer before the model speaks. In one model the signal surfaces in the confidence rather than the words: its yes-or-no report never varies, while the confidence attached to it separates intervention from sham at AUROC 0.647. The failure sits in the path from internal state to verbal report, so oversight that reads a model's own testimony needs validating against an internal reference.
  While our results show the inability of current open-weight models to introspect, the debate is not settled for future models.

</details>


### [206] [FlavourBench: Ranking Frontier Language Models with Executable Culinary Ground Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20574)
*Josef Chen,Erim Hayretci*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlavourBench 是一个自动化基准测试平台，为开放式的烹饪组合任务提供密集的可执行地面真值，消除了传统语言模型评测中评判者偏差和样本缺失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的开放式语言模型基准测试通常依赖人类偏好面板、模型评判者或精确匹配键作为评判标准，这些方法存在主观性、脆弱性和不一致性。作者希望构建一个可执行、可验证且无缺失数据的自动化基准。

Method: FlavourBench 由版本化的烹饪系统提供密集的可执行地面真值。每个任务给出8种食材，要求输出3种食材组合（共56种可能性），由Epicure在模型执行前对所有组合评分。在534个任务核心集上评估27个前沿模型端点，每位排名模型在每个面板和家族中恰好有89个有效响应（共14,418个模型-任务单元格），消除不可比缺失。使用50,000次锚点聚类自助复制生成同步95%分数带，100,000次符号翻转抽样用于351对模型对比（Holm校正）。

Result: 两个独立编译的面板相关系数 r = 0.89（秩相关rho = 0.80）。Grok 4.6 取得最大点估计值65.1（同步95% CI 61.0-69.2）；351对模型对比中有101对得到显著区分。发布内容包含提示词、所有组合分数映射、原始响应、精确路径、内容哈希和离线验证器。
