  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抗疟疾虚拟筛选（VS）对资源高效、可靠的候选化合物识别有迫切需求。传统机器学习方法在结构挑战性条件下表现有限，而专有大规模推理模型成本高且难以微调。作者旨在探索开源LLM在经过针对性微调后，能否在疟疾虚拟筛选任务中同时超越经典ML管线和专有推理模型，从而提供一种资源高效的新范式。

Method: 作者从ChEMBL Legacy Malaria语料库构建了Malaria-Instruct指令微调数据集；在严格的分布外（out-of-distribution）数据划分下，对五个开源LLM（Gemma-2 2B/9B、TxGemma-2B/9B、LlaSMol-Mistral-7B）进行系统评估；以经典ML模型（RF、XGBoost）和专有模型（Gemini 2.5、OpenAI o3）在少样本条件下作为基线；采用ROC-AUC和EF@1%作为评价指标，并设计消融实验验证领域微调的必要性及不同预训练策略的影响。

Result: 微调后的开源LLM全面超越所有基线。TxGemma-9B达到最高ROC-AUC（0.731±0.005），LlaSMol-Mistral-7B取得最佳EF@1%（≈4.99）。消融实验显示，TxGemma-9B在最优少样本条件下微调后ROC-AUC从0.731跌至0.499（未微调），证实微调不可或缺；Gemini 2.5（≈0.53）和o3（≈0.59）在无微调时均无法实现可靠区分。生物医学预训练（TxGemma）在同等规模下优于通用预训练（Gemma），而化学感知预训练（LlaSMol）在富集效果上表现更优。

Conclusion: 微调后的开源LLM为抗疟疾虚拟筛选提供了一种有说服力且资源高效的新范式，在结构挑战性条件下同时超越经典ML管线和专有推理模型。领域特定微调是被证实不可或缺的关键环节，预训练策略的选择（生物医学或化学感知）应依据具体评估维度（判别能力或富集效果）而决定。

Abstract: We introduce Malaria-Instruct, a curated instruction-following dataset derived from the ChEMBL Legacy Malaria corpus for Malaria virtual screening, and conduct a systematic evaluation of five open-source LLMs; Gemma-2 2B/9B, TxGemma-2B/9B, and LlaSMol-Mistral-7B, on a rigorous out-of-distribution data split. Performance was benchmarked against classical ML models (Random Forest, XGBoost) and frontier proprietary models (Gemini 2.5, OpenAI o3) under few-shot conditions. Fine-tuned LLMs substantially outperformed all baselines: TxGemma-9B achieved the highest ROC-AUC ($0.731 \pm 0.005$) and LlaSMol-Mistral-7B the best enrichment factor (EF@1\% $\approx$ 4.99). Domain-specific fine-tuning proved categorically indispensable with TxGemma-9B collapsing from ROC-AUC 0.731 to 0.499, under its best few-shot condition, and neither Gemini 2.5 (ROC-AUC $\approx$ 0.53) nor o3 (ROC-AUC $\approx$ 0.59) achieved reliable discrimination without fine-tuning. Biomedical pretraining conferred a measurable advantage at equivalent scale, while chemistry-aware pretraining yielded superior prospective enrichment. Fine-tuned open-source LLMs represent a compelling, resource-efficient paradigm for antimalarial VS, outperforming both classical pipelines and proprietary reasoning models under structurally challenging conditions.

</details>


### [272] [PerturbRx: Learning Treatment-Conditioned Latent Transitions for Patient Drug Response Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21349)
*Yoshitaka Inoue,Minoh Jeong,Alfred Hero,Rui Kuang,Augustin Luna*

Main category: q-bio.QM

TL;DR: PerturbRx通过与细胞配对无关但上下文匹配的对照和药物处理单细胞群体，学习药物和剂量条件化的潜空间转变预测器，并将其迁移到预处理患者的特征上，从而预测癌症治疗反应，在TCGA和患者来源异种移植基准上取得了最优综合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀缺的数据和肿瘤异质性限制了患者层面的癌症治疗反应预测。现有方法仅从预处理分子谱和药物表征预测反应，未显式建模治疗条件下预期的分子变化，从而无法充分利用干预引起的生物学信息。

Method: PerturbRx是一个治疗条件化表示学习框架，核心包括：(1) 从上下文匹配但细胞未配对的对照与处理单细胞群体中训练一个药物和剂量条件化的转变预测器，学习干预引起的潜空间转变；(2) 冻结该预测器并将其迁移到预处理患者图谱上，无需治疗后测量；(3) 将潜转变与患者和药物表示结合以预测反应。

Result: 在TCGA和患者来源异种移植基准上，PerturbRx在所有评估方法中取得了最强的综合预测性能。

Conclusion: 扰动预训练的潜空间转变可作为患者层面药物反应预测的有效表示，为利用单细胞扰动数据增强临床预测提供了新思路。

Abstract: Scarce data and tumor heterogeneity limit patient-level cancer treatment-response prediction. Existing approaches predict response from pretreatment molecular profiles and drug representations, without explicitly modeling the molecular changes expected under treatment. We propose PerturbRx, a treatment-conditioned representation learning framework that learns intervention-induced latent transitions and uses them as patient-drug response features. PerturbRx trains a drug- and dose-conditioned transition predictor from context-matched but cell-unpaired control and treated single-cell populations, then freezes and transfers the predictor to pretreatment patient profiles without requiring post-treatment measurements. The transition is combined with patient and drug representations to predict response. Across TCGA and patient-derived xenograft benchmarks, PerturbRx achieves the strongest aggregate predictive performance among the evaluated methods. These results support perturbation-pretrained latent transitions as useful representations for patient-level drug-response prediction.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [273] [AEGIS: Preventing Cross-Domain Resource Abuse in MCP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20481)
*Shriti Priya,Teryl Taylor,Frederico Araujo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出AEGIS，一个针对MCP（Model Context Protocol）工具的策略执行组件，利用LLM的推理能力来分析、分类和归一化异构工具调用，通过集成Open Policy Agent和ContextForge AI Gateway来检测和缓解资源滥用行为，防止恶意主体利用MCP工具造成拒绝服务等服务质量下降问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP作为标准化LLM与外部系统交互的开放协议，其工具可被攻击者或恶意代理利用（如请求过大的搜索半径或过长的视频），导致后端系统过载和拒绝服务。不同模态（文本、图像、视频、位置）和跨域工具带来多样化的请求模式和参数，使得制定既通用又精确的策略变得困难。

Method: AEGIS的核心方法是利用LLM的推理能力对异构MCP工具调用进行分析、分类和归一化，将其转换为统一的、策略友好的表示形式，供安全从业人员使用。同时与开源策略引擎Open Policy Agent和ContextForge AI Gateway集成，实现策略制定与执行。

Result: AEGIS能够检测和缓解跨异构MCP工具和模态的滥用行为，同时保留MCP代理生态系统的灵活性。

Conclusion: AEGIS为基于MCP的代理应用提供了一种细粒度的资源滥用防护方案，兼顾安全性与生态灵活性，为多模态MCP工具环境下的策略执行提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) is an open source JSON-RPC protocol that standardizes how large language models (LLMs) interact with external systems through programmatic functions known as tools. Attackers or malicious agents can exploit certain modalities of these MCP tools to degrade the overall quality of service of agent-based applications. For example, an agent may request an excessively large search radius or very long videos, overloading backend systems and potentially causing slowdowns or denial-of-service. Each modality including text, images, video, and location introduces distinct vectors for resource abuse, complicating the development of consistent mitigation strategies. Moreover, multimodal and crossdomain tools expose diverse request schemas and parameters, making it difficult to define policies that are both generalizable and precise enough to enforce meaningful resource constraints. In this paper, we present AEGIS, a policy enforcement component that enables administrators to define fine-grained safeguards against resource abuse across heterogeneous MCP tools and modalities. AEGIS leverages the reasoning capabilities of large language models to analyze, categorize, and normalize diverse tool invocations into a unified, policy-friendly representation accessible to security practitioners. Integrated with the Open Policy Agent and the ContextForge AI Gateway, AEGIS detects and mitigates abusive behaviors while preserving the flexibility of MCP-based agent ecosystems.

</details>


### [274] [aiXamine: Unified Black-Box Evaluation of Cross-Dimensional Trade-offs in LLM Safety, Security, and Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20554)
*Fatih Deniz,Yazan Boshmaf,Dorde Popovic,Issa Khalil*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: aiXamine是一个统一的黑盒平台，通过46项测试和9种服务，对超过120个LLM进行安全性、安全性和隐私性的联合评估，揭示了三种单维度评估无法发现的跨维度现象：安全税（更强的对齐导致过度拒绝）、隐私与其他维度近乎正交、以及蒸馏诱导的鲁棒性崩溃（56.9→2.6）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估框架将安全性、安全性和隐私性独立评估，无法检测部署LLM中的跨维度失效模式，例如模型在安全对齐得分99.3的同时拒绝三分之一的良性查询，或在所有能力指标提升的同时隐私得分下降21点。

Method: aiXamine通过自动化红队测试流水线，编排46项测试跨越9种服务，生成从提示级别诊断到跨服务权衡分析的分层风险配置，在相同条件下对专有和开源模型进行可复现的对比评估。

Result: 对120多个LLM进行5000多次测试运行，进行了迄今最大规模的联合安全、安全和隐私研究。发现了三个跨维度现象：安全对齐的系统性增加过度拒绝（安全税）；隐私与其他信任维度近乎正交；离线策略蒸馏导致熵崩溃从而灾难性破坏鲁棒性（56.9→2.6）。此外还发现规模收益递减和类别依赖的安全行为。

Conclusion: 信任本质上是多维的：一个维度上的进展不能保证其他维度上的进展，甚至可能主动破坏其他维度，而当前的对齐方法将其视为单一目标来处理，这是根本性的缺陷。

Abstract: The critical failure modes in deployed large language models (LLMs) are cross-dimensional: a model can score 99.3 in safety alignment while refusing one in three benign queries, or improve across every capability metric while losing 21 points in privacy. Existing evaluation frameworks that assess safety, security, and privacy independently cannot detect these patterns. We introduce aiXamine, a unified black-box platform that evaluates LLM trustworthiness across safety, security, and privacy as interdependent properties. aiXamine orchestrates 46 tests across nine services through an automated red-teaming pipeline, producing hierarchical risk profiles, from prompt-level diagnostics to cross-service trade-off analytics, that enable reproducible comparison of proprietary and open-weight systems under identical conditions. Applying aiXamine to over 120 LLMs through more than 5,000 test runs, we conduct the largest joint safety, security, and privacy study to date and uncover three cross-dimensional phenomena invisible to single-axis evaluation. First, safety enforcement incurs a quantifiable safety tax: stronger alignment systematically increases over-refusal, forcing providers to choose between protection and utility. Second, privacy is near-orthogonal to other trustworthiness dimensions and not captured by standard alignment. Third, we identify and formally characterize distillation-induced robustness collapse: off-policy distillation without on-policy correction causes entropy collapse, catastrophically destroying robustness (56.9$\to$2.6) on the same base architecture. These findings, compounded by diminishing returns from scale and category-dependent safety behaviors, demonstrate that trustworthiness is inherently multi-dimensional: progress along one axis does not guarantee, and can actively undermine, progress along others, yet current alignment methods treat it as a single objective.

</details>


### [275] [Beyond End-to-End Success: Diagnosing Failures in Long-Horizon Security LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20563)
*Wei Shao,Chongzhou Fang,Zuxiong Tan,Zequan Liang,Setareh Rafatirad,Avesta Sasan,Houman Homayoun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种诊断安全领域长时程LLM智能体失败原因的方法论：通过设置检查点、区分能力暴露前后的失败、并利用受控干预测试上游瓶颈。在四个任务族上的评估表明，模型代际间失败主因会显著转变，提示评估应关注失败的位置与原因而非仅看整体成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程安全LLM智能体依靠早期发现的信息、状态或访问权限进行后续决策，导致最终任务成功难以解释——智能体可能在能力被真正检验之前就失败。单纯依赖总体任务成功率会掩盖失败的真实位置和原因。

Method: 提出一种诊断式评估方法论：(1) 在安全任务中植入检查点（checkpoints）；(2) 区分能力暴露之前与之后的失败；(3) 使用受控干预（controlled interventions）测试疑似上游瓶颈。横跨四个任务族：延迟复用已发现信息、复用观察到的状态、从失败策略中恢复、以及在不确定结果后做决策。

Result: 在状态复用任务上，检查点分析显示Gemini 2.5 Flash的多数失败发生在模型尚未观察到待复用状态之前。在预注册的92个种子研究中，针对性的协议消歧指导将状态观察率从对照组的65.5%提升到95.4%。但同样的实验设计在Gemini 3.7 Flash上产生相反效果，且状态观察不再可靠预测任务完成。

Conclusion: 不同模型代际的失败主导来源可能发生显著迁移，这强烈支持采用诊断式评估方法——定位和解释长时程安全智能体在何处以及为何失败，而非仅依赖聚合的任务成功指标。

Abstract: Long-horizon security LLM agents must carry information and decisions across many dependent interactions, where later actions often depend on services, state, or access discovered much earlier. This makes final task success difficult to interpret: an agent may fail before it ever reaches the point where the capability of interest can be exercised. We present a diagnostic methodology that instruments security tasks with checkpoints, separates failures before and after capability exposure, and uses controlled interventions to test suspected upstream bottlenecks. We evaluate the methodology across four task families involving delayed reuse of discovered information, reuse of observed state, recovery from failed strategies, and decision making after uncertain outcomes. On observed state reuse, checkpoint analysis shows that many Gemini 2.5 Flash failures occur before the model observes the state it is later expected to reuse. In a pre-specified 92-seed study, targeted protocol-disambiguation guidance increases state observation from 65.5\% under a matched non-guidance control message to 95.4\%. Repeating the same design with Gemini 3.7 Flash produces the opposite effect, while state observation no longer reliably predicts task completion. These results show that the dominant source of failure can shift across model generations, motivating evaluation that diagnoses where and why long-horizon security agents fail rather than relying only on aggregate task success.

</details>


### [276] [Temporal Risk on Satellites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20575)
*Shiqi Liu,Kun Sun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一个将时间作为显式维度的卫星安全风险评估框架，通过时间索引的似然-影响矩阵取代单一风险评分，帮助运营商识别高风险时段并优化防御资源调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星漏洞会随轨道漂移、功率裕度下降和空间环境恶化而随时间变化，但现有网络安全风险框架仍将威胁视为静态的。同一漏洞在关键机动期间的破坏性远大于日常操作，因此需要将时间纳入风险评估。

Method: 扩展现有的对手行为分类法，提出一个五维时间能力模型，并在任务的不同时间窗口内估计利用难度。将任务离散化为有操作意义的时间窗口和环境带，输出一系列时间索引的似然-影响矩阵而非单一风险评分。

Result: 框架能够展示系统在不同时间窗口的暴露程度，帮助运营商避免在高风险期安排敏感操作，并根据随时间变化的威胁态势调整防御资源部署。

Conclusion: 该时间感知的风险评估框架使卫星安全分析从静态评估转向动态、时间序列化的评估，为卫星运营商提供了更具操作指导性的安全决策依据。

Abstract: Satellite vulnerabilities change over time as orbits shift, power margins tighten, and the space environment deteriorates. However, most cybersecurity risk frameworks still treat threats as static. In practice, the same exploit can be far more damaging during a critical maneuver than during routine operations. We propose a temporal risk assessment framework that makes time an explicit axis in satellite security analysis. It extends existing adversary behavior taxonomies with a five-dimensional temporal capability model and estimates exploitation difficulty across distinct temporal windows of a mission. Rather than producing a single risk score, the framework outputs a series of time-indexed likelihood-impact matrices. It discretizes missions into operationally meaningful time windows and environmental bands to show when systems are most exposed. This view helps operators avoid scheduling sensitive operations in high-risk periods and align defensive resources with a threat landscape that shifts over time.

</details>


### [277] [MATEE: Efficiently Bridging the Semantic Gap in TrustZone via Arm Pointer Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20583)
*Shiqi Liu,Xiang Li,Jie Wang,Yongpeng Gao,Jiajin Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了MATEE系统，利用Arm Pointer Authentication机制来解决TrustZone TEE中secure world与normal world之间的语义鸿沟漏洞（SGVs），通过将每个请求与CA身份绑定并验证，防止恶意CA伪造请求窃取其他CA的敏感数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Arm TrustZone将系统划分为secure和normal两个世界，但secure world对normal world的运行信息可见性有限，导致语义鸿沟。secure world在接收normal world数据请求时缺乏有效的用户身份认证，恶意CA可利用精心构造的请求参数欺骗secure world中的TA，从而危害其他CA的敏感数据。

Method: MATEE系统：1）系统性地分类Semantic Gap Vulnerabilities（SGVs）；2）利用Arm Pointer Authentication（PA）将每个请求绑定到对应CA的身份；3）在CA访问敏感数据时验证身份，防止恶意请求伪造；4）在不修改现有CA和TA的前提下隔离不同CA的敏感数据。

Result: 实验评估表明MATEE成功防御了SGVs，且仅引入了极小的运行时开销（2.19%）。

Conclusion: MATEE为TEE语义鸿沟问题提供了有效的解决方案，通过硬件辅助的身份绑定机制在不影响现有应用的前提下增强了TEE的安全性，且开销可忽略不计。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) employ hardware-based isolation mechanisms to safeguard the confidentiality and integrity of sensitive code and data. One such prevalent implementation is Arm TrustZone, which partitions the system into the secure and normal (non-secure) worlds. However, this partitioning results in the secure world having very limited visibility into the operating information of the normal world, creating a semantic gap between these two worlds. Specifically, the secure world lacks an effective user identity authentication when receiving data requests from the normal world. Consequently, malicious Client Applications (CAs) in the normal world can deceive Trusted Applications (TAs) in the secure world by utilizing elaborate request parameters, compromising the sensitive data stored by other CAs. We systematically classify these Semantic Gap Vulnerabilities (SGVs) and propose a mate system for the TEE called MATEE to defend against SGVs. MATEE utilizes Arm Pointer Authentication (PA) to bind each request to the corresponding CA's identity and then verifies the identity when the CA accesses sensitive data, thereby preventing malicious request forgery. In particular, MATEE isolates sensitive data of different CAs without modifying existing CAs and TAs. Our evaluation demonstrates that MATEE successfully defends against SGVs with a minimal runtime overhead (2.19%).

</details>


### [278] [More Granular, Less Trust: Enforcing Intra-Process Isolation with Arm CCA in an Untrusted Management Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20584)
*Shiqi Liu,Zhouqi Jiang,Jie Wang,Wei Zhou,Kun Sun,Zhaohui Chen,Yulai Xie*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CCAegis，一种基于Arm CCA架构的进程内隔离系统，通过静态分析追踪敏感数据流，在函数调用点插入权限切换指令，利用GPT配置实现敏感数据与操作的进程内隔离，抵御来自OS和进程内对手的权限攻击。系统仅在Secure Monitor中配置信任，TCB极小，在真实工作负载下性能开销为1.01x-1.43x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机密计算环境下，CVM中的安全敏感应用仍面临来自OS的特权攻击。现有细粒度隔离方案（如SHELTER）提供进程级保护，但无法抵御进程内攻击以及OS与进程内对手的合谋。当前多数进程内隔离技术仍依赖OS管理和执行隔离域，导致TCB过大。因此需要更细粒度、更少信任依赖的机密计算解决方案。

Method: CCAegis扩展了Arm CCA架构以实现进程内隔离。具体包括：(1) 采用静态分析追踪敏感数据流并识别处理敏感数据的函数；(2) 在函数调用和返回点插入权限切换指令；(3) 通过Granule Protection Table (GPT)调整权限，确保仅指定函数可访问隔离数据；信任仅放置在配置GPT和管理域切换的Secure Monitor中。

Result: 在官方模拟器和真实开发板上实现并评估了CCAegis。实验结果表明，CCAegis能有效隔离敏感数据和操作，在真实密码学工作负载下，相比原始版本性能开销在1.01x至1.43x范围内。

Conclusion: CCAegis通过将信任仅置于Secure Monitor，显著缩小了TCB，同时实现了进程内级别的敏感数据隔离，能够同时抵御OS和进程内对手的攻击，且性能开销可接受。

Abstract: With the increasing adoption of confidential computing, security-sensitive applications are often deployed in confidential virtual machines (CVMs), which reduce reliance on third-party cloud providers. However, privilege attacks originating from the OS remain a significant threat in these environments. Existing finer-grained isolation schemes, such as SHELTER, provide process-level protection but are still vulnerable to intraprocess attacks and potential collusion between the OS and intra-process adversaries. Many current intra-process isolation techniques continue to depend on the OS to manage and enforce isolation domains, leading to a large Trusted Computing Base (TCB). This gap highlights the need for more granular, less trust-dependent confidential computing solutions. In this paper, we present CCAegis, a system that extends the Arm Confidential Compute Architecture (CCA) to enforce intra-process isolation of sensitive data and operations, safeguarding them from both intraprocess adversaries and the OS. We employ static analysis to track the flow of sensitive data and identify functions that handle such data. Permission-switching instructions are inserted at the function call and return points, adjusting permissions via the Granule Protection Table (GPT) to ensure that only designated functions can access the isolated data. Notably, CCAegis places trust solely in the Secure Monitor, which configures the GPTs and manages domain switching, thereby minimizing the TCB. We implemented CCAegis on both an official emulator and a real development board to assess its performance. Our experimental results show that CCAegis effectively isolates sensitive data and operations, with performance overheads ranging from 1.01x to 1.43x compared to the original version across real-world cryptographic workloads.

</details>


### [279] [ARQ: Agentic CodeQL Query Refinement for C/C++ Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20637)
*Chunyi Wang,Yunfei Ke,Junfeng Yang,Yun-Yun Tsai,Penghui Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ARQ是一个智能体框架，通过从合成C/C++程序中获取执行证据来自动优化CodeQL查询。核心思路是：合成程序的执行结果与查询判定不一致时暴露查询缺陷——真漏洞未报警说明有假阴性，安全代码误报说明有假阳性。ARQ不需要标注数据集、提交历史或漏洞模板，仅依赖LM驱动的迭代精化循环。在12个官方CodeQL查询上用三种商业LM验证，相比原始查询真正例最多提升119.8%，精度始终不低于98.0%，修复了三个悬置27个月的官方GitHub问题，并发现了libpng和zlib中的两个未知漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于查询的静态分析器（如CodeQL）在C/C++漏洞检测中被广泛使用，但现有查询仍存在较高的假阳性和假阴性。之前的查询精化方法依赖标注数据集、提交历史或特定漏洞模板，这些资源往往稀缺或不可用。因此需要一种无需这些外部依赖、能自动发现并修复查询缺陷的通用方法。

Method: ARQ包含两个关键环节：1) 执行验证：合成C/C++程序，将查询裁决与程序实际执行结果对比，当两者不一致时（查询判定漏洞但程序安全→FP，查询沉默但程序漏洞→FN），该合成程序即作为暴露查询弱点的证据；2) LLM精化循环：利用这些执行不一致作为ground truth，驱动大语言模型迭代修复查询。整个过程无需标注数据集、提交历史或漏洞模板。

Result: 在Juliet v1.3和FormAI v2数据集上，ARQ精化后的12个官方CodeQL查询相比原始查询真正例检测数量提升最高119.8%，精度在所有实验中始终不低于98.0%。ARQ修复了官方CodeQL仓库中三个悬置长达27个月的GitHub问题，并在真实世界库libpng和zlib中发现了两个此前未知的漏洞。

Conclusion: ARQ证明了"执行grountruth与查询裁决不一致"这一信号可以有效地作为无监督查询精化的驱动力，从根本上摆脱了对标注数据、提交历史和漏洞模板的依赖。该方法不仅在基准数据集上显著提升查询的查全率且保持高精度，还能修复实际开源项目中的长期缺陷并发现真实世界漏洞，展示了其实际部署价值。

Abstract: Static analyzers have been widely adopted for vulnerability detection in C/C++ programs. Query-based static analyzers (e.g., CodeQL) encode vulnerable code patterns in detection queries and match them against source code. However, existing queries still suffer from false positives (FPs, incorrectly flagging benign code as vulnerable) and false negatives (FNs, missing real vulnerabilities). We present ARQ, an agentic framework that automatically refines C/C++ CodeQL queries using execution-grounded evidence from synthesized C/C++ programs. Our key insight is that a synthesized program exposes a query's weakness whenever its execution disagrees with the query's verdict. If the program is genuinely vulnerable but the query stays silent, the query has an FN weakness; if the program is safe but the query fires anyway, it has an FP weakness. ARQ then runs an LLM-based refinement loop that repairs the query using these disagreements as ground truth. Unlike previous query refining methods, ARQ requires no labeled datasets, no commit history, and no vulnerability-specific templates. We demonstrate the effectiveness of ARQ by refining 12 official CodeQL queries using three commercial LLMs (GPT-5.4, Claude-Sonnet-4.6, and Gemini-3.5-flash). We compare both ARQ-refined and original CodeQL queries on the Juliet v1.3 and FormAI v2 datasets and show that ARQ-refined queries detect substantially more true positives, by up to 119.8\%, with a Precision of at least 98.0\% throughout. ARQ successfully fixed three unresolved GitHub issues raised in the official CodeQL query repository that had remained open for as long as \textit{27 months}. The refined queries also exposed two previously undiscovered bugs in the real-world libraries libpng and zlib.

</details>


### [280] [The Claws in Plain Sight: Unauthorized Context Disclosure through LLM Agent Tool Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20658)
*Ben Dong,Zhonghao Guo,Tianyi Lu,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"Claw in Plain Sight"攻击：LLM智能体在构造工具调用参数时，通过将受保护属性伪装为任务相关或操作必需的内容，诱导模型在合法生成的参数中泄露敏感信息。在包含六个压力级别、四个隐私策略级别和五个DeepSeek/Claude模型配置的合成基准上评估，会话级泄露率在20.8%到75.0%之间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在从用户画像、对话历史、检索文档和先前工具结果中构造工具调用参数时，对上下文的合法访问并不意味着对所有用途和目的都有传输授权。现有的提示级隐私策略无法提供可移植的执行边界，需要在工具参数执行前进行目的和目的地感知的检查。

Method: 提出一种"权威压力"（authority-pressure）攻击方法，通过将受保护属性框架化为操作性或程序性必需内容，诱导模型将其包含在合法生成的工具参数中。使用控制变量合成的基准测试，交叉组合六个压力级别和四个隐私策略级别，对五个DeepSeek和Claude模型配置进行了共计120次调用评估。

Result: 在完整的压力-策略矩阵中，会话级泄露率在所有测试模型中范围为20.8%至75.0%。更强的隐私指令虽能减少总体泄露，但无法在所有模型中一致地消除泄露，表明提示级策略不能提供可移植的执行边界。实验仅使用合成画像并在本地捕获所提议的参数，测量的是上下文到参数边界处的策略违规生成，而非实际网络外泄。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持在工具参数执行前进行目的和目的地感知的检查。提示级隐私策略不足以作为可移植的安全边界，需要更系统性的防护措施来防止上下文到工具参数边界的敏感信息泄露。

Abstract: LLM agents routinely construct tool-call arguments from user profiles, conversation history, retrieved documents, and prior tool results. However, legitimate access to contextual information does not imply authorization to transmit that information for every purpose or destination. We present Claw in Plain Sight, an authority- pressure attack in which task-adjacent content frames protected attributes as operationally or procedurally required, causing a model to include them in otherwise valid generated arguments. We evaluate Claw in Plain Sight using a controlled synthetic benchmark that crosses six pressure levels with four privacy-policy levels across five DeepSeek and Claude model configurations, producing 120 calls. Across the complete pressure-policy matrix, session-level disclosure rates range from 20.8% to 75.0% among the tested models. Stronger privacy instructions reduce aggregate disclosure but do not eliminate it consistently across models, showing that prompt-level policies do not provide a portable enforcement boundary. Our experiments use only synthetic profiles and capture proposed arguments locally; they measure policy-violating generation at the context-to-argument boundary, not completed network exfiltration or leakage from deployed users. These findings motivate purpose- and destination-aware inspection of generated tool arguments before execution.

</details>


### [281] [Bootstrapping Mutual Attestation with Kleene's Second Recursion Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20671)
*Takuma Imamura*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出并解决了互证（mutual attestation）中的参考值引导（reference-value bootstrapping）问题，通过Kleene第二递归定理将无限回归表达为互斥不动点方程组，使节点无需可信第三方或外部参考值即可重构对等节点的源码并派生参考测量值。作者实现了PyReflect（Python转译器）和NixReflect（Nix转译器）两个原型，分别用于TPM互证PoC和AWS Nitro Enclaves互证PoC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无中心可信操作者的网络中，节点间互证需要每个节点持有对等节点的参考值（期望代码测量值）。朴素地将这些参考值互相嵌入节点代码会导致无限回归（infinite regress）。现有方案依赖可信第三方、外部提供的参考值或架构特定的测量机制，但这些都会引入新的信任假设或限制通用性。论文旨在消除这些依赖，使互证在无中心信任的前提下自然成立。

Method: 作者将参考值引导问题形式化为互斥不动点方程组，并利用Kleene第二递归定理进行求解，构造出可互相引用对方代码并仅从内建数据重构每个对等节点完整源码的节点。对于直接测量部署的源文件（如Python脚本），节点通过对重构源码直接应用测量函数获得参考值；对于测量构建镜像（如AWS Nitro Enclaves），节点通过可复现构建从重构源码重建对等镜像并派生参考测量值。具体实现包括PyReflect（Python源码转译器，用于TPM互证PoC）和NixReflect（Nix转译器，用于两个Nitro Enclaves互相复现对方参考PCR值的PoC）。

Result: 论文实现了两个工作原型：PyReflect驱动的TPM互证PoC，以及NixReflect驱动的Nitro Enclaves互证PoC（两个Enclaves仅凭内建数据即可复现对方的参考PCR值）。该方案架构无关，不需要可信第三方或外部提供的参考值，并且可以与现有证明栈无缝兼容。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了通过递归不动点理论可以优雅地解决互证中的参考值引导问题，彻底消除了对TTP和外部参考源的依赖，为无中心信任环境下的安全互证提供了一种通用、架构无关的解决方案。

Abstract: Mutual attestation among nodes with no central trusted operator requires each node to hold reference values (expected code measurements) for its peers. The naïve approach of mutually embedding these reference values in the nodes' code leads to an infinite regress. We call the problem of resolving this infinite regress the reference-value bootstrapping problem for mutual attestation. Existing solutions avoid this regress by relying on a trusted third party (TTP), externally supplied reference values, or architecture-specific measurement mechanisms. We instead express the bootstrapping problem as a system of mutual fixed-point equations and solve it by Kleene's second recursion theorem. The construction produces nodes that mutually reference one another's code and reconstruct every peer's exact source from built-in data alone. When a deployed source file is measured directly, as with a Python script, a node obtains the peer's reference value by applying the measurement function directly to the reconstructed source. When a built image is measured, as with AWS Nitro Enclaves, a node instead reproducibly rebuilds the peer's image from the reconstructed source and derives its reference measurement. For the first case, we develop PyReflect, a Python transpiler, and use it to implement a TPM mutual-attestation PoC. For the second, we develop NixReflect, a Nix transpiler, and use it in a PoC in which two Nitro Enclaves reproduce each other's reference PCRs from built-in data alone. Our solution is architecture-independent, requires neither a TTP nor externally supplied reference values, and works with existing attestation stacks unchanged.

</details>


### [282] [The Rising Cost of Trust: Practitioners' Trust Signals, Controls, and Responses in the Software Supply Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20675)
*Ranindya Paramitha,Siri Paidipalli,Laurie Williams,Christian Kästner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究通过对38名从业者的半结构化访谈，以社会科学中的信任理论为基础进行主题分析，发现软件供应链中的信任正在侵蚀且成本日益上升，从业者通过自动化验证、委托信任决策给"守护者"或退出供应链来应对，为未来干预措施提供了信任视角的词汇和概念框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件供应链日益复杂，AI正在重塑其威胁格局（如AI生成依赖的质量问题）。从信任视角看，供应链信任侵蚀的后果严重，但目前缺乏关于从业者信任状况的实证基线，无法为从业者提供有依据的行动指导。

Method: 对38名行业及开源从业者进行半结构化访谈，专注于揭示其"显示偏好"（实际采用的控制措施）而非"陈述态度"，并使用扎根于社会科学信任理论的主题分析方法对数据进行编码和分析。

Result: 研究发现信任正在侵蚀且代价高昂：有意识的从业者正在积累各种控制措施。为应对不断上升的信任成本，从业者采取三种策略——自动化验证流程、将信任决策委托给守护者（guardians）、或考虑完全退出软件供应链。

Conclusion: 从信任视角理解软件供应链动态，提供了相关词汇和概念（如信任守护者、系统信任、信号）来塑造未来干预措施，以构建信任水平适当且良性运转的供应链。

Abstract: The software supply chain is becoming more complex, and AI is reshaping its threat landscape, e.g., raising concerns about the quality of AI-generated dependencies. Seen through the lens of trust, the stakes of eroding trust in the software supply chain are high, yet we lack an empirical baseline on practitioners' trust. The goal of this study is to aid software practitioners in taking informed actions as trust in the software supply chain evolves, through an interview study with 38 practitioners. We conducted semi-structured interviews with industry and open-source practitioners, focusing on their revealed preferences (the controls they adopted) rather than their stated attitudes, and analyzed the data using thematic analysis grounded in established trust concepts from the social sciences. We find that trust is eroding, which is becoming costly: aware practitioners are accumulating controls. To cope with the rising cost of trust, practitioners automate verification, delegate trust decisions to guardians, or consider exiting the software supply chain entirely. Understanding software supply chain dynamics through the lens of trust provides the vocabulary and concepts (e.g., guardians of trust, system trust, signals) to shape future interventions for a well-functioning supply chain with appropriate levels of trust.

</details>


### [283] [The Software Supply Chain as a Market for Lemons: A Multivocal Review of Trust Signal Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20678)
*Ranindya Paramitha,Christian Kästner,Laurie Williams*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过对252个Google搜索来源和870个Reddit帖子的多声道综述，研究了开源依赖采纳信任信号（如下载量、贡献者活动等）的可靠性，发现这些廉价信任信号在对抗性操纵、与合法行为难以区分的游戏行为、以及非对抗性的AI驱动膨胀三重力量下崩溃，且生态系统的应对主要是建议而非实际行动，最终指向"柠檬市场"问题，建议强制使用加密认证等成本更高的信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践者在评估开源依赖时依赖廉价信任信号（如星标、下载量、贡献者活动）作为直接代码审查的替代品，但先前工作仅记录了单一信号的游戏化行为，缺乏对整个依赖采纳信号崩溃全景及其生态系统响应的系统研究。

Method: 采用多声道综述（multivocal review）方法，对252个Google Search来源和870个Reddit帖子进行编码分析，系统梳理信任信号失效的机制及生态系统的应对方式。

Result: 发现三种同步力量导致廉价信号崩溃：对抗性操纵、与合法行为难以区分的游戏技术、以及非对抗性AI驱动的信号膨胀。生态系统响应以建议为主而非实际行动（54.6%的Google来源仅提供建议），提出的替代方案（信号替换或聚合）本身同样可被游戏化，且对非对抗性AI驱动的膨胀没有任何实际行为变化的记录。

Conclusion: 廉价信任信号的失效导致"柠檬市场"困境——当伪造信号成本低于获取真实信号时，好坏依赖难以区分。依赖个体实践者验证廉价信号不可持续，应强制推行加密认证等成本更高的信号，使其成为所有人的默认机制而非少数人的自愿选择。

Abstract: Practitioners evaluating open-source dependencies rely on cheap trust signals, e.g., stars, download counts, and contributor activity, as substitutes for direct code inspection, assuming those signals reflect genuine trustworthiness. Prior work has documented individual signal gaming, but the landscape of collapses across all dependency-adoption signals, as well as the ecosystem's response, remains unexplored. The goal of this study is to aid software practitioners in understanding the reliability of dependency adoption trust signals, such as download counts and contributor activity, by conducting a multivocal review of 252 Google Search sources and 870 Reddit threads. After coding the corpora, we find that cheap trust signals collapse under three simultaneous forces: adversarial manipulation, gaming techniques indistinguishable from legitimate behavior, and non-adversarial AI-driven inflation. The documented responses are more advice than actual action: 54.6% of Google Search sources contain advice on what practitioners should do, with no actual action taken. Responses proposed substituting one cheap signal for another or aggregating multiple signals, which are now also gameable. Non-adversarial inflation, i.e., degradation caused by the emergence of legitimate AI tooling, lacks documented actual behavior change in either corpus. The gap between known remedy and actual practice points toward a market for lemons: when faking signals costs less than earning them, good and bad dependencies become indistinguishable. Relying on individual practitioners to verify the cheap signals is not sustainable. Costlier signals, such as cryptographic attestation, should be made mandatory so that they become the default for all, not a voluntary choice for the few.

</details>


### [284] [Enabling Threshold Custody for the Lightning Network with Nested Threshold Multi-Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20705)
*Paul Gerhart,Nadav Kohen,Jesse Posner,Matias Furszyfer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了嵌套阈值多重签名（nested threshold multi-signatures）这一新原语，并给出了首个实例Iceberg，使得闪电网络通道一方可以以t-of-n阈值组的形式运行，而对外表现为标准MuSig2参与者，无需修改Bitcoin、闪电协议或通道对手方即可部署阈值托管。安全性和性能均得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闪电网络保护着数亿美元的资金，但通道端点依赖易受攻击的单一在线密钥。虽然阈值签名已常用于保护链上Bitcoin，但尚无实际部署可用于闪电通道，因为阈值化闪电通道参与者需要在既有的两方MuSig2协议内部嵌套阈值签名方案，同时不能改变其nonce交换或消息流程。

Method: 形式化定义嵌套阈值多重签名这一新型密码学原语，用于在多重签名协议中对一个参与者进行阈值化；作为该原语的实例，提出Iceberg构造——嵌套阈值MuSig2签名，使闪电通道一侧作为t-of-n阈值组运行，同时对外呈现为标准MuSig2参与者。

Result: 证明了Iceberg的安全性，将其原型集成到生产级闪电节点中并进行基准测试。测试表明，阈值化闪电通道仅产生适度开销：容忍一个腐败成员的阈值组仍能维持未修改端点的93%以上的支付吞吐量。

Conclusion: Iceberg首次实现了闪电通道的阈值托管，可在现有闪电网络上单方面部署，无需对Bitcoin、闪电协议或通道对手方做任何修改，且性能开销较小。

Abstract: The Bitcoin Lightning Network secures hundreds of millions of dollars, yet channel endpoints rely on vulnerable single online keys. Although threshold signatures are routinely used to protect on-chain Bitcoin, no practical deployment has been possible for Lightning channels. This is because thresholdizing a Lightning party requires nesting a threshold signature scheme inside of an established two-party MuSig2 protocol without altering its nonce exchange or message flow.
  In this work, we resolve this limitation by formalizing nested threshold multi-signatures, a new cryptographic primitive for thresholdizing one participant inside a multi-signature protocol. As an instance of this primitive, we present Iceberg, the first construction for nested threshold MuSig2 signatures. Iceberg enables one side of a Lightning channel to operate as a $t$-of-$n$ threshold group while appearing to the counterparty as a standard MuSig2 participant. As a result, threshold custody can be deployed unilaterally on today's Lightning Network without requiring any modifications to Bitcoin, the Lightning protocol, or channel counterparties.
  We prove the security of Iceberg, integrate a prototype into a production Lightning node, and benchmark its performance. Our measurements show that thresholdizing a Lightning channel incurs only modest overhead, since a threshold group tolerating one corrupted member sustains over $93\%$ of the payment throughput of an unmodified endpoint.

</details>


### [285] [Privacy-Preserving Object Detection for Vision Transformer-Based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20712)
*Homare Sueyoshi,Kiyoshi Nishikawa,Hitoshi Kiya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于感知加密的目标检测方法，利用Vision Transformer（ViT）的嵌入结构配合密钥域适应技术，在保护测试图像敏感视觉信息的同时保持与无保护模型几乎相同的检测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往涉及视觉信息保护的研究仅聚焦于图像分类任务，目标检测场景下的敏感视觉信息保护尚未被研究。本文首次将感知加密引入目标检测任务，以解决测试图像中敏感视觉内容泄露的问题。

Method: 利用Vision Transformer（ViT）的嵌入结构特性，结合基于密钥的域适应技术（domain adaptation technique with keys），对测试图像进行感知加密处理，在保持模型语义理解能力的同时实现对敏感视觉信息的保护。

Result: 在ViTdet（基于ViT的目标检测模型）上验证了方法的有效性，在精度方面与无保护模型几乎持平，同时在视觉保护方面表现良好。

Conclusion: 本文首次证明了感知加密可以成功应用于目标检测任务，通过ViT的嵌入结构和密钥域适应技术，能够在不显著损失检测精度的前提下有效保护测试图像的敏感视觉信息。

Abstract: We propose a novel object detection method that enables us to protect sensitive visual information of test images. Previous studies considering visual information protection focus on image classification tasks. This paper proposes an object detection method using perceptual encryption for the first time. The proposed method can achieve almost the same accuracy as that of models without any protection by utilizing the embedding structure of the Vision Transformer (ViT) and a domain adaptation technique with keys. In experiments, the effectiveness of the proposed method is verified in terms of accuracy and visual protection under the use of ViTdet, which is a ViT-based object detection model.

</details>


### [286] [Uncovering and Understanding Hidden Dependencies in the LLM API Reseller Ecosystem via Prefix-Cache Side Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20732)
*Zimo Ji,Xin Wei,Congying Xu,Wenyuan Jiang,Xin Yang,Zongjie Li,Yudong Gao,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CacheTracer，第一个仅基于API的隐藏依赖测量工具，利用前缀缓存复用作为侧信道来揭示LLM API转售商之间的隐藏供应链依赖关系。通过39个转售端点的真实测量（110万次API请求，636对端点），发现37.1%的端点对存在共享缓存可达性，包含层级最深达七层，且存在高度集中的依赖结构，揭示了看似独立的转售商之间存在的深层隐藏依赖及其潜在的爆炸半径风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM API转售商已成为现代LLM服务的核心访问层，但多级转售形成不透明的供应链：用户请求可能经过未披露的上游转售商，每个环节都可能检查或修改提示与响应，带来生态级机密性和完整性风险。现有研究仅审计单个转售商，无法提供跨转售商隐藏依赖的可见性。

Method: 核心洞察是利用前缀缓存复用作为侧信道，通过缓存可达性（cache-reach）关系来测量依赖。实现两个原语：Flood（通过一个端点填充新鲜缓存状态）和Prove（探测另一端点是否可复用该缓存，同时排除探测自身产生的命中）。随后对39个转售端点进行真实世界测量，发送110万次API请求覆盖636个端点对。

Result: 测量揭示了深层且集中的缓存可达结构：37.1%的测量端点对表现出共享缓存可达性，包含顺序跨越七层，且一个缓存可达节点至少包含在31个其他节点中。恢复的结构具有模型特异性。通过真实世界一致性检查和受控实验验证，CacheTracer具有高可靠性和准确性。

Conclusion: 这些发现揭示了看似独立的API转售商之间存在大量隐藏依赖。这种深层且集中的依赖结构可能产生巨大的潜在爆炸半径——共同上游路径上的机密性或完整性故障可能影响多个下游转售商的用户，凸显了LLM生态系统中供应链透明度和安全审计的重要性。

Abstract: LLM API resellers have become an important access layer to modern LLM services. However, multi-level resale creates an opaque supply chain: a user's request may traverse undisclosed upstream resellers, each of which can inspect or modify prompts and responses, inducing ecosystem-level confidentiality and integrity risks. Existing studies audit individual resellers, but provide little visibility into hidden dependencies across resellers. We present CacheTracer, the first API-only measurement of such hidden dependencies. Our key insight is to exploit prefix-cache reuse as a side channel to measure dependency via cache-reach relations. CacheTracer operationalizes this insight with two primitives: Flood populates fresh cache state through one endpoint, and Prove probes whether another can reuse it while excluding probe-created hits.
  We then conduct a real-world measurement study with CacheTracer on 39 reseller endpoints, sending 1.1 million API requests across 636 endpoint pairs. Our measurements reveal a deep, concentrated cache-reach structure: 37.1% of measured pairs exhibit shared cache reach, the containment order spans seven layers, and one cache reach is contained within at least 31 of other nodes. We further find that the recovered structure is model-specific. We also evaluate the validity of CacheTracer through both real-world consistency checks and controlled experiments. The results show its high reliability and accuracy. These findings reveal substantial hidden dependencies among seemingly independent API resellers. Such deep and concentrated dependencies can create a large potential blast radius, where a confidentiality or integrity failure along a common upstream path may affect users across multiple downstream resellers.

</details>


### [287] [Beyond Explicit Generators: Distribution-Free Linear-Decomposition Attacks on Public-Key Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20798)
*Ziyan Chen,Ding-Xuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究线性分解攻击在线性密钥传输公钥方案中的威力，证明公开可采样且采样轨道维数为多项式的线性密钥传输与IND-CPA安全不兼容，并成功攻击2024年基于扭曲-歪斜群环的概率公钥加密方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性分解攻击可以在不恢复秘密代数作用的情况下攻破公钥方案，但现有研究未充分建模仅依赖公开采样-求值预言机的攻击场景。作者希望分析在任意诱导分布下、仅使用公开信息攻击未来密文的安全性边界。

Method: 将公开配对样本建模为固定秘密线性传输下的分布，定义采样轨道维数作为加密分布的有效维度；证明分布无关的一次性恢复、未来密文覆盖的高概率证书以及最优采样跨度复杂度m*_span(r,ε,δ)=Θ((r+log(1/δ))/ε)；在此基础上推导通用不可能性定理。

Result: 证明公开可采样的线性密钥传输若采样轨道维数为多项式级，则与IND-CPA安全不兼容（当传输值决定解密载荷时）。对2024年扭曲-歪斜群环概率PKE方案给出仅需采样器的线性攻击，实现明文恢复和常数IND-CPA优势，实验验证了线性传输机制和端到端恢复，并表明高未来密文覆盖率可能先于完整代数跨度的恢复。

Conclusion: 公开采样性本身就是线性密钥传输方案的安全漏洞来源——攻击者无需恢复秘密代数动作，仅靠独立的公开协议样本即可破坏此类方案的IND-CPA安全性。这一通用不可能性定理为设计安全公钥密码方案提供了明确的反向约束。

Abstract: Linear-decomposition attacks can break public-key schemes without recovering the secret algebraic action: when a target public state lies in a known linear span, its decomposition coefficients transfer through the unknown action to reveal the shared value. We study a setting in which the adversary uses only the public sampling-and-evaluation oracle available to honest participants, the induced distribution is arbitrary, and the goal is to attack future ciphertexts rather than recover the full algebraic span.
  We model public paired samples under a fixed secret linear transport and define the sampled-orbit dimension as the effective dimension of the encryption distribution. We prove distribution-free one-shot recovery, a high-probability certificate for the future-ciphertext coverage of a sampled span, and the optimal sampled-span complexity $m^\star_{\mathrm{span}}(r,\varepsilon,δ) =Θ((r+\log(1/δ))/\varepsilon)$. These results yield a generic impossibility theorem: publicly samplable linear key transport with polynomial sampled-orbit dimension is incompatible with IND--CPA security when the transported value determines the decryption payload.
  We apply the framework to the 2024 probabilistic PKE from twisted--skew group rings. Its underlying Computational Twisted--Skew Problem admits a sampler-only linear attack using independently generated public protocol samples, yielding plaintext recovery and constant IND--CPA advantage. Experiments verify the linear transport and end-to-end recovery, and show that high future-ciphertext coverage may precede recovery of the full algebraic span.

</details>


### [288] [GhostTac: Manipulating Tactile Sensors without Physical Contact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20817)
*Kun Wang,Xuancun Lu,Ruochen Zhou,Kai Wang,Tongjun Ye,Yihao Shao,Chen Yan,Xiaoyu Ji,Wenyuan Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GhostTac，据我们所知是首个通过电磁干扰（EMI）对触觉传感器进行非接触式攻击的方法。利用非线性和带宽受限放大效应，将精心设计的EMI信号转化为持续DC偏移，绕过板载滤波并引起稳定的测量偏差，从而实现对传感器输出的细粒度可控操纵，可能引发机器人有害行为。在10个传感器模块和2只灵巧手上共15种不同类型触觉传感器上均验证了攻击有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉传感器是机器人系统的核心组件，使机器人能够通过触觉反馈感知和与物理环境交互。然而，尽管其重要性，触觉传感器的物理层安全性在以往研究中几乎没有得到关注。

Method: 识别出EMI利用了触觉传感器的非线性整流效应和受限带宽放大效应两个机制，使精心设计的EMI信号被转换为持续的DC偏移，从而绕过板载滤波并产生稳定的测量偏差。在此基础上，GhostTac通过重塑空间分布和操纵目标位置信号幅值，实现对传感器输出的细粒度、可控操纵。

Result: 在10个传感器模块和2只灵巧手上进行了评估，覆盖15种不同类型的触觉传感器，所有测试设备均表现出一致的攻击有效性。此外，通过三个案例研究（触觉抓取、打滑检测和材料分类）展示了在真实机器人任务中的实际影响。

Conclusion: GhostTac揭示了一种针对机器人触觉感知的新物理攻击向量，可能诱导机器人产生过度施力等有害行为，造成物理损坏或人身伤害，为触觉感知系统的物理层安全研究提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Tactile sensors are integral components of modern robotic systems, enabling robots to perceive and interact with the physical environment through tactile feedback. Despite their importance, the physical-layer security of tactile sensors has received little attention in prior work. In this paper, we present GhostTac, to the best of our knowledge, the first contactless attack that manipulates tactile sensing via electromagnetic interference (EMI). We identify that EMI exploits the nonlinear rectification and limited bandwidth amplification effects, allowing carefully crafted EMI signals to be converted into a persistent DC offset that bypasses on-board filtering and induces stable measurement deviations. Building on this mechanism, GhostTac enables fine-grained and controllable manipulation of sensor outputs by reshaping the spatial distribution and manipulating the magnitude at the targeted location. Such interference can induce unintended and harmful robot behaviors, such as causing a domestic robot to exert excessive force, resulting in physical damage or human injury. We evaluate GhostTac on 10 sensor modules and 2 dexterous hands, covering 15 tactile sensors of different types, and demonstrate consistent attack effectiveness across all tested devices. We further present three case studies on tactile grasping, slip detection, and material classification to illustrate practical impacts in real robotic tasks. We envision that our findings shed light on a new physical attack vector against tactile sensing in robotic systems.

</details>


### [289] [On the Additive FFT Techniques over Binary Extension Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20855)
*Susanta Samanta,Mohammadtaghi Badakhshan,Guang Gong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文受Bailey四步FFT算法启发，提出二元扩域上仿射子空间多项式求值的加法FFT（AFFT）技术。核心洞察是：关于子空间消失多项式的Taylor展开提供了Bailey矩阵公式的结构对应物，可将AFFT分解为与矩阵行列相关的独立子AFFT。论文提出了通用基AFFT，并针对Cantor特殊基得到两个特化算法：一个支持任意维度分解且Taylor展开阶段无需有限域乘法；另一个保持子空间多项式的二项式形式，恰好需要1/2·n·log2 n次乘法。实现显示该算法在42种配置中的37种上快于LCH AFFT。论文还形式化了偏Cantor特殊基概念，并识别了von zur Gathen-Gerhard算法和通用基AFFT优于Gao-Mateer算法的参数区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bailey四步FFT算法在计算复杂度上具有优势，但将其思想推广到二元扩域上的加法FFT（AFFT）尚未系统开展。现有AFFT算法（如LCH、Gao-Mateer等）在计算效率、内存局部性和结构灵活性方面存在改进空间，需要一个统一的框架来度量不同基选择和维度分解策略的性能差异。

Method: 提出关于子空间消失多项式的Taylor展开作为Bailey矩阵公式的结构对应物，将AFFT分解为与矩阵行列相关联的独立子AFFT。首先给出适用于任意有序基和任意维度拆分的通用基AFFT作为基线；然后特化到Cantor特殊基，得到两个算法：第一个利用Cantor特殊基结构使Taylor展开阶段无需有限域乘法；第二个采用保持子空间多项式二项式形式的分解，其乘法数恰好为1/2·n·log2 n。此外，形式化定义了偏Cantor特殊基概念用于复杂度比较分析。

Result: 第二个特化算法在42种测试配置中37种快于LCH AFFT，其优势源于完全递归结构带来的内存局部性和无需独立的基转换与求值阶段。该算法乘法复杂度达到精确的1/2·n·log2 n，加法数由m的二进制表示决定并具有闭式形式。在部分Cantor特殊基参数区间内，von zur Gathen-Gerhard算法和通用基AFFT在加法和乘法数上均优于第一个Gao-Mateer算法。

Conclusion: 基于Taylor展开的框架为二元扩域上的AFFT提供了统一的分析和优化手段，Cantor特殊基下的特化算法在计算复杂度和工程实现（内存局部性）上均具有优势。这一工作为AFFT的设计空间提供了更精细的刻画，并为后续在不同基和维度拆分策略下的算法选择提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Motivated by Bailey's four-step FFT algorithm (1989), we develop additive FFT techniques for polynomial evaluation over affine subspaces of binary extension fields. Our key insight is that the Taylor expansion with respect to vanishing polynomials of subspaces provides a structural counterpart to Bailey's matrix formulation. It decomposes an additive FFT (AFFT) into independent sub-AFFTs associated with the columns and rows of a matrix. We first present a general-basis AFFT that applies to any ordered basis and any split of the dimension, providing a unified baseline for measuring the gains from specialization. We then specialize the framework to the Cantor special basis and obtain two AFFT algorithms. The first supports an arbitrary decomposition of the AFFT dimension and exploits the Cantor special basis structure to perform the Taylor expansion stage without finite field multiplications. The second uses a decomposition that preserves the binomial form of the relevant subspace polynomials. It requires exactly $\frac{1}{2}n\log_2 n$ multiplications, together with a closed-form addition count determined by the binary representation of $m$. Our implementation results show that this algorithm is faster than the LCH AFFT over a Cantor special basis in 37 of the 42 configurations tested across two hardware platforms. This performance advantage stems from its fully recursive structure, which provides memory locality by design and avoids separate basis-conversion and evaluation stages. Finally, in a separate analysis, we formalize the notion of partial Cantor special bases and identify parameter regimes in which both the von zur Gathen-Gerhard algorithm and our general-basis AFFT require fewer additions and multiplications than the first Gao-Mateer algorithm.

</details>


### [290] [Vibe Coding and Web Application Security: A Twin-Prompt Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20963)
*Darko Andročec*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了在自然语言提示中显式添加安全需求是否会改善LLM生成Web应用的安全性。通过6个功能不同的Web应用、每个应用生成基线(A)和安全感知(B)两种变体，采用静态、依赖、动态和手动技术分析12个程序，发现安全感知变体在每个应用中的问题都更少(24对比51)，且无严重或高危问题。由于语料规模小且每次仅生成一次，本文作为初步研究，报告描述性观察而非统计性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM越来越多地直接从自然语言提示生成完整Web应用，一个关键问题浮现：明确请求安全最佳实践是否能改善生成结果的安全性。此前缺乏对这一具体干预效果的系统性研究。

Method: 研究选取6个功能不同的Web应用，每个应用生成两个仅末尾附有安全需求章节的提示变体：基线(A)和安全感知(B)。所有12个程序由同一agentic编码助手和同一模型版本在单轮非迭代生成中产生，随后通过静态分析、依赖分析、动态分析和手动测试四种技术进行分析，共筛选出85个候选发现并确认75个。

Result: 安全感知变体在每个应用中都产生更少的确认发现(24对比51)，且不包含任何Critical或High级别问题；最严重的问题仅通过手动测试检测到。实验共确认75个发现(来自85个候选)。由于语料规模小且每种变体仅生成一次，结果是描述性观察而非统计确立的效果。

Conclusion: 显式添加安全需求提示似乎能够减少LLM生成的Web应用中的安全缺陷，但当前工作属于初步研究，结论需谨慎对待。研究管线将被扩展到多个模型和多次重复运行以验证观察结果的普适性。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly generate complete web applications from natural-language prompts, raising the question of whether explicitly requesting security best practice improves the result. We study six functionally distinct web applications, each generated in two prompt variants that are identical except for an appended security-requirements section: a baseline (A) and a security-aware (B) variant. All twelve programs were produced by the same agentic coding assistant and the same model version in a single, non-iterative generation round, and were then analyzed with static, dependency, dynamic and manual techniques, yielding 75 confirmed findings out of 85 candidates. The security-aware variant produced fewer confirmed findings in every application (24 versus 51) and contained no Critical or High issues; the most severe finding was detected only by manual testing. Because the corpus is small and each variant was generated once, we report descriptive observations rather than statistically established effects, and position the work as a preliminary study whose pipeline is being scaled to multiple models and repeated runs.

</details>


### [291] [Structured but Fragile: On the Limits of LLMs in Cybersecurity Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20966)
*Pasquale Malacaria,Yunxiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM在网络安全防御选择任务中的结构化推理能力，通过攻击图上的防御选择问题，对比LLM策略与博弈论优化基线，发现LLM在显式结构下表现出条件性胜任，但能力脆弱，对提示变化和图形复杂度高度敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用于网络安全工作流，但目前尚不清楚它们能否进行结构化的安全推理，还是仅仅依赖表面线索和先验知识。这一问题对于AI辅助安全决策系统的设计和评估至关重要。

Method: 在来自真实威胁场景（勒索软件、供应链攻击、云滥用、Kubernetes攻击、POS恶意软件、ICS/OT入侵）的攻击图上，设置预算约束下的防御选择任务；将LLM策略与博弈论优化基线进行比较；通过改变提示框架、标签操作等测试鲁棒性；并让LLM生成求解器以测试其编程实现能力。

Result: LLM在显式攻击图结构下常能产生接近优化基线的连贯策略，但能力脆弱：图形复杂度增加时行为恶化；对提示措辞高度敏感，小改动可显著改变排名；将差策略重新标注为"最优"会大幅提升评估分数；正式风险与LLM判断存在非单调关系；LLM生成的结构化求解器扩展性差。

Conclusion: LLM能在受控表示下近似结构化网络安全推理，但无法稳健地应用这种推理。这为AI辅助安全决策支持系统的设计与评估提供了重要启示。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in cybersecurity workflows, yet it remains unclear whether they can perform structured security reasoning or merely rely on superficial cues and prior knowledge. We study this question in the context of defence selection over attack graphs derived from real-world threat scenarios, including ransomware, supply-chain compromise, cloud abuse, Kubernetes attacks, POS malware, and ICS/OT intrusion. Given a budget constraint, LLMs must select security controls to minimise attacker success. We compare their strategies against each other and against a game-theoretic optimization baseline used as a normative reference for structured reasoning. Our results show that LLMs exhibit conditional competence. When explicit attack-graph structure is provided, they often produce coherent strategies close to the optimization baseline. However, their capabilities are fragile. LLM behaviour becomes increasingly fragile with graph complexity and is highly sensitive to framing. Small prompt changes can substantially alter rankings, and merely relabeling a poor strategy as ``optimal'' dramatically improves its evaluation. We further observe a non-monotonic relationship between formal risk and LLM judgement: strategies closest to the optimum are not necessarily ranked highest by LLM evaluators. To further probe reasoning ability, we ask LLMs to generate solvers for the same optimization problem. While the generated implementations recover the correct high-level formulation, they scale poorly compared to a purpose-built solver. Overall, our findings show that LLMs can approximate structured cybersecurity reasoning under controlled representations, but do not apply it robustly. This has important implications for the design and evaluation of AI-assisted security decision-support systems.

</details>


### [292] [$Z^2$-ACT: End-to-End Verifiable Agentic Intent Control for Open 6G RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21049)
*Sunder Ali Khowaja,Kapal Dev,George C. Alexandropoulos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Z²-ACT架构，将零知识认证控制与零信任可验证智能体意图协同集成到6G开放无线接入网络的非实时与近实时RIC中，通过智能体协调、形式化意图约束、零信任提示验证和加密问责四个原语，实现AI辅助控制环在多方运营商意图和不可信模型输入下的安全、可验证和可审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着6G开放和解耦RAN向多供应商演进，AI辅助控制环需要在并发运营商意图和不可信模型输入下保持安全、可验证和可审计。现有研究分别孤立地解决智能体协调、形式化意图约束、零信任提示验证和加密问责，导致单独使用时缺少预实现安全性、持续语义验证和跨域审计能力。

Method: 提出Z²-ACT架构，集成四个原语跨越非实时（non-RT）与近实时（near-RT）RIC。将类型化Intent Contracts编码为运营商目标；LLM输入仅在实际对抗性意图检查后准入；技能序列仅在自管理门控满足后释放；每次成功提交记录为带零知识证明的绑定承诺。实验使用公共ColO-RAN测量数据，全架构与定向消融及传统强化学习基线对比，非实时路径使用实时LLM翻译运营商意图，近实时控制基于公共KPM序列进行轨迹驱动。

Result: 实验结果表明，Z²-ACT在近实时时延和信令开销适度增加的情况下，实现了改进的执行过滤能力和攻击韧性；报告了意图翻译准确率、无效或幻觉合约率、非实时时延以及在对抗性或误导性意图下的行为表现。

Conclusion: Z²-ACT通过融合四个安全原语，在6G O-RAN多供应商场景下实现了安全、可验证且可审计的AI辅助控制，在近实时包络内以适度开销换取了更强的安全性和攻击防御能力。

Abstract: With the progression in open and disaggregated 6G radio access networks, it is expected that the system will be able to host multi-vendors. In order to host multi-vendors, it is essential that AI-assisted control loops remain safe, verifiable, and auditable under concurrent operator intents and untrusted model inputs. The existing studies address the agentic coordination, formal intent constraints, zero-trust prompt verification and cryptographic accountability in isolation, which leaves pre-realization safety, continuous semantic verification and cross-domain audit incomplete when used individually. In this regard, we propose zero-knowledge auditable control and zero-trust verifiable agentic intent architecture ($Z^2$-ACT), which integrates the aforementioned four primitives across the non-real-time and near-real-time RICs. We encode the typed Intent Contracts as operator goals while the large language model inputs are only admitted after a practical adversarial intent check. The skill sequences in the proposed study are released only when a self-management gate is satisfied while every successful commit is recorded as a binding commitment with a zero-knowledge proof. Our experimental evaluation on public ColO-RAN measurements compares the full architecture against targeted ablations and a conventional reinforcement-learning baseline. A live large language model is used in the non-real-time path to translate operator intents into Intent Contracts; we report translation accuracy, the rate of invalid or hallucinated contracts, non-real-time latency, and behavior under adversarial or misleading intents. Near-real-time control remains trace-driven on the public KPM sequences. Results indicate improved actuation filtering and attack resilience at modest latency and signaling cost inside the near-real-time envelope.

</details>


### [293] [ClawSentry: A Progressive Multi-Tier Security Monitor for Safeguarding Autonomous LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21101)
*Kai Wang,Zeming Wei,BiaoJie Zeng,Chang Jin,An Wang,Xiaokun Luan,Zhixiao Lin,Jingjing Qu,Xia Hu,Xingcheng Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ClawSentry，一个开源、框架无关的Agent安全监督网关，针对LLM Agent在技能（skill）启用、调用意图、执行效果和事后后果四个控制环节点上的渐进式安全风险，提供从技能包审计（FSPR）到运行时三级递进决策引擎再到会话级抗绕过机制的完整防护。在SkillInject基准上将上下文攻击成功率从39.55%降至2.61%，同时保持完成任务成功率仅小幅下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent正从对话转向执行代码、读取文件、编排外部工具，单一被恶意第三方技能劫持的Agent可导致数据泄露、权限提升或级联破坏。现有安全防护通常局限在单一生命周期边界或单次调用，无法应对可在表面形式、工具或轮次间重现的危险目标，因此需要一个覆盖Agent控制循环多节点且能对抗跨形式绕过企图的统一安全监管方案。

Method: ClawSentry包含四个核心组件：（1）首次使用技能包审查（FSPR），在技能执行前基于确定性证据下限进行审计，未解决案例升级至有界只读Agent审查（locus A）；（2）运行时三层递进决策引擎——确定性L1层、规则锚定的L2语义审查器、只读L3证据搜寻Agent，仅对残余模糊性消耗上下文审查（loci B-C）；（3）会话级抗绕过机制，识别工具切换和改写重试（loci B-C）；（4）事后方馈路径，将高严重性证据非追溯性地引入后续审查（locus D）。此外通过Agent Harness Protocol（AHP）抽象，无需修改Agent内部即可将统一策略应用于Codex、Claude Code、Kimi CLI和Gemini CLI。

Result: 在SkillInject基准上（Codex/GPT-5.4），上下文攻击成功率从39.55%降至2.61%，而上下文任务成功率仅从83.78%微降至83.05%。在完整SkillsSafety基准的五个Work Agents上，ClawSentry将攻击成功率从未防护的33.5-49.7%限制在9.09-15.03%，干净技能上的聚合任务成功率保持98.7%。

Conclusion: ClawSentry证明了通过覆盖Agent控制循环四个节点（技能接纳、调用意图、执行效果、事后后果）的渐进式防护设计，可以在不显著牺牲任务完成率的前提下极大降低恶意技能带来的安全风险，为多框架Agent运行时提供统一且可扩展的安全监管方案。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) agents move from conversation to executing code, reading local files, and orchestrating external tools, a single agent hijacked by a malicious third-party skill can cause data exfiltration, privilege escalation, or cascading compromise. We argue that agentic risk is progressive: it can enter at four loci of the agent control loop--skill admission, invocation-time intent, execution-time effect, and post-action consequence--while a denied dangerous objective can reappear across surface forms, tools, or turns; existing safeguards are typically local to one lifecycle boundary or one call. Guided by this threat model, we present ClawSentry, an open-source, framework-agnostic security supervision gateway for agent runtimes. Before a skill package is ever executed, First-use Skill Package Review (FSPR) audits it under a deterministic evidence floor, escalating unresolved cases to bounded read-only agentic review (locus A). At runtime, a three-tier progressive decision engine--a deterministic L1 layer, a rule-anchored L2 semantic reviewer, and a read-only L3 evidence-seeking agent--spends contextual review only on the residual ambiguity, while a session-level anti-bypass mechanism recognizes tool-switching and rephrased retries (loci B--C); a post-action path feeds high-severity evidence non-retroactively into later review (locus D). An Agent Harness Protocol (AHP) abstraction applies one policy across Codex, Claude Code, Kimi CLI, and Gemini CLI without modifying agent internals. On SkillInject with Codex/GPT-5.4, contextual ASR falls from 39.55% to 2.61% while contextual TSR moves only from 83.78% to 83.05%. Across five Work Agents on the full SkillsSafety benchmark, ClawSentry confines ASR to 9.09--15.03% from 33.5--49.7% unprotected, and aggregate TSR on clean skills remains 98.7%.

</details>


### [294] [TraceGrant: A Contract-Governed Security Framework for the Task-Effect Lifecycle of Networked LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21126)
*Bohao Liao,Jingchao Wang,Qipeng Song,Jin Cao,Jieling Wang,Boyu Deng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TraceGrant是一个面向网络化大语言模型代理的安全框架，通过显式合约（Contract）覆盖任务执行全生命周期，在949个AgentDojo和400个Agent Security Bench攻击案例下实现零攻击成功，同时保持77.32%和83.00%的攻击场景下实用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络化LLM代理需要从多种外部源检索信息以完成多步任务，但合法内容中可能隐藏间接提示注入攻击，导致工具使用被重定向、敏感参数被篡改或任务被中断。现有防御主要局限于约束不可信内容或单个工具调用，未能将用户意图、运行时证据、实际效果和任务完成情况充分关联起来，缺乏统一治理机制。

Method: TraceGrant通过显式合约（Contract）治理任务效果生命周期：执行前，从可信用户请求建立任务效果边界；执行过程中，被接纳的证据只能实例化合约中已经建立的权限；执行后，根据实际工具结果验证任务完成情况。整体框架将用户意图、运行时证据、具体工具执行和验证的任务完成连接为统一治理层。

Result: 在固定基准设置下，TraceGrant在949个AgentDojo和400个Agent Security Bench攻击案例中无攻击成功记录，同时分别在77.32%和83.00%的攻击率下保持实用性。此外还进行了白盒防御感知攻击评估、合约质量分析、分阶段消融实验、针对性压力测试和运行时开销测量。

Conclusion: TraceGrant提供了统一的治理层，有效连接可信用户意图、运行时证据、具体工具执行和验证的任务完成，在抵御间接提示注入攻击的同时保持了较高的实用率，为网络化LLM代理任务执行生命周期提供了一种系统化的安全治理方案。

Abstract: Networked large language model (LLM) agents retrieve information from email, cloud storage, calendars, transaction platforms, and Web services to complete multistep tasks that produce persistent external effects. The same content needed for legitimate execution may also contain indirect prompt injections that redirect tool use, alter sensitive arguments, or disrupt task completion. Existing defenses mainly constrain untrusted content or individual tool calls, leaving user intent, runtime evidence, realized effects, and task completion insufficiently connected. We present TraceGrant, a security framework that governs the task-effect lifecycle of networked LLM agents through an explicit Contract. Before execution, TraceGrant establishes a task-effect boundary from the trusted user request. During execution, admitted evidence can instantiate only authority already established by the Contract. After execution, task completion is verified against actual tool results. Across 949 AgentDojo and 400 Agent Security Bench attack cases under fixed benchmark settings, TraceGrant recorded no attack successes while retaining utility under attack rates of 77.32% and 83.00%, respectively. We further evaluate TraceGrant through white-box defense-aware attacks, Contract quality analysis, stage ablations, targeted stress tests, and runtime overhead measurements. The results show that TraceGrant provides a unified governance layer that connects trusted user intent, runtime evidence, concrete tool execution, and verified task completion.

</details>


### [295] [Billion-Scale Nearest-Neighbor Search under Fully Homomorphic Encryption on a Single GPU, Balancing Leakage and Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21131)
*Isamu Isozaki,Madison Bratina,Edward Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在完全同态加密（FHE）下对十亿级向量数据库进行隐私保护最近邻搜索的系统，结合秩缩减与分层路由策略在GPU上实现加密查询，在DataComp-1B（10.9亿向量）上达到recall@10为0.90、每查询约6秒的延迟，并分析了分层访问模式带来的数据库几何信息泄露及缓解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模向量数据库中，用户查询需要与数据库向量进行相似度匹配，但传统方案会让服务器直接看到明文查询，侵犯用户隐私。使用FHE加密查询可以解决隐私问题，但直接对十亿向量逐行加密评分在计算上不可行，因此需要设计高效的加密查询方案来兼顾隐私与性能。

Method: 将两种核心技术结合在FHE下执行：一是秩缩减（rank reduction），对每个向量的维度进行压缩以降低加密计算开销；二是层次结构（hierarchy），通过路由将查询导向较小的候选集而非全量扫描。整个过程在单个GPU上执行。系统还描述了完整的客户端-服务器协议，并针对访问模式泄露问题采用种子化（固定分组）填充技术。

Result: 在DataComp-1B（10.9亿向量，512维CLIP）上，达到recall@10为0.90（含近重复图像时0.95），加密查询延迟约6秒/次；轻量配置在约1.8秒达到0.78/0.83。在Deep1B（10亿向量，96维）上，全层级FHE下recall@10为0.90（实测0.9045，与明文路由0.906高度一致），延迟2.3秒/次。同时测出层次访问模式泄露了数据库几何信息（可恢复72%粗粒度单元邻居图），种子化填充可将泄露降低约35倍至约2%。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了在完全同态加密下对十亿级向量数据库进行实用化的隐私保护最近邻搜索是可行的，通过秩缩减与分层路由的组合在GPU上实现了可部署的延迟；但同时也揭示了分层结构的访问模式存在数据库几何信息泄露问题，种子化填充提供了有效缓解手段，为后续隐私保护向量检索系统的设计提供了重要参考。

Abstract: We build a system that answers "which database vectors are most similar to my query?" without the server ever seeing the query. The query is encrypted with fully homomorphic en- cryption (FHE); the server does all its scoring on ciphertexts and returns encrypted results that only the client can read. The challenge is speed: at a billion vectors, scoring every row under encryption is far too slow, so we combine two ideas - rank reduction (shrink each vector's dimen- sion) and a hierarchy (route to a small candidate set instead of scanning everything) - executed under encryption on a single GPU. We evaluate on three corpora at very different scales: a face corpus of 222 049 centroids clustered from ~10 M face images (512-dim), DataComp-1B (1.39 x 10^9 vectors, 512-dim CLIP), and Deep1B (10^9 vectors, 96-dim). On DataComp-1B we reach a recall@10 of 0.90 against the single labeled answer, or 0.95 when a near-duplicate im- age in the top-10 also counts as correct (the data is web-scraped and full of duplicates), at ~6 s per encrypted query on a GPU; a lighter configuration reaches 0.78/0.83 at ~1.8 s. These are warm (deployable) server-side latencies - client decryption and network transfer are excluded. On Deep1B we reach recall@10 0.90 under all-levels FHE (0.9045 measured over 2000 FHE queries, matching the 0.906 plaintext routing - the 96 -> 128 zero-pad is exact, correlation 1.0) at 2.3 s warm per query. We describe the full client-server protocol in enough detail to repro- duce it, and report accuracy and latency for every configuration. We also measure what this speed costs: the hierarchy's access pattern leaks the database geometry (an observer recovers 72% of the coarse-cell neighbor graph from access patterns alone), and we show that seeded (fixed-group) padding cuts this leak by ~35x (to ~2%), where naive padding is defeated by a repeated-query attack.

</details>


### [296] [AID-Guard: Stateful Authorization for Delegated Agent Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21159)
*Yingzhe Tong,Leyu Dai,Songhui Guo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AID-Guard，一个状态化的"授权到效果"闭合协议，用于解决AI智能体工具调用中授权在提交后失效、重试/恢复导致重复副作用等问题，通过提交时重新验证、模糊状态下保留单一预留、以及交付栅栏确保一个授权最多产生一个效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的工具型AI智能体授权机制通常在准入阶段就结束，但提供者状态、交付、重试和恢复会在授权后持续演化。请求可能在提交前被修改，或响应丢失导致替代请求从同一授权中产生第二次效果。这些安全缺口可能导致未授权的提供者效应或重复副作用。

Method: AID-Guard协议包含三个核心机制：(1) 在提交时重新验证已批准的请求和提供者状态；(2) 在模糊（不确定性）状态下保留一个预留（reservation）；(3) 仅在得到终结结果或通过交付栅栏认证"无效果"后，才允许释放预留或产生一个后继。实现采用Python/SQLite原型，在回环MCP域和Stripe等真实提供者合约上进行评估。

Result: 在回环MCP域中，13次实时变更无未授权效果，3个并发历史可线性化，证据束支持公开验证与重放。210次Stripe提供者合约试验全部匹配预先声明结果。在Stripe和Resend上，40个终结-后继调度、30个重叠竞争和10个崩溃恢复调度均无重复效果。在完全提议者妥协下阻断44/44攻击并接纳44/44合法提议。严格精确清单模式降低了35.4-43.8个百分点的良性效用，类型化前沿恢复9-10个完成且无观察到不安全效果。组合研究阻断20/20后准入生命周期攻击，保留8/8有效或精确重试执行。

Conclusion: AID-Guard是首个在单一生命周期中统一授权后重新验证、预留保留与交付栅栏的Agent授权协议，实验结果表明在所评估的效果路径清单、提供者合约和故障调度范围内，它能有效实现从授权到效果的绑定。

Abstract: Tool-using AI agents turn delegated tasks into provider effects, yet authorization often ends at admission while provider state, delivery, retry, and recovery evolve. A request may change before commit, or response loss may cause a replacement to create a second effect from one approval. We present AID-Guard, a stateful authorization-to-effect closure protocol. It revalidates the approved request and provider state at commit, retains one reservation under ambiguity, and permits release or one successor only after a terminal result or certified no effect with a delivery fence. For supported provider contracts, one reservation yields at most one effect across retry and recovery. To our knowledge, it is the first evaluated agent-authorization protocol to unify these controls in one lifecycle.
  We implement a Python/SQLite prototype. In a declared loopback MCP domain, 13 live mutations caused no unauthorized provider effects, three concurrent histories were linearizable, and evidence bundles supported public verification and replay. All 210 Stripe provider-contract trials matched predeclared outcomes. Across Stripe and Resend, 40 terminalize-successor schedules, 30 overlapping races, and 10 crash-recovery schedules completed without duplicate effects. Under complete proposer compromise, AID-Guard blocked 44/44 attacks and admitted 44/44 matched legitimate proposals. Its strict exact-manifest profile reduced benign utility by 35.4 to 43.8 percentage points; a typed frontier recovered 9-10 completions without observed unsafe effects. A composition study blocked 20/20 post-admission lifecycle attacks and preserved 8/8 valid or exact-retry executions. The results support authorization-to-effect binding under the evaluated effect-path inventory, provider contracts, and failure schedules.

</details>


### [297] [Workplace Surveillance and Insider Threat Risk Management: Legal Limits and Privacy Harms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21205)
*Haywood Gelman,John D. Hastings,Suvineetha Herath,Quentin Covert*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文探讨工作场所监控的双重属性：合法正当的组织用途与过度监控造成的法律合规和数据隐私问题。研究聚焦于内部威胁（InT）人物画像的行为与心理检测标准，评述员工监控工具与技术，分析工作场所隐私法律、过度监控案例及其隐私危害，并指出研究空白（如过多告警掩盖真正InT信号），最后提出改进透明性、实施InT培训及调优检测工具等建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工作场所监控日益普遍，但过度监控可能违反法律和数据隐私原则；同时，监控的首要安全目标是检测内部威胁（InT），因此需要理解InT的行为和心理特征，以平衡组织安全需求与员工隐私权。

Method: 采用综述性研究方法：回顾InT人物画像以理解行为与心理检测标准；评述员工监控工具与技术以刻画监控格局；梳理工作场所隐私法律、过度监控案例及隐私危害；识别研究空白。

Result: 识别出过度监控相关的关键研究空白，包括告警过载问题——海量告警可能掩盖有意义的InT指标；并提出三项改进建议：提升监控透明度、实施InT培训项目以增强组织检测能力、调优InT工具以检测相关心理与行为指标。

Conclusion: 工作场所监控需要在合法目的与隐私保护之间取得平衡，通过提高透明度、加强培训和优化工具参数，可以有效提升InT检测能力，同时降低过度监控带来的法律与隐私风险。

Abstract: Workplace surveillance is used by organizations to protect corporate assets and monitor employee productivity. This research presents two central arguments on workplace surveillance: although surveillance serves legitimate organizational purposes, over-surveillance can violate legal requirements and data privacy principles; and a primary security objective of workplace surveillance is the detection of insider threats (InT). InT are comprised of individuals with authorized resource access whose intentional or unintentional actions may damage or compromise corporate assets. This paper investigates InT personas to understand behavioral and psychological detection criteria. Employee surveillance tools and techniques are reviewed to characterize the employee surveillance landscape. Workplace privacy laws, examples of over-surveillance, and the resulting privacy harms are addressed. The review identifies research gaps related to over-surveillance, including the generation of excessive alerts that may obscure meaningful InT indicators. The paper concludes with recommendations to improve workplace surveillance transparency, implement InT training programs to improve organizational detection capabilities, and tune InT tools to detect relevant psychological and behavioral indicators.

</details>


### [298] [Utility Under Attack: Agent Memory Poisoning and the Limits of Content Screening and Provenance Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21230)
*Arulnidhi Karunanidhi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了持久记忆系统中虚假信息注入的脆弱性。仅用1.2%的投毒记忆就能将检索准确率从0.850降至0.300。现有的写入端内容筛查管线无法识别任何投毒记忆，而来源加权检索在量化评估下要么统计上与无防御无异，要么只能通过排除所有不可信内容来恢复效用。作者主张采用检索端的占有率约束（bounded occupancy constraints）而非加法来源罚项，并发布了相关测试工具和语料库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久记忆系统允许检索和调用历史会话内容，但一旦虚假信息被存储为记忆，就会在未来的相关会话中被反复取出，造成持续的信息污染。现有防御（如写入端内容筛查和来源加权检索）是否有效缺乏严格的定量评估，作者旨在量化这一失败模式的成本并检验现有防护手段的边界。

Method: 作者在LongMemEval语料库中注入1.2%的普通措辞虚假断言（单次生成、无指令/触发器/检索器优化），测量准确率下降幅度。评估了两类防御：(1) 写入端四阶段筛查管线（对间接提示注入召回率0.832，误报1.5%）；(2) 来源加权检索，包括实际部署权重（p=0.80，统计上与无防御无差异）和更强权重。在混合来源语料库中测试不同来源可信度条件下的表现，并分析检索相似度与来源罚项的交互作用。

Result: 投毒1.2%语料使准确率从0.850降至0.300。筛查管线对被投毒记忆的拒绝率为0/360。来源加权检索中，实际部署权重与无防御无统计差异；更强权重仅在排除不可信内容时恢复效用——不可信内容多属良性时准确率从0.3167升至0.7000，但当答案证据本身来自不可信来源时，证据召回率降至零，准确率跌至0.0417。在测量的相似度区间内，加法来源罚项不存在可用设置：能抵抗查询形投毒的权重也会抑制合法但不可信来源的证据。

Conclusion: 内容筛查存在根本边界：区分真假断言通常需要超出文本本身的外部依据。加法来源罚项在检索中缺乏鲁棒的工作区间，因此应转向检索端的占有率约束来限制不可信内容在结果中的比例，而非对不可信内容施加罚项。作者公开了测试框架、语料库和聚合运行报告以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Persistent memory makes false information durable: once a false statement is stored, it can be retrieved into future sessions that match it. We measure the cost of this failure mode using plainly worded false assertions generated in a single pass, with no instruction, trigger, or retriever optimization. Poisoning 1.2% of a LongMemEval corpus reduces accuracy from 0.850 to 0.300. A four-stage write-time screening pipeline that reaches 0.832 recall on indirect prompt injection while flagging 1.5% of trigger-word-laden benign text rejects 0 of 360 poisoned memories. We argue this exposes a boundary of content-only screening: distinguishing a false assertion from a true one generally requires external grounding beyond the text itself.
  We then evaluate provenance-weighted retrieval. The shipped weight is statistically indistinguishable from no defense (p=0.80), while a stronger weight recovers utility only by excluding untrusted content. In a mixed-provenance corpus where untrusted content is mostly benign, accuracy rises from 0.3167 to 0.7000; when the answer-bearing evidence itself arrives untrusted, evidence recall falls to zero and accuracy to 0.0417. Under the measured similarity regime, the additive provenance term has no usable setting: a weight strong enough to resist query-shaped poison is also strong enough to suppress legitimate untrusted evidence. We therefore argue for bounded occupancy constraints at retrieval rather than additive provenance penalties, and release the harnesses, corpora, and aggregate run reports.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [299] [Bankruptcy Prediction via Hybrid Resampling and Stacking Ensemble Techniques with Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI)-Driven Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20343)
*Obu-Amoah Ampomah,Edmund Fosu Agyemang,Kofi Acheampong,Louis Agyekum,Enock Adu Bonsu,Eric Nyarko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一个集成共识特征选择、混合重采样、堆叠集成和可解释AI的破产预测框架，用于严重不平衡的财务数据。基于台湾破产预测数据集，通过共识规则将特征降至23个，比较了五种机器学习模型和五种深度学习模型及其堆叠组合。结果显示GRU结合SMOTE-ENN在独立模型中表现最佳（召回率0.8627，G-mean 0.8517，ROC-AUC 0.9431），SMOTE-ENN加(LGBM+XGB等)堆叠LSTM的集成方案在敏感性-特异性间取得最佳平衡。SHAP分析识别出杠杆率、盈利能力、偿债能力和运营效率为最关键预测指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 严重不平衡的财务数据导致传统破产预测模型对少数类（破产企业）识别能力不足，而现有研究缺乏对特征选择、重采样、集成学习和可解释性统一整合的系统性框架，难以满足金融机构对高召回率和可解释性的双重需求。

Method: 采用五步框架：(1) 五种特征选择算法结合共识保留规则降维至23个变量；(2) 使用SVM-SMOTE、SMOTE-Tomek和SMOTE-ENN三种混合重采样技术平衡训练数据；(3) 构建五种机器学习集成模型（GB、XGB、HGB、LightGBM、AdaBoost）与五种深度学习模型（RNN、LSTM、GRU、DNN、MLP）进行对比；(4) 以机器学习模型为基学习器、深度学习模型为元学习器构建混合堆叠集成；(5) 使用SHAP进行模型可解释性分析，评估指标包括准确率、召回率、特异性、G-mean和ROC-AUC。

Result: 重采样策略显著影响模型行为：SVM-SMOTE和SMOTE-Tomek偏向准确率和特异性，SMOTE-ENN则提升少数类检出能力。独立模型中，GRU+SMOTE-ENN表现最佳（召回率0.8627，G-mean 0.8517，ROC-AUC 0.9431）。堆叠集成中，SMOTE-ENN与(GB+XGB+HGB+LGBM+AB)+LSTM组合在敏感性和特异性之间取得最佳平衡。SHAP分析表明杠杆率、盈利能力、偿债能力和运营效率指标为破产风险的最关键预测因子。

Conclusion: 该框架证明重采样策略与模型架构的选择对破产预测性能具有关键影响，堆叠集成结合深度元学习器能有效平衡敏感性与特异性，SHAP增强了模型的透明度，为财务困境企业提供更可靠、可解释的早期预警系统。

Abstract: This study develops and evaluates a bankruptcy prediction framework that integrates consensus-based feature selection, hybrid resampling, stacking ensembles, and explainable artificial intelligence to improve minority-class detection in severely imbalanced financial data. Using the Taiwanese Bankruptcy Prediction dataset from the UCI Machine Learning Repository, five feature-selection algorithms were first applied, and a consensus retention rule reduced the input space to 23 robust variables. The balanced training data were then generated using SVM-SMOTE, SMOTE-Tomek, and SMOTE-ENN. Five ensemble machine learning classifiers, namely gradient boosting, extreme gradient boosting, histogram-based gradient boosting, LightGBM, and AdaBoost, were compared with five deep learning models, including RNN, LSTM, GRU, DNN, and MLP. In addition, hybrid stacking ensembles combined the five machine learning classifiers as base learners with each deep learning model as a meta-learner. Model performance was assessed using accuracy, recall, specificity, G-mean, and ROC-AUC, while SHAP was used to explain feature contributions. The results show that resampling strategy materially shaped model behavior. SVM-SMOTE and SMOTE-Tomek favored accuracy and specificity, whereas SMOTE-ENN delivered stronger minority-class detection. Among standalone models, the GRU with SMOTE-ENN achieved the best overall predictive balance, with recall of 0.8627, G-mean of 0.8517, and ROC-AUC of 0.9431. Among stacking ensembles, SMOTE-ENN with (GB+XGB+HGB+LGBM+AB)+LSTM provided the strongest compromise between sensitivity and specificity. SHAP analysis identified leverage, profitability, solvency, and operational efficiency indicators as the most influential predictors of bankruptcy risk. These findings support more reliable and interpretable early warning systems for financially distressed firms.

</details>


### [300] [Machine Learning and ARIMA Model Averaging for Adaptive Public Health Forecasting: Comparative Evaluation and an Ontario COVID-19 Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20406)
*Yushu Zou,Ye Li,Johra Moosa,Martin Grunnill,Samir N. Patel,Venkata R. Duvvuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了ARIMA、随机森林和XGBoost三种模型在预测加拿大安大略省COVID-19周病例数中的表现，并提出了MLAMA（机器学习与ARIMA模型加权平均）集成方法，结果表明没有单一模型在所有场景下最优，MLAMA在多数预测时域上取得最低误差，支持根据运行条件选择预测模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共卫生预测必须在监控数据出现突变时快速响应，同时避免对噪声、报告假象或临时趋势的过度外推。现有研究很少系统性评估不同模型在转折点响应、预测时域和训练数据量这三个操作维度上的表现差异，需要一种能根据实际运行条件动态组合统计与机器学习模型的方法。

Method: 使用2020年1月至2023年10月安大略省190周公开COVID-19病例数据，采用滚动起点时间序列交叉验证保持时间顺序。评估ARIMA、随机森林和XGBoost模型在转折点响应、1-6周预测时域和训练数据量三个维度上的表现。提出MLAMA（Machine Learning and ARIMA Model Averaging）非负性能加权集成方法，权重随预测时域和响应设置动态变化。

Result: ARIMA在转折点后适应迅速，但归一化误差在长预测时域下增大；随机森林和XGBoost初始响应较慢但在长时域下误差更稳定。研究期末的两周预测中，使用最近数据训练优于长历史数据，尤其对XGBoost显著。MLAMA在大多数预测时域上获得最低归一化平均绝对百分比误差，且在响应设置中排名前列。

Conclusion: 不存在单一普适最优的预测模型，应根据运行条件选择模型。MLAMA提供了一个实用框架，可有效结合互补的统计与机器学习预测。

Abstract: Public health forecasts must respond to abrupt changes in surveillance data without over-extrapolating noise, reporting artifacts, or temporary trends. We evaluated autoregressive integrated moving average (ARIMA), random forest, and extreme gradient boosting (XGBoost) models using 190 weekly observations of publicly available Ontario COVID-19 case counts from January 2020 to October 2023. Rolling-origin time-series cross-validation preserved temporal order during model tuning and evaluation. Performance was assessed across three operating dimensions: responsiveness following selected turning points, forecast horizons of one to six weeks, and the amount of historical training data. We also developed Machine Learning and ARIMA Model Averaging (MLAMA), a non-negative performance-weighted ensemble with weights that vary by forecast horizon and responsiveness setting. Retrospective comparisons showed that ARIMA adapted rapidly after turning points but its normalized error increased at longer horizons. Random forest and XGBoost were less responsive initially but maintained more stable normalized error over longer horizons. For two-week forecasts at the end of the study period, training on the most recent data outperformed using longer historical periods, particularly for XGBoost. MLAMA achieved the lowest normalized mean absolute percentage error across most forecast horizons and ranked among the best-performing methods across responsiveness settings. These findings support selecting forecasting models according to operating conditions rather than relying on a single universally preferred approach. MLAMA provides a practical framework for combining complementary statistical and machine-learning forecasts. The accompanying Python package is currently maintained in a private repository while software validation and reproducibility testing are completed.

</details>


### [301] [From Thermal Preference Prediction to Adaptive Thermal Intervention: A Reinforcement Learning Approach Using Physiological and Environmental Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20423)
*Isibor Kennedy Ihianle,Emmanuel Manu,Ehsan Asnaashari,Mojgan Jadidi,Pedro Machado,Amrit Sagoo,Ahmad Lotfi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段个性化热舒适方法，将多模态生理与环境感知与基于强化学习的决策相结合，以克服传统HVAC系统依赖静态设定点和群体级舒适模型的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统暖通空调系统依赖静态设定点和群体级舒适模型，无法捕捉个体生理差异，难以满足个性化的热舒适需求。

Method: 采用两阶段方法：第一阶段整合多模态生理与环境感知数据进行个性化舒适建模；第二阶段基于强化学习进行决策以优化控制策略。

Result: 未在摘要中提供具体实验结果或量化指标。

Conclusion: 该方法有望为响应式建筑控制策略提供支持，提升居住者的热舒适体验。

Abstract: Personalised thermal comfort is essential for occupant wellbeing and for the development of more responsive building-control strategies, yet conventional Heating, Ventilation, and Air Conditioning (HVAC) systems rely on static setpoints and population-level comfort models that fail to capture individual physiological variability. This paper presents a two-stage personalised thermal comfort approach integrating multimodal physiological and environmental sensing with reinforcement learning-based decision-making.

</details>


### [302] [Bern2Edge: A Neurosymbolic Compiler for Edge Deployment via Bernstein Polynomial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20497)
*Malak Gamal El-Din,Yifan Zhang,Yasser Shoukry,Sitao Huang,Salma Elmalaki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Bern2Edge是一个端到端框架，通过知识蒸馏将预训练教师网络转换为基于Bernstein多项式激活的硬件高效表示，支持LUT高保真部署和符号规则可解释部署两条路径，在FPGA上实现最高99.8%的延迟降低和95.2%的BRAM减少，同时保持精度损失在0.5pp以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将训练、压缩和硬件综合视为独立阶段，导致软件训练模型与高效端到端部署之间存在鸿沟，且缺乏可解释性支持。

Method: 提出Bern2Edge框架，通过知识蒸馏将预训练教师前馈网络转换为Bernstein多项式激活的硬件高效表示。该表示支持两条部署路径：(i)基于查找表(LUT)的高保真实现，在压缩下保持模型保真度；(ii)基于Bernstein激活几何的符号规则表示，提供显式输入空间约束的可解释推理。

Result: 生成的BNN在相同压缩约束下相比ReLU实现了最高2.12个百分点的精度提升。系统层面，在AMD Xilinx KV260 FPGA上相比W8A8量化教师网络实现最高99.8%延迟降低和95.2% BRAM减少，精度保持0.5pp以内；进一步部署到低功耗Spartan-7 XC7S15 FPGA。规则路径以1.5pp总精度为代价减少最高89.0%的DSP使用。

Conclusion: Bern2Edge通过Bernstein多项式激活的统一表示弥合了训练、压缩与硬件综合之间的鸿沟，同时在高精度LUT部署和可解释规则部署两条路径之间提供灵活权衡，证明了性能、资源和可解释性的协同可行性。

Abstract: Deploying high-accuracy neural networks on resource-constrained edge devices remains challenging, as existing approaches treat training, compression, and hardware synthesis as separate stages, leaving a gap between software-trained models and efficient end-to-end deployment with limited support for interpretability. We propose Bern2Edge, an end-to-end framework that uses knowledge distillation to convert a pretrained teacher feed-forward network into hardware-efficient representations via Bernstein polynomial activations. This representation enables two deployment paths: (i) a high-fidelity LUT-based realization that preserves model fidelity under compression, and (ii) a symbolic rule-based representation derived from Bernstein activation geometry, enabling interpretable inference with explicit input-space constraints. The resulting BNNs achieve up to 2.12 percentage-point (pp) accuracy improvement over ReLU under identical compression constraints.
